The Value Axis: Language Models Encode Whether They're on the Right Track
Authors: Nick Jiang, Isaac Kauvar, Jack Lindsey
First: 2026-06-15T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Code repository: https://github.com/nickjiang2378/value-axis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We investigate whether language models internally track the value of their current trajectory, defined as the likelihood that their ongoing strategy will achieve their goals. Using synthetic, in-context reinforcement learning data, we construct a "value" axis for Qwen3-8B. We find that activations along this axis distinguish between high vs. low verbalized confidence, rollouts without and with backtracking, and correct vs. corrupted code. Steering towards high value causally suppresses self-correction and reduces explanatory verbosity, while steering towards low value induces backtracking and exploration. We demonstrate that direct preference optimization (DPO) can increase the internal value of rewarded behaviors (e.g. use a certain word), causing the model to act more confidently after exhibiting them. Finally, we apply the value axis to study in-the-wild settings. For example, we find that Qwen assigns low value to politically sensitive chat queries after post-training and that supervised fine-tuning increases internal confidence within the training domain. Our results suggest that language models linearly encode an estimate of expected goal success that modulates their confidence in pursuing a direction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：价值轴：语言模型编码其当前轨迹是否在正轨上
我们研究语言模型是否在其内部追踪当前轨迹的价值，即其持续策略实现目标的可能性。通过合成的上下文强化学习数据，我们为Qwen3-8B构建了一个“价值”轴。我们发现，该轴上的激活值可区分高与低的显式置信度、有无回溯的模拟轨迹，以及正确与损坏的代码。向高价值方向引导会因果性地抑制自我修正并减少解释冗余，而向低价值方向引导则会引发回溯与探索。我们证明，直接偏好优化（DPO）可提高被奖励行为（如使用特定词汇）的内部价值，使模型在展示这些行为后表现得更自信。最后，我们将价值轴应用于真实场景研究。例如，我们发现Qwen在微调后对政治敏感的聊天查询赋予较低价值，而监督微调可提升其在训练域内的内部置信度。我们的结果表明，语言模型线性编码了对预期目标成功概率的估计，该估计调节其对特定方向的自信程度。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether language models internally track the value of their current trajectory—defined as the likelihood of achieving goals—and finds that Qwen3-8B encodes this value along a linear axis. Using synthetic reinforcement learning data, researchers construct this 'value' axis and show that activations along it correlate with verbalized confidence, backtracking behavior, and correctness of code outputs. Steering the model toward high value reduces self-correction and verbosity, while low value induces backtracking and exploration. Direct preference optimization (DPO) increases internal value for rewarded behaviors, boosting confidence after exhibiting them. In real-world settings, the model assigns low value to politically sensitive queries post-training, and supervised fine-tuning boosts confidence within training domains, suggesting language models linearly encode expected goal success to modulate confidence.
该研究探讨语言模型是否内部追踪当前策略的价值（即达成目标的可能性），发现Qwen3-8B沿一条线性轴编码该价值。通过合成强化学习数据构建并验证该价值轴，激活值可区分自信程度、是否回溯及代码正确性。向高价值方向引导会抑制自我修正并减少冗余解释，向低价值引导则促进回溯与探索。直接偏好优化可提升被奖励行为的内部价值，使模型在表现后更自信。在真实场景中，模型对政治敏感查询赋予低价值，监督微调则提升训练域内的内部信心，表明语言模型线性编码预期目标达成度以调节自信程度。
T-Rex: Tactile-Reactive Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Dantong Niu, Zhuoyang Liu, Zekai Wang, Boning Shao, Zhao-Heng Yin, Anirudh Pai, Yuvan Sharma, Stefano Saravalle, Ruijie Zheng, Jing Wang, Ryan Punamiya, Mengda Xu, Yuqi Xie, Yunfan Jiang, Letian Fu, Konstantinos Kallidromitis, Matteo Gioia, Junyi Zhang, Jiaxin Ge, Haiwen Feng, Fabio Galasso, Wei Zhan, David M. Chan, Yutong Bai, Roei Herzig, Jiahui Lei, Fei-Fei Li, Ken Goldberg, Jitendra Malik, Pieter Abbeel, Yuke Zhu, Danfei Xu, Jim, Fan, Trevor Darrell
First: 2026-06-15T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://tactile-rex.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The ability to react dynamically to tactile signals has long been considered crucial to agile human-level dexterity. Yet contemporary learning-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation generally either overlook the tactile modality or are limited to encoders with static cues, due in part to the scarcity of diverse training data and standardized evaluation, architectural constraints in current VLA models, and limitations of static tactile encoders. In this paper, we push the frontier of tactile-reactive manipulation by addressing all of these limitations. We propose a large-scale, 100-hour tactile-rich dataset collected via a novel, data-efficient recipe that prioritizes elementary motor primitives. To effectively exploit naturally high-frequency touch signals without sacrificing the existing capabilities of existing VLAs, we introduce a variable-rate Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture equipped with a novel temporal tactile VQ-VAE encoder. We demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-reactive policies on 12 manipulation tasks requiring delicate force control and deformable object manipulation, achieving over 30% higher average success rate than the strongest baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：T-Rex：触觉反应型灵巧操作
动态响应触觉信号的能力长期以来被认为是实现敏捷的人类级灵巧操作的关键。然而，当前基于学习的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人操作模型通常忽略触觉模态，或受限于静态提示编码器，这在一定程度上源于多样训练数据稀缺、标准化评估缺失、现有VLA模型架构限制以及静态触觉编码器的局限性。本文通过解决上述所有限制，推动触觉反应型操作的前沿。我们提出一个大规模、100小时触觉丰富的数据集，通过一种新颖且数据高效的采集方案，优先采集基础运动原语。为有效利用天然高频触觉信号，同时不牺牲现有VLA模型的能力，我们引入一种可变速率混合变换器（MoT）架构，配备新型时序触觉VQ-VAE编码器。我们在12项需要精细力控和可变形物体操作的任务上验证了触觉反应策略的有效性，平均成功率比最强基线高出30%以上。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to dynamically respond to tactile feedback for human-level dexterity, this work addresses limitations in current Vision-Language-Action models that either ignore or poorly handle tactile data. The authors introduce a large-scale, 100-hour tactile-rich dataset collected using efficient motor primitive sampling, and propose a variable-rate Mixture-of-Transformers architecture with a novel temporal tactile VQ-VAE encoder to process high-frequency touch signals without compromising existing VLA capabilities. Experimental results across 12 manipulation tasks show tactile-reactive policies achieve over 30% higher average success rate than the strongest baseline, demonstrating significant gains in force control and deformable object manipulation.
为实现机器人对触觉反馈的动态响应以达到人类级别的灵巧性，本文针对现有视觉-语言-动作模型忽略触觉或依赖静态编码器的局限性，提出了一种通过高效运动原语采样收集的100小时触觉丰富数据集，并设计了一种可变速率混合变换器架构，配备新型时序触觉VQ-VAE编码器，以处理高频触觉信号而不影响现有VLA能力。在12项需要精细力控和可变形物体操作的任务中，触觉反应策略平均成功率比最强基线高出30%以上，验证了动态触觉反馈在机器人操作中的有效性。
Scale-Invariant Neural Network Optimization: Norm Geometry and Heavy-Tailed Noise
Authors: Jiayu Zhang, Tianyi Lin
First: 2026-05-18T15:13:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:59:48+00:00
Comments: Polished writing; Fixed typos and references; 45 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A growing lesson from neural network optimization is that optimizer design should respect how the model is parametrized. The layerwise input-output structure of neural networks motivates scale-invariant optimizers, such as Muon and Scion, whose updates also support hyperparameter transfer. At the same time, stochastic gradient noise in deep learning is often far from sub-Gaussian and may exhibit heavy tails. These observations have shaped recent algorithmic principles for training neural networks, yet their joint theoretical consequences are underexplored. In particular, it remains unclear what dimension dependence is unavoidable for gradient-based methods given the problem class is defined by input-output norm and under heavy-tailed noise, and whether higher-order smoothness can accelerate training. We study these questions through nonconvex smooth stochastic optimization over $\mathbb R^{m\times n}$ equipped with general norms and under $p^\mathrm{th}$-moment heavy-tailed noise, where the goal is to achieve an $ε$-stationary point in the dual norm. Our first contribution is a dimension-dependent lower bound: when $\frac{\max\{m,n\}}{(\min\{m,n\})^2}$ is large enough, any gradient-based method requires $Ω(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}})$ oracles for the problem class defined by the spectral norm, which is a common input-output norm. We prove that a scale-invariant Scion method with the spectral norm can achieve the matching upper bound of $O(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}})$. To exploit higher-order smoothness, we propose a transported Scion method and improve the bound to $O(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{5p-3}{2p-2}})$ when the Hessian is Lipschitz. Finally, we incorporate heuristics into our transported method and evaluate it across multiple architectures and model sizes, demonstrating its flexibility and compatibility with neural network training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：尺度不变神经网络优化：范数几何与重尾噪声
神经网络优化的一个重要启示是，优化器设计应尊重模型的参数化方式。神经网络的层间输入输出结构促使了尺度不变优化器（如Muon和Scion）的发展，其更新机制也支持超参数迁移。同时，深度学习中的随机梯度噪声往往远非次高斯分布，可能表现出重尾特性。这些观察塑造了近期神经网络训练的算法原则，但它们的联合理论后果尚未被充分探索。特别是，在输入输出范数定义的问题类和重尾噪声条件下，梯度方法不可避免的维度依赖性尚不明确，且高阶光滑性是否能加速训练也存疑。我们通过在$\mathbb R^{m\times n}$上配备一般范数并在$p^\mathrm{th}$矩重尾噪声下研究非凸光滑随机优化问题，目标是达到对偶范数下的$ε$-平稳点。我们的第一项贡献是维度依赖的下界：当$\frac{\max\{m,n\}}{(\min\{m,n\})^2}$足够大时，任何梯度方法都需要$Ω(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}})$次调用，该问题类由谱范数定义（一种常见输入输出范数）。我们证明，使用谱范数的尺度不变Scion方法可达到匹配的上界$O(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}})$。为利用高阶光滑性，我们提出一种传输Scion方法，并在Hessian为Lipschitz时将界改进为$O(\min\{m, n\}ε^{-\frac{5p-3}{2p-2}})$。最后，我们将启发式方法融入传输方法，并在多种架构和模型规模上评估其性能，证明其灵活性和与神经网络训练的兼容性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that neural network optimization benefits from scale-invariant methods and that stochastic gradients often exhibit heavy-tailed noise, this paper investigates the theoretical limits and practical performance of gradient-based methods under general norms and heavy-tailed noise. It derives a dimension-dependent lower bound showing that achieving an ε-stationary point requires Ω(min{m,n}ε^(-(3p-2)/(p-1))) oracles when using spectral norm, and proves that the scale-invariant Scion method matches this bound. Further, by leveraging higher-order smoothness via a transported Scion variant, the upper bound improves to O(min{m,n}ε^(-(5p-3)/(2p-2))) under Lipschitz Hessian. Empirical evaluations across architectures confirm the method’s effectiveness and compatibility with real neural network training.
为设计尊重神经网络参数化结构并适应重尾噪声的优化器，本文研究了在一般范数和p阶矩重尾噪声下尺度不变的非凸光滑随机优化。研究证明，对于谱范数定义的问题类，任何梯度方法需Ω(min{m,n}ε^(-(3p-2)/(p-1)))次计算，而尺度不变的Scion方法可达到此下界。进一步，通过引入传输Scion方法并利用Hessian利普希茨光滑性，上界提升至O(min{m,n}ε^(-(5p-3)/(2p-2)))。实验在多种架构上验证了该方法的实用性和与神经网络训练的兼容性。
Human Universal Grasping
Authors: Kevin Yuanbo Wu, Tianxing Zhou, Isaac Tu, Billy Yan, Irmak Guzey, David Fouhey, Dandan Shan, Lerrel Pinto
First: 2026-06-15T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类通用抓取
人类可以轻松抓取物体，而多指机器人尚远未达到这种通用性。我们认为，机器人抓取数据最自然的来源是人类，他们每天会抓取成千上万的物体。我们提出了HUG，一种流匹配模型，可基于立体相机拍摄的单张RGB-D图像，为任意用户指定的物体生成多样化的抓取方式。我们使用智能眼镜收集了1M-HUGs数据集，该数据集包含100万帧（27.8小时）、6707个物体实例，覆盖41栋建筑的自视角人类抓取数据。为建模自然人类抓取的分布，我们的新型流匹配模型融合RGB与深度观测，输出由手腕平移、手腕旋转和MANO手部姿态参数化的抓取方案。预测的抓取方案可重定向至多种机器人手部，实现日常场景中的零样本抓取。为标准化评估，我们构建了新的模拟基准HUG-Bench，包含5类几何形状、多种尺寸的90个未见过的物体，配有度量级3D网格。我们在HUG-Bench的30个物体测试集上，于多种立体相机、机器人形态和家庭环境中对HUG进行真实世界评估。HUG在我们的挑战性物体集上，相较现有最先进抓取基线分别提升23%和34%。代码、数据、基准、检查点及交互式演示已发布于我们的网站：https://grasping.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the natural dexterity of human grasping, this work proposes HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse, human-like grasps for any object from a single RGB-D image. The method fuses visual and depth inputs to predict grasp parameters including wrist position, orientation, and MANO hand pose, enabling zero-shot transfer to robotic hands. Evaluated on a new simulated benchmark (HUG-Bench) and real-world household scenes, HUG achieves +23% and +34% performance gains over existing baselines on challenging object sets across multiple robot platforms and camera setups.
为解决多指机器人抓取能力不足的问题，该研究提出HUG模型，通过融合RGB-D图像中的视觉与深度信息，从单帧图像生成多样化的自然人类抓取姿态。模型输出包括手腕位姿、旋转和MANO手部姿态参数，支持零样本迁移到不同机器人手部。在新构建的模拟基准HUG-Bench（含90个未见物体）上，HUG在30个测试物体上实测表现优于现有方法23%至34%。
Context-Aware RL for Agentic and Multimodal LLMs
Authors: Peiyang Xu, Bangzheng Li, Sijia Liu, Karthik R. Narasimhan, Pramod Viswanath, Prateek Mittal, Xingyu Fu
First: 2026-06-15T17:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:59:28+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often fail when answering requires identifying a small but decisive piece of evidence within a long or complex context, such as a single line in a tool trace or a subtle detail in an image. We propose ContextRL, a context-aware reinforcement learning (RL) method that improves long-horizon reasoning and multimodal performance through an \emph{indirect} auxiliary objective. Instead of supervising only the final answer, ContextRL presents the model with a query, an answer, and two highly similar contexts, and rewards it for selecting the context that supports the query--answer pair, thereby encouraging fine-grained grounding. We construct contrastive context data in two domains: for coding agents, trajectories serve as contexts, yielding 1k pairs built via condition filtering; for multimodal reasoning, images serve as contexts, yielding 7K pairs built via generative editing and similarity search. ContextRL achieves average gains of +2.2% over standard GRPO on 5 long-horizon benchmarks, and +1.8% across 12 diverse visual question answering benchmarks. To disentangle the effect of the proposed objective from that of additional data, we compare against data-augmentation baselines that repurpose the same contrastive contexts as standard query--context--answer examples. These baselines provide little to no improvement, showing that the gains arise from the proposed context-selection objective rather than from the contrastive data alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向代理与多模态大语言模型的上下文感知强化学习
大语言模型（LLMs）在回答需要从长或复杂上下文中识别出一小段关键证据的问题时往往表现不佳，例如工具调用轨迹中的一行或图像中的细微细节。我们提出ContextRL，一种上下文感知的强化学习方法，通过一个间接的辅助目标提升长程推理和多模态性能。与仅监督最终答案不同，ContextRL向模型呈现一个查询、一个答案和两个高度相似的上下文，并奖励其选择支持该查询-答案对的上下文，从而鼓励细粒度的定位。我们在两个领域构建对比上下文数据：对于代码代理，轨迹作为上下文，通过条件过滤构建1000对；对于多模态推理，图像作为上下文，通过生成式编辑和相似性搜索构建7000对。ContextRL在5个长程推理基准上相较标准GRPO平均提升2.2%，在12个多样化的视觉问答基准上平均提升1.8%。为分离所提目标与额外数据的影响，我们与将相同对比上下文重新用作标准查询-上下文-答案示例的数据增强基线进行比较。这些基线几乎无提升，表明收益主要来自所提上下文选择目标，而非对比数据本身。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in locating critical evidence within long or complex contexts, the authors propose ContextRL, a context-aware reinforcement learning method that uses an indirect auxiliary objective to improve grounding. Instead of only optimizing final answers, ContextRL trains models to select the context that best supports a given query-answer pair, using contrastive context data constructed via trajectory filtering for coding and generative editing for vision tasks. Experiments show ContextRL achieves +2.2% average gains over GRPO on five long-horizon benchmarks and +1.8% across twelve visual QA benchmarks, while baselines using the same contrastive data without the context-selection objective show negligible improvement, confirming the method’s effectiveness stems from the objective design, not the data.
为了解决大语言模型在长上下文或复杂信息中难以定位关键证据的问题，作者提出了ContextRL方法，通过间接辅助目标提升模型的细粒度定位能力。该方法训练模型在给定查询与答案时选择最匹配的上下文，使用通过轨迹过滤构建的代码上下文和通过生成编辑构建的视觉上下文数据。实验表明，ContextRL在五个长推理基准上平均提升2.2%，在十二个视觉问答任务上平均提升1.8%；对比使用相同对比上下文数据但无上下文选择目标的基线方法，后者几乎无提升，证明性能增益源于目标设计而非数据增强本身。
BRDFusion: Physics Meets Generation for Urban Scene Inverse Rendering
Authors: Yi-Ruei Liu, Jie-Ying Lee, Zheng-Hui Huang, Yu-Lun Liu, Chih-Hao Lin
First: 2026-06-15T17:58:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:58:31+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://shigon255.github.io/brdfusion-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Inverse rendering of urban scenes from captured videos enables numerous applications, including content creation and autonomous driving simulation. Physically-based rendering methods follow and control lighting physics, but suffer from reconstruction and rendering artifacts. While generative models produce realistic videos, they offer limited consistency and controllability. We present BRDFusion, a unified framework that combines two complementary models for inverse and forward rendering. Specifically, BRDFusion recovers explicit, consistent scene properties with physical modeling and alleviates optimization ambiguity with generative priors. During forward rendering, the physical model provides controllable rendering from the scene configuration, and the generative model denoises and fixes artifacts. Therefore, our method produces high-quality videos while allowing precise control, outperforming baselines in real and synthetic scenes. Moreover, BRDFusion supports novel-view relighting, night simulation, and dynamic object insertion/editing. Project page: https://shigon255.github.io/brdfusion-page/
中文标题/摘要
标题：BRDFusion：物理与生成结合的城市场景逆渲染
从捕获视频中逆渲染城市场景可支持多种应用，包括内容创作和自动驾驶仿真。基于物理的渲染方法遵循并控制光照物理，但存在重建与渲染伪影问题；而生成模型虽能生成逼真视频，却缺乏一致性和可控性。我们提出BRDFusion，一种统一框架，结合两种互补模型实现逆渲染与正向渲染。具体而言，BRDFusion通过物理建模恢复显式一致的场景属性，并利用生成先验缓解优化歧义。正向渲染时，物理模型提供可控渲染，生成模型则去噪并修复伪影。因此，我们的方法在真实与合成场景中均生成高质量视频并支持精确控制，优于基线方法。此外，BRDFusion支持新视角重光照、夜间仿真及动态物体插入/编辑。项目页面：https://shigon255.github.io/brdfusion-page/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-quality, controllable urban scene rendering for applications like autonomous driving and content creation, BRDFusion combines physical modeling with generative priors to address limitations of each approach individually. The method first recovers consistent scene properties using physics-based inverse rendering, then leverages a generative model to resolve optimization ambiguities and fix artifacts. Experimental results show BRDFusion produces superior video quality and enables precise control over lighting, materials, and geometry, outperforming existing methods in both real and synthetic scenes, while also supporting novel-view relighting, night simulation, and dynamic object editing.
为满足自动驾驶模拟和内容创作等领域对高质量可控城市场景渲染的需求，BRDFusion结合物理建模与生成先验，克服单一方法的局限。该方法首先通过物理逆渲染恢复一致的场景属性，再利用生成模型消除优化歧义并修复伪影。实验表明，BRDFusion在真实与合成场景中均优于现有方法，生成视频质量更高、控制更精确，同时支持新视角重光照、夜间模拟及动态物体插入与编辑。
Exact Posterior Score Estimation for Solving Linear Inverse Problems
Authors: Abbas Mammadov, Ozgur Kara, Kaan Oktay, Iskander Azangulov, Adil Kaan Akan, Hyungjin Chung, James Matthew Rehg, Yee Whye Teh
First: 2026-06-15T17:58:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:58:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion and flow-based models learn powerful data priors by training a denoiser to reverse Gaussian corruption. To use this prior to solve a linear inverse problem, one needs to sample from the posterior, but the score that the prior provides is the unconditional score, not the posterior score. Existing methods either steer a fixed pretrained denoiser with approximate measurement-matching corrections, or train a conditional restoration model that abandons the denoising structure of the prior. We derive the exact posterior score in closed form for linear Gaussian inverse problems under general Gaussian interpolants, and show that posterior sampling reduces to a denoising problem at an operator-dependent shifted pivot under an anisotropic noise covariance. We turn this identity into Exact Posterior Score (EPS), a denoising training objective that preserves the input/output structure of standard pretraining and can therefore be trained from scratch or fine-tuned from a pretrained denoiser. At inference, EPS uses the same sampler as the underlying backbone, with no likelihood gradients or projections. We evaluate EPS on five linear inverse problems across FFHQ and ImageNet, where it outperforms training-free and training-based baselines on fidelity, perceptual, and distributional metrics, while using roughly an order of magnitude fewer denoiser evaluations than gradient-based posterior samplers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于求解线性逆问题的精确后验得分估计
扩散和流式模型通过训练去噪器以逆转高斯噪声来学习强大的数据先验。为利用该先验求解线性逆问题，需从后验分布采样，但先验提供的得分是无条件得分，而非后验得分。现有方法要么通过近似测量匹配校正引导固定预训练去噪器，要么训练条件恢复模型而放弃先验的去噪结构。我们推导出在一般高斯插值下线性高斯逆问题的精确后验得分闭式解，并表明后验采样可转化为在各向异性噪声协方差下、在算子依赖的偏移点处的去噪问题。我们将此恒等式转化为精确后验得分（EPS），一种保留标准预训练输入/输出结构的去噪训练目标，因此可从头训练或从预训练去噪器微调。推理时，EPS使用底层骨干网络的相同采样器，无需似然梯度或投影。我们在FFHQ和ImageNet上的五个线性逆问题中评估EPS，其在保真度、感知度和分布度量上优于无训练和有训练基线，同时去噪器评估次数约为梯度后验采样器的十倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to solve linear inverse problems using diffusion-based priors, this work addresses the gap between unconditional denoiser scores and the required posterior scores. It derives an exact closed-form expression for the posterior score under linear Gaussian settings and reformulates posterior sampling as a denoising task at a shifted pivot under anisotropic noise. The Exact Posterior Score (EPS) method leverages this identity to train denoisers without altering their structure, enabling both from-scratch and fine-tuning. Experiments across five inverse problems on FFHQ and ImageNet show EPS achieves superior fidelity, perceptual, and distributional metrics compared to existing baselines, while requiring roughly one order of magnitude fewer denoiser evaluations than gradient-based samplers.
为解决线性逆问题，该研究旨在弥合扩散模型中无条件去噪器得分与所需后验得分之间的差距。在一般高斯插值器下，推导出后验得分的闭式表达，表明后验采样可转化为在各向异性噪声下特定偏移点的去噪问题。提出Exact Posterior Score（EPS）方法，利用该恒等式构建训练目标，保留标准预训练结构，支持从零训练或微调。在FFHQ和ImageNet上的五个逆问题实验中，EPS在保真度、感知与分布度量上优于现有训练免和训练基方法，同时仅需梯度采样方法约一个数量级的去噪器评估次数。
Geometric Action Model for Robot Policy Learning
Authors: Jisang Han, Seonghu Jeon, Jaewoo Jung, René Zurbrügg, Honggyu An, Tifanny Portela, Marco Hutter, Marc Pollefeys, Seungryong Kim, Sunghwan Hong
First: 2026-06-15T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/Geometric-Action-Model/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generalist robot policies must follow user instructions while reasoning about how objects, cameras, and robot actions interact in the 3D physical world. Recent vision-language-action models (VLAs) and video world-action models (WAMs) inherit strong semantic or temporal priors from large-scale foundation models, but they still operate primarily on 2D image frames or 2D-derived latent spaces, leaving implicit the 3D geometry required for contact-rich manipulation. We propose the Geometric Action Model (GAM), a language-conditioned manipulation policy that directly repurposes a pretrained geometric foundation model (GFM) as a shared substrate for perception, temporal prediction, and action decoding. GAM splits the GFM at an intermediate layer: the shallow layers serve as an observation encoder, and a causal future predictor inserted at the split layer forecasts future latent tokens conditioned on language, proprioception, and action history. The predicted future tokens are then routed through the remaining GFM blocks for feature propagation and decoding, allowing a single backbone to produce both future geometry and actions. This design equips the GFM with language-conditioned temporal world modeling through minimal architectural modification while preserving its rich geometric priors. Across a broad suite of simulation and real-robot manipulation benchmarks, GAM is more accurate, more robust, faster, and lighter than current foundation-model-scale baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人策略学习的几何动作模型
通用型机器人策略必须在理解物体、相机和机器人动作如何在三维物理世界中相互作用的同时遵循用户指令。近期的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）和视频世界-动作模型（WAMs）虽继承了大规模基础模型的强语义或时序先验，但仍主要在二维图像帧或二维衍生潜在空间上操作，隐含了执行接触密集型操作所需的三维几何信息。我们提出几何动作模型（GAM），一种语言引导的操控策略，直接复用预训练的几何基础模型（GFM）作为感知、时序预测和动作解码的共享基底。GAM在GFM中间层进行拆分：浅层作为观测编码器，插入于拆分层的因果未来预测器根据语言、本体感觉和动作历史预测未来潜在标记。预测的未来标记随后通过剩余GFM模块进行特征传播和解码，使单一骨干网络同时生成未来几何结构和动作。该设计通过最小架构修改赋予GFM语言引导的时序世界建模能力，同时保留其丰富的几何先验。在广泛的仿真与真实机器人操控基准测试中，GAM比当前基础模型规模基线更准确、更鲁棒、更快、更轻量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robot policies that understand 3D physical interactions while following natural language instructions, the authors propose the Geometric Action Model (GAM), which repurposes a pretrained geometric foundation model (GFM) as a shared backbone for perception, prediction, and action decoding. GAM splits the GFM at an intermediate layer, using shallow layers for observation encoding and inserting a causal future predictor that forecasts latent tokens based on language, proprioception, and action history. These predicted tokens are then propagated through the remaining GFM layers to generate both future geometry and actions. Experimental results across simulation and real-robot benchmarks show GAM outperforms current foundation-model-scale baselines in accuracy, robustness, speed, and computational efficiency.
为使机器人策略能在遵循语言指令的同时理解三维物理交互，作者提出几何动作模型（GAM），将预训练的几何基础模型（GFM）作为感知、预测与动作解码的共享骨干网络。GAM在GFM中间层进行拆分，浅层用于观测编码，中间插入因果未来预测器，根据语言、本体感觉和动作历史预测未来潜在标记，再通过剩余GFM模块传播并解码为未来几何与动作。实验表明，GAM在仿真与真实机器人操作基准测试中，相比当前基础模型规模基线，在精度、鲁棒性、速度和计算效率上均更优。
Canonical Variates in Wasserstein Metric Space
Authors: Jia Li, Lin Lin
First: 2024-05-24T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:57:43+00:00
Comments: single space 39 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we address the classification of instances represented by distributions on a vector space rather than single points. We consider classification algorithms based on pairwise distances, specifically, the Wasserstein metric between distributions. Central to our investigation is dimension reduction within the Wasserstein metric space to enhance classification accuracy. We introduce a novel approach grounded in the principle of maximizing Fisher's ratio, defined as the quotient of between-class variation to within-class variation. The directions in which this ratio is maximized are termed discriminant coordinates or canonical variates axes. In practice, both between-class and within-class variations are defined as the average squared Wasserstein distances between pairs of distributions, with the pairs either belonging to the same class or to different classes. This ratio optimization is achieved through an iterative algorithm, which alternates between optimal transport and maximization steps within the vector space. Empirical studies are conducted to assess the algorithm's convergence; and experimental results demonstrate that the dimension reduction technique substantially enhances classification performance. Moreover, the new method outperforms well-established algorithms that operate on vector representations derived from distributional data. It also exhibits robustness to variations in how instances are summarized by distributions, such as the number of components in a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) representation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wasserstein度量空间中的典型变量
本文研究了由向量空间上的分布而非单点表示的实例的分类问题。我们考虑基于成对距离的分类算法，特别是分布之间的Wasserstein度量。研究的核心是Wasserstein度量空间中的降维以提升分类精度。我们提出了一种基于最大化Fisher比率的新方法，该比率定义为类间变异与类内变异之比。最大化该比率的方向被称为判别坐标或典型变量轴。在实践中，类间和类内变异均定义为同类别或不同类别分布对之间的平均平方Wasserstein距离。该比率优化通过交替进行最优传输和向量空间内最大化步骤的迭代算法实现。通过实证研究评估算法收敛性，实验结果表明该降维技术显著提升分类性能。此外，新方法优于基于分布数据向量表示的传统算法，且对实例如何通过分布（如高斯混合模型GMM的分量数）进行总结的差异具有鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to classify data represented as probability distributions rather than single points, this paper proposes a dimension reduction method based on maximizing Fisher’s ratio within the Wasserstein metric space. The approach defines between-class and within-class variations as averages of squared Wasserstein distances between distribution pairs, and identifies discriminant directions—canonical variates—by iteratively optimizing transport and maximization steps. Experimental results show that this method significantly improves classification accuracy compared to existing techniques operating on vectorized distribution representations, and remains robust to variations in distributional summaries such as the number of components in Gaussian mixture models.
为解决以概率分布而非单点表示的数据分类问题，本文提出一种基于Wasserstein度量空间的降维方法，通过最大化Fisher比值（类间变异与类内变异之比）来寻找判别方向（即典型变量轴）。类间与类内变异分别定义为同类别或异类别分布对间Wasserstein距离的平均平方值，通过交替进行最优传输计算与优化步骤实现比值最大化。实验表明该方法收敛稳定，显著提升分类性能，优于现有基于分布数据向量化表示的算法，且对分布表示方式（如高斯混合模型的分量数量）变化具有鲁棒性。
Hierarchical Advantage Weighting for Online RL Fine-Tuning of VLAs from Sparse Episode Outcomes
Authors: Tongyan Fang, Siyuan Huang, Naiyu Fang, Ganlong Zhao, Zhongjin Luo, Jianbo Liu, Xiaogang Wang, Ying Dong, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-06-15T17:57:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:57:14+00:00
Comments: Website: https://acerobotics-vla.github.io/HABC-Website
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
When pretrained VLA policies are fine-tuned through online RL, each rollout episode produces only a single binary outcome (success or failure), yet the actor update requires per-transition supervision. Existing approaches commonly reduce this sparse outcome to a single scalar reward or advantage signal, which conflates distinct forms of transition-level feedback and provides limited guidance once basic task success becomes achievable. First, a single scalar signal conflates the two objectives of viability and efficiency; once basic success is achieved, the binary label provides no gradient to distinguish efficient completions from slow ones. Second, real-world rollouts mix autonomous and intervention segments; naively assigning episode outcomes across these boundaries introduces incorrect credit assignment. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC), which trains separate critic heads for these two objectives on different data subsets and combines their outputs with a state-adaptive balance. A state-adaptive gate $g_t$ merges their one-step advantages, prioritizing viability when success is uncertain and shifting to efficiency only when viability is high, and converts the result into per-transition weights on the actor loss. Intervention-aware credit assignment further restricts outcome labels to segments executed by the current policy, preventing supervision from leaking across intervention boundaries. In real-robot experiments on three contact-rich bimanual tasks, HABC raises success from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines of 36%, 44%, and 12% to 92%, 88%, and 38%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分层优势加权的在线强化学习微调视觉语言代理方法
当预训练的视觉语言代理（VLA）策略通过在线强化学习微调时，每个滚动回合仅产生一个二元结果（成功或失败），但演员更新需要每一步的监督。现有方法通常将这种稀疏结果简化为单一标量奖励或优势信号，这混淆了不同形式的过渡级反馈，并在基本任务成功后提供有限的指导。首先，单一标量信号混淆了可行性和效率两个目标；一旦基本成功达成，二元标签无法提供梯度以区分高效完成与缓慢完成。其次，现实世界中的滚动过程混合了自主与干预段；若简单地在这些边界上分配回合结果，会导致错误的信用分配。为解决这些问题，我们提出分层优势加权行为克隆（HABC），在不同数据子集上训练针对这两个目标的独立批评头，并通过状态自适应平衡合并其输出。状态自适应门控 $g_t$ 合并一步优势，当成功不确定时优先考虑可行性，仅在可行性高时转向效率，并将结果转换为演员损失的每步权重。干预感知的信用分配进一步限制结果标签仅适用于当前策略执行的段落，防止监督信息跨干预边界泄漏。在三个接触密集型双臂任务的真实机器人实验中，HABC 将监督微调（SFT）基线的成功率从 36%、44% 和 12% 提升至 92%、88% 和 38%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of fine-tuning vision-language agents (VLAs) using sparse binary episode outcomes in online reinforcement learning, this work introduces Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC) to address two key issues: conflated viability and efficiency signals in scalar reward methods, and incorrect credit assignment across autonomous and intervention segments. HABC employs separate critic heads trained on distinct data subsets to model these objectives independently, then combines their one-step advantages via a state-adaptive gate that prioritizes viability during uncertain success and shifts to efficiency when viability is assured. This merged signal is converted into per-transition weights for actor updates, while intervention-aware labeling ensures supervision is confined to policy-executed segments. Experiments on three real-robot bimanual tasks show HABC significantly outperforms supervised fine-tuning baselines, boosting success rates from 36% to 92%, 44% to 88%, and 12% to 38%.
为了解决使用稀疏的二元任务成功/失败结果进行视觉语言代理（VLA）在线强化学习微调时反馈混淆与信用分配错误的问题，本文提出分层优势加权行为克隆（HABC）方法。该方法通过两个独立的批评头分别建模“可行性”与“效率”目标，并在不同数据子集上训练，再通过一个状态自适应门控机制融合二者的一步优势值——在任务结果不确定时优先关注可行性，当可行性高时转向效率优化，最终将融合结果转化为动作更新的每步权重；同时，通过干预感知的信用分配机制，确保监督信号仅作用于当前策略执行的片段，避免跨干预边界的信息泄露。在三个真实机器人双臂接触密集任务中，HABC将监督微调基线的成功率分别从36%提升至92%，44%提升至88%，12%提升至38%。
Benchmarking LLM Agents on Meta-Analysis Articles from Nature Portfolio
Authors: Anzhe Xie, Weihang Su, Yujia Zhou, Yiqun Liu, Qingyao Ai
First: 2026-06-15T17:56:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:56:41+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 7 figures, preprint for arXiv, dataset and code available at https://github.com/BFTree/MetaSyn
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Meta-analysis is a demanding form of evidence synthesis that combines literature retrieval, PI/ECO-guided study selection, and statistical aggregation. Its structured, verifiable workflow makes it an ideal substrate for evaluating systematic scientific reasoning, yet existing benchmarks lack ground truth across the full retrieval-screening-synthesis pipeline. We introduce MetaSyn, a dataset of 442 expert-curated meta-analyses from Nature Portfolio journals. Each entry pairs a research question with PI/ECO criteria, a retrieval corpus of 140k PubMed articles, verified positive studies, hard negatives that are topically similar but PI/ECO-ineligible, and complete search strategies and date bounds. Benchmarking twelve pipeline configurations (nine RAG variants and a protocol-driven agent) reveals a critical screening bottleneck: despite a retrieval ceiling of 90.9% recall at K=200, no system recovers more than 52.7% of ground-truth included literature. Current LLMs fail to reliably separate eligible studies from PI/ECO-failing distractors in pools of comparable topical relevance. Stage-attributed metrics capture where systems succeed and fail; a single end-to-end score does not.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在《自然》出版集团旗下期刊的元分析文章上对大语言模型代理进行基准测试
元分析是一种要求较高的证据综合形式，结合了文献检索、PI/ECO引导的研究筛选和统计聚合。其结构化、可验证的工作流程使其成为评估系统性科学推理的理想载体，但现有基准缺乏从检索-筛选-综合全流程的地面真实数据。我们引入MetaSyn，一个包含《自然》出版集团旗下期刊442篇专家整理的元分析文章的数据集。每个条目配有一个研究问题、PI/ECO标准、14万篇PubMed文献检索语料库、经验证的阳性研究、主题相似但不符合PI/ECO标准的硬负样本，以及完整的检索策略和日期范围。 对十二种流程配置（九种RAG变体和一种协议驱动代理）的基准测试揭示了一个关键的筛选瓶颈：尽管在K=200时检索召回率上限为90.9%，但没有任何系统能恢复超过52.7%的地面真实包含文献。当前大语言模型无法在主题相关性相近的文献池中可靠地区分符合条件的研究与PI/ECO失败的干扰项。按阶段划分的指标能准确捕捉系统在哪些环节成功或失败；单一端到端分数无法体现这一点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM agents on structured, verifiable scientific reasoning tasks, this work introduces MetaSyn, a curated dataset of 442 meta-analyses from Nature Portfolio journals, complete with retrieval corpora, ground-truth included studies, hard negatives, and search protocols. The study benchmarks twelve agent configurations—including nine RAG variants and a protocol-driven agent—across the full meta-analysis pipeline. Results reveal a critical screening bottleneck: despite high retrieval recall (90.9% at K=200), no system recovered more than 52.7% of ground-truth included studies, indicating LLMs struggle to reliably distinguish eligible studies from topically similar but PI/ECO-failing distractors. Stage-attributed metrics show where systems succeed or fail, emphasizing that end-to-end performance metrics are insufficient.
为评估大语言模型在结构化、可验证的科学推理任务中的表现，本研究构建了MetaSyn数据集，包含442篇Nature Portfolio期刊专家整理的元分析文章，每篇均配有PI/ECO筛选标准、检索语料库、已验证正样本、硬负样本及完整检索策略。研究人员对十二种系统配置（含九种RAG变体及一种协议驱动型代理）进行评测，发现尽管在K=200时检索召回率达90.9%，但无系统能召回超过52.7%的真实纳入文献，揭示了筛选环节的关键瓶颈——大模型难以在语义相近的干扰项中准确区分合格与不合格研究。阶段化评估指标揭示了系统失败的具体环节，表明单一端到端指标无法反映真实性能差距。
R2RDreamer: 3D-aware Data Augmentation for Spatially-generalized 2D Manipulation Policies
Authors: Xiuwei Xu, Haowen Sun, Angyuan Ma, Yiwei Zhang, Zhenyu Wu, Xiaofeng Wang, Bingyao Yu, Zheng Zhu, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
First: 2026-06-15T17:56:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:56:19+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://r2rdreamer.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Spatial generalization is critical for imitation-learned manipulation policies, but achieving it typically requires scaling demonstrations across diverse object poses, robot configurations, and camera viewpoints. Data augmentation from a few source demonstrations offers a practical alternative to costly real-world collection. Simulation-based augmentation can create controllable variation, but requires complex environment and object setup and may introduce a sim-to-real gap. Recent real-to-real methods avoid these issues by jointly editing 3D observations and action trajectories from real demonstrations, yet they still rely on strong 3D scene parsing and geometry completion, and often produce observations tailored to 3D pointcloud policies rather than RGB-based 2D policies. We propose R2RDreamer, a real-to-real demonstration augmentation framework that preserves the geometric consistency of 3D action-observation editing while moving visual completion to 2D video space. Specifically, R2RDreamer first performs lightweight 3D augmentation by editing incomplete object pointclouds and end-effector trajectories in a shared 3D frame; it then projects the edited scene into masked image-space control videos with occlusion-aware reasoning and uses a dense-control image-to-video model to complete temporally coherent RGB observations. Experiments on spatially shifted manipulation tasks with both 2D diffusion-style policies and vision-language-action policies show that R2RDreamer improves spatial generalization from limited source demonstrations, with analyses validating the contributions of 3D editing, occlusion-aware projection, and video completion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：R2RDreamer：面向空间泛化2D操作策略的3D感知数据增强
空间泛化对模仿学习的操作策略至关重要，但实现它通常需要在多样化的物体姿态、机器人配置和相机视角下扩展演示数据。从少量源演示数据进行数据增强是一种成本较低的替代方案。基于模拟的增强可生成可控变化，但需复杂环境与物体设置，可能引入仿真到现实的差距。近期的实现实现方法通过联合编辑真实演示中的3D观测与动作轨迹来避免这些问题，但仍依赖强大的3D场景解析与几何补全，且常生成针对3D点云策略而非RGB基2D策略的观测。我们提出R2RDreamer，一种实现实现演示增强框架，它在保持3D动作-观测编辑几何一致性的同时，将视觉补全移至2D视频空间。具体而言，R2RDreamer首先在共享3D坐标系中进行轻量级3D增强，编辑不完整物体点云和末端执行器轨迹；然后通过遮挡感知推理将编辑后的场景投影到带掩码的图像空间控制视频中，并使用密集控制图像到视频模型完成时序一致的RGB观测。在空间偏移操作任务上的实验表明，R2RDreamer能从有限源演示中提升空间泛化能力，分析验证了3D编辑、遮挡感知投影和视频补全的贡献。
Summary / 总结
To enhance spatial generalization in 2D manipulation policies without costly real-world data, R2RDreamer proposes a real-to-real data augmentation framework that edits 3D pointclouds and trajectories in a shared 3D frame, then projects them into masked video space using occlusion-aware reasoning and completes RGB observations via a dense-control video model. Experiments demonstrate improved performance on spatially shifted tasks using both 2D diffusion and vision-language-action policies, with ablation studies confirming the value of 3D editing, occlusion-aware projection, and video completion.
为提升2D操作策略的空间泛化能力，R2RDreamer提出一种真实到真实的数据增强方法：先在共享3D坐标系中轻量编辑物体点云与末端执行器轨迹，再通过遮挡感知投影生成掩码图像序列，并用密集控制视频模型完成时序一致的RGB观测。实验表明，该方法在有限源演示下显著提升对物体姿态和视角变化的泛化能力，消融实验验证了3D编辑、遮挡感知投影和视频补全三个模块的有效性。
deFOREST: Fusing Optical and Radar satellite data for Enhanced Sensing of Tree-loss
Authors: Julio Enrique Castrillon-Candas, Hanfeng Gu, Caleb Meredith, Yulin Li, Xiaojing Tang, Pontus Olofsson, Mark Kon
Venue: IEEE Transactions on Geoscience and Remote Sensing, vol. 64, 2026, Art no. 4409213
First: 2025-10-15T21:02:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:55:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we develop a deforestation detection pipeline that incorporates optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) data. A crucial component of the pipeline is the construction of anomaly maps of the optical data, which is done using the residual space of a discrete Karhunen-Loéve (KL) expansion. Anomalies are quantified using a concentration bound on the distribution of the residual components for the nominal state of the forest. This bound does not require prior knowledge on the distribution of the data. This is in contrast to statistical parametric methods that assume knowledge of the data distribution, an impractical assumption that is especially infeasible for high dimensional data such as ours. Once the optical anomaly maps are computed they are combined with SAR data, and the state of the forest is classified by using a Hidden Markov Model (HMM). We test our approach with Sentinel-1 (SAR) and Sentinel-2 (Optical) data on a $92\,km \times 92\,km$ region in the Amazon forest. The results show that both the hybrid optical-radar and optical only methods achieve high accuracy that is superior to the recent state-of-the-art hybrid method. Moreover, the hybrid method is significantly more robust in the case of sparse optical data that are common in highly cloudy regions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：deFOREST：融合光学与雷达卫星数据以增强树木损失感知
本文开发了一种结合光学与合成孔径雷达（SAR）数据的森林砍伐检测流程。该流程的关键组成部分是利用离散Karhunen-Loéve（KL）展开的残差空间构建光学数据的异常图。异常通过森林正常状态残差分量分布的集中界进行量化，该方法无需数据分布的先验知识，与假设已知数据分布的统计参数方法不同，后者在高维数据（如本研究数据）中尤其不切实际。计算出光学异常图后，将其与SAR数据结合，利用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）对森林状态进行分类。我们在亚马逊森林一个92公里×92公里区域，使用Sentinel-1（SAR）和Sentinel-2（光学）数据测试该方法。结果表明，混合光学-雷达方法与仅光学方法均达到高精度，优于近期最先进的混合方法。此外，混合方法在高云量地区常见稀疏光学数据情况下显著更具鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately detecting deforestation in cloudy regions where optical data is sparse, this study develops a hybrid sensing pipeline that fuses optical and SAR satellite data. The method constructs anomaly maps from optical data using the residual space of a discrete Karhunen-Loéve expansion, quantifying anomalies via a concentration bound that does not require prior knowledge of data distribution—unlike traditional parametric methods. These optical anomalies are then combined with SAR data and classified using a Hidden Markov Model. Tested on Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 data over a 92 km by 92 km Amazon region, the hybrid approach achieved higher accuracy than recent state-of-the-art methods and demonstrated superior robustness under sparse optical conditions typical of cloudy environments.
为应对云量大地区光学数据稀疏导致的森林损失检测难题，该研究构建了融合光学与SAR卫星数据的检测流程。通过离散Karhunen-Loéve展开的残差空间生成光学异常图，并利用分布浓度界量化异常，无需假设数据分布，避免了传统参数方法在高维数据中的局限。将光学异常图与SAR数据结合，采用隐马尔可夫模型进行森林状态分类。在亚马逊92公里×92公里区域的Sentinel-1与Sentinel-2数据上测试表明，该混合方法精度优于现有最先进方法，且在光学数据稀疏场景下显著更鲁棒。
The Importance of Phase in Neural Representations: An Internal Oppenheim-Lim Test of Image Classifiers
Authors: Alper Yıldırım
First: 2026-06-15T17:54:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:54:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Oppenheim and Lim (1981) showed that natural images stay recognizable when reconstructed from their Fourier phase alone, while the magnitude carries little of their identity. We ask whether trained image classifiers reproduce this asymmetry inside their hidden layers, and we test it causally: given two images, we transplant the phase of one onto the magnitude of the other at a chosen layer and record which image the prediction follows. In PRISM2D, GFNet, and ViT-B/16 the prediction follows the phase or sign donor, and deleting all image-specific magnitude barely moves accuracy, so identity rides on phase while image-specific magnitude is largely dispensable to the readout. ResNet-50 at first seems to break the pattern, because transplanting sign after its ReLUs does nothing; a fair intervention before the ReLU reveals a strong latent sign code in the late blocks, and a DC-only control shows the readout consumes a channel-wise spatial average. Controls rule out the trivial case in which magnitude simply stops depending on the image. The architectures therefore share a phase/sign identity code but expose it in different bases, set by rectification and readout geometry, which gives a mechanistic account of the texture--shape gap between CNNs and attention models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相位在神经表示中的重要性：图像分类器内部的奥本海姆-林测试
奥本海姆和林（1981）表明，自然图像仅用其傅里叶相位重建仍可保持可识别性，而幅度几乎不携带其身份信息。我们探究训练好的图像分类器是否在其隐藏层中再现这一不对称性，并通过因果测试验证：给定两张图像，我们在选定层将一张图像的相位移植到另一张图像的幅度上，并记录预测结果跟随哪张图像。在PRISM2D、GFNet和ViT-B/16中，预测结果跟随相位或符号供体，且删除所有图像特异性幅度几乎不影响准确率，因此身份信息依赖于相位，而图像特异性幅度对读出几乎可忽略。ResNet-50初看似乎打破此模式，因在其ReLU后移植符号无影响；但在ReLU前进行公平干预，揭示后期块中存在强隐含符号编码，且DC-only对照显示读出消耗通道间空间平均值。对照实验排除了幅度简单不再依赖图像的平凡情况。因此，这些架构共享相位/符号身份编码，但以不同基底暴露，由整流和读出几何决定，从而解释了CNN与注意力模型之间纹理-形状差距的机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Oppenheim and Lim's finding that image identity resides in Fourier phase rather than magnitude, this study tests whether neural classifiers encode this asymmetry internally. The authors causally transplant phase or sign from one image onto another at specific layers and measure classification outcomes. Across PRISM2D, GFNet, and ViT-B/16, predictions follow the phase or sign donor, and removing image-specific magnitude barely affects accuracy, indicating phase carries identity while magnitude is largely dispensable. ResNet-50 initially appears to violate this pattern, but fair interventions before ReLU reveal a latent sign code in late blocks, and DC-only controls show readout relies on spatial averaging. The architectures thus share a phase/sign identity code but express it differently based on rectification and readout structure, explaining the texture–shape gap between CNNs and attention models.
受Oppenheim和Lim发现图像身份主要存在于傅里叶相位而非幅度的启发，本研究检验深度图像分类器内部是否重现这一不对称性。作者通过在特定层交换两幅图像的相位与幅度，并观察分类器预测结果。在PRISM2D、GFNet和ViT-B/16中，预测结果跟随相位供体，移除图像特异性幅度对准确率影响极小，表明身份信息依赖相位而幅度基本可忽略。ResNet-50初看违背此规律，但公平干预在ReLU前揭示其后期块存在潜藏符号码，DC控制实验表明读出层使用通道间空间平均。各架构共享相位/符号身份码，但因整流与读出结构不同而表达方式各异，从而解释了CNN与注意力模型间纹理—形状差异的机制。
Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning
Authors: Xiaolin Li, Ning Wang, Ninghui Li, Wenhai Sun
First: 2026-06-15T17:53:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:53:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior research suggests that differential privacy (DP) inherently enhances the robustness of federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. Through an empirical analysis of two baseline attack strategies, we uncover a fundamental tension in DP-FL: while bypassing DP allows state-of-the-art defenses to detect and filter malicious updates, complying with DP inadvertently masks their distinguishing statistical characteristics. Consequently, existing defenses become ineffective as DP reduces the raw backdoor signal. Building on this masking effect, we propose RING, a novel attack that explicitly exploits DP to conceal malicious contributions while maximizing attack impact. By collaboratively crafting adversarial perturbations, compromised clients reconstruct a strong backdoor signal during aggregation without triggering anomaly detection. RING operates as a perturbation layer that is agnostic to the underlying backdoor technique, making it broadly applicable and composable with existing attacks -- a property that significantly amplifies the threat it poses to DP-FL. Extensive evaluations across four image and text datasets under non-iid distributions show that RING achieves an average attack success rate of 90.3% against six state-of-the-art defenses under a moderate privacy budget, an improvement of up to 26.08x over baseline strategies. Finally, we evaluate potential countermeasures and find that mitigating this threat incurs significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security gap in the deployment of differentially private FL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的隐私是我的斗篷：差分隐私联邦学习中的后门攻击
先前研究认为，差分隐私（DP）能天然增强联邦学习（FL）对后门攻击的鲁棒性。本文对此假设提出挑战。通过对两种基线攻击策略的实证分析，我们发现DP-FL存在根本性张力：绕过DP可使最先进防御机制检测并过滤恶意更新，而遵守DP则无意中掩盖了恶意更新的统计特征。因此，现有防御机制在DP削弱原始后门信号后失效。基于此掩盖效应，我们提出RING攻击，该攻击显式利用DP隐藏恶意贡献同时最大化攻击影响。通过协同构造对抗扰动，被攻陷客户端在聚合阶段重建强后门信号而不触发异常检测。RING作为与底层后门技术无关的扰动层，具有广泛适用性和与现有攻击方法组合的能力——这一特性显著放大了其对DP-FL的威胁。在四个图像和文本数据集（非独立同分布）上的广泛评估表明，RING在中等隐私预算下对六种最先进防御机制的平均攻击成功率高达90.3%，较基线策略提升高达26.08倍。最后，我们评估潜在缓解措施，发现缓解该威胁需付出显著的效用代价，揭示了差分隐私FL部署中的根本性安全缺陷。
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the prevailing belief that differential privacy (DP) inherently strengthens federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. Through empirical analysis, it reveals that DP masks the statistical signatures of malicious updates, rendering existing defenses ineffective. The authors propose RING, a novel backdoor attack that leverages DP to conceal adversarial perturbations while amplifying attack impact through collaborative client coordination. RING operates as a perturbation layer compatible with any backdoor method, and evaluations across four datasets under non-iid conditions show it achieves a 90.3% average attack success rate against six state-of-the-art defenses under moderate privacy budgets — up to 26.08x higher than baseline attacks. The study concludes that countering RING requires significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security vulnerability in DP-FL deployments.
该论文质疑了差分隐私（DP）能天然增强联邦学习（FL）对后门攻击鲁棒性的假设。通过实证分析发现，DP会掩盖恶意更新的统计特征，使现有防御失效。作者提出RING攻击方法，利用DP隐藏对抗扰动，同时通过协作重构聚合阶段的强后门信号以最大化攻击效果。在四个非独立同分布数据集上的评估显示，RING在中等隐私预算下对六种先进防御的平均攻击成功率高达90.3%，较基线策略提升高达26.08倍。研究指出，缓解此威胁需付出显著的性能代价，揭示了DP-FL部署中的根本性安全缺陷。
KVEraser: Learning to Steer KV Cache for Efficient Localized Context Erasing
Authors: Mufei Li, Shikun Liu, Dongqi Fu, Haoyu Wang, Yinglong Xia, Hong Li, Hong Yan, Pan Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-15T17:53:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:53:09+00:00
Comments: Oral at the ICML 2026 Workshop on the Impact of Memorization on Trustworthy Foundation Models
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-hoc context erasing over the KV cache is challenging because a local edit has a global consequence: once a span has been processed, its influence propagates into the cached states of all subsequent tokens. This issue arises naturally in long-context LLM applications, where stale retrieved facts, incorrect tool observations, retracted user preferences, or harmful prompt injections may be identified only after prefill. Exact erasing must then recompute all tokens after the deleted span, making its computational cost depend on suffix length rather than erased-span length. We introduce KVEraser, a learned KV-cache editing method for efficient localized context erasing. Given a processed context and a span to remove, KVEraser replaces only the KV states of the erased interval with learned steering states while reusing the remaining cache unchanged. To learn a transferable erasing mechanism, we build a two-stage training pipeline: generic span-neighbor pre-training teaches the eraser to suppress the influence of the erased span, while task-specific fine-tuning adapts this capability to downstream scenarios. Experiments show that KVEraser nearly matches full recomputation in post-erasure performance on in-domain tasks across 1K--32K context lengths, while its latency increases by only 24% compared with a 17.6x increase for full recomputation. KVEraser also generalizes to unseen long-document QA tasks with harmful factual distractors, achieving the best performance among approximate baselines with a 3--4x speedup over full recomputation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KVEraser：学习引导KV缓存以实现高效局部上下文擦除
在KV缓存上进行事后上下文擦除具有挑战性，因为局部编辑会产生全局影响：一旦某段上下文被处理，其影响会传播到后续所有令牌的缓存状态。这一问题在长上下文大语言模型应用中自然出现，例如在预填充后才识别出过时检索事实、错误工具观察、撤回的用户偏好或有害提示注入。精确擦除需重新计算删除段之后的所有令牌，其计算成本取决于后缀长度而非被擦除段长度。我们提出KVEraser，一种学习型KV缓存编辑方法，用于高效局部上下文擦除。给定已处理上下文和待删除段，KVEraser仅替换被擦除区间的KV状态为学习到的引导状态，其余缓存保持不变。为学习可迁移的擦除机制，我们构建了两阶段训练流程：通用段邻域预训练使擦除器抑制被擦除段的影响，任务特定微调则将其能力适配至下游场景。实验表明，KVEraser在1K至32K上下文长度的领域内任务中，擦除后性能几乎与完全重计算相当，而其延迟仅增加24%，相比完全重计算的17.6倍增长显著降低。KVEraser还能泛化至未见过的长文档问答任务（含有害事实干扰项），在近似基线中表现最佳，且比完全重计算快3至4倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of full recomputation when erasing localized context in long-context LLMs, KVEraser introduces a learned method to selectively overwrite only the KV cache states of the erased span while preserving the rest. It employs a two-stage training pipeline: first pre-trained on generic span-neighbor suppression, then fine-tuned for specific tasks. Experiments show that KVEraser achieves nearly full recomputation performance across 1K–32K context lengths with only a 24% latency increase, compared to a 17.6x increase for full recomputation, and outperforms other approximate methods on unseen long-document QA tasks with a 3–4x speedup.
KVEraser 解决了在长上下文大语言模型中，对 KV 缓存进行局部上下文擦除时因局部修改引发全局依赖而导致的高成本问题。它通过学习仅替换被删除片段对应的 KV 状态为引导状态，而保留其余缓存不变，实现局部擦除无需重算后续所有 token。采用两阶段训练流程——先在通用片段邻域上预训练以抑制被擦除片段的影响，再针对具体任务微调——KVEraser 在 1K–32K 上下文长度的领域内任务中，性能接近完整重算，延迟仅增加 24%，远低于完整重算的 17.6 倍增长。它还能泛化到含有害事实干扰的未见长文档问答任务，相较完整重算快 3–4 倍，且在近似方法中表现最优。
Qwen-RobotWorld Technical Report: Unifying Embodied World Modeling through Language-Conditioned Video Generation
Authors: Jie Zhang, Xiaoyue Chen, Anzhe Chen, Chenxu Lv, Deqing Li, Gengze Zhou, Hang Yin, Haoqi Yuan, Haoyang Li, Jiahao Li, Jiazhao Zhang, Jingren Zhou, Kaiyuan Gao, Kun Yan, Lihan Jiang, Ningyuan Tang, Pei Lin, Qihang Peng, Shengming Yin, Tianhe Wu, Tianyi Yan, Xiao Xu, Yan Shu, Yanran Zhang, Ye Wang, Yi Wang, Yilei Chen, Yixian Xu, Yiyang Huang, Yuxiang Chen, Zekai Zhang, Zhendong Wang, Zhixing Lei, Zhixuan Liang, Zihao Liu, Zikai Zhou, Xiong-Hui Chen, Chenfei Wu
First: 2026-06-15T17:52:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:52:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Qwen-RobotWorld, a language-conditioned video world model for embodied intelligence. With natural language as a unified action interface, it predicts physically grounded future visual trajectories from current observations across robotic manipulation, autonomous driving, indoor navigation, and human-to-robot transfer. This unified formulation provides three promising application directions: synthetic data generation for policy training augmentation, scalable virtual environments for policy evaluation, and language-guided planning signals for downstream robot control. This is achieved through a three-part design: a) Double-Stream MMDiT with MLLM Action Encoding, where a 60-layer double-stream diffusion transformer couples frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents through layer-wise joint attention; b) Embodied World Knowledge (EWK), an 8.6M video-text corpus (200M+ frames) with action-language mapping over 20+ embodiments and 500+ action categories; and c) General+Expert Progressive Curriculum, a two-stage training strategy that first learns general visual priors and then injects embodied specialization under a shared language interface. Extensive results show strong competitiveness: ranks 1st overall on EWMBench and DreamGen Bench, outperforms all open-source models on WorldModelBench and PBench. Additional zero-shot analyses on RoboTwin-IF benchmark further support robust generalization and multi-view consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-RobotWorld技术报告：通过语言引导的视频生成统一具身世界建模
我们引入了Qwen-RobotWorld，这是一种基于自然语言条件的视频世界模型，用于具身智能。以自然语言作为统一的动作接口，它能从当前观测中预测物理上合理的未来视觉轨迹，涵盖机器人操作、自动驾驶、室内导航及人机交互等场景。该统一框架提供了三个应用方向：用于策略训练增强的合成数据生成、可扩展的虚拟环境用于策略评估，以及为下游机器人控制提供语言引导的规划信号。该模型通过三部分设计实现：a) 双流MMDiT与MLLM动作编码，其中60层双流扩散Transformer通过逐层联合注意力将冻结的Qwen2.5-VL语义与视频VAE潜在表示耦合；b) 具身世界知识（EWK），一个包含860万视频-文本对（2亿多帧）的语料库，覆盖20多种具身形态和500多个动作类别，具备动作-语言映射；c) 通用+专家渐进式课程，一种两阶段训练策略，先学习通用视觉先验，再在共享语言接口下注入具身专业化能力。大量实验结果表明其具有强大竞争力：在EWMBench和DreamGen Bench上综合排名第一，在WorldModelBench和PBench上全面超越所有开源模型。在RoboTwin-IF基准上的零样本分析进一步验证了其泛化能力和多视角一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified, language-driven world modeling across diverse embodied tasks, Qwen-RobotWorld introduces a language-conditioned video generation model that predicts physically plausible future visual trajectories from natural language commands. It employs a double-stream MMDiT architecture with MLLM action encoding to fuse frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents via layer-wise attention, trained on a large Embodied World Knowledge corpus of 200M+ video frames across 20+ robotic embodiments and 500+ actions.
