为实现具身智能体的统一语言驱动框架，Qwen-RobotWorld提出一种基于自然语言指令生成物理可信未来视觉轨迹的视频世界模型，适用于机器人操作、自动驾驶、室内导航及人机交互等多场景。该模型采用双流MMDiT架构，结合冻结的Qwen2.5-VL语义与视频VAE潜在表示，通过层间联合注意力实现融合。训练数据为包含2000万+帧、500+动作类别的Embodied World Knowledge语料库，采用先学习通用视觉先验、再注入具身特化的渐进式课程策略。实验表明，该模型在EWMBench和DreamGen Bench上排名第一，在WorldModelBench和PBench上全面超越所有开源模型，且在RoboTwin-IF零样本测试中展现出强泛化能力与多视角一致性。
DEEPRUBRIC: Evidence-Tree Rubric Supervision for Efficient Reinforcement Learning of Deep Research Agents
Authors: Minghang Zhu, Chuyang Wei, Junhao Xu, Yilin Cheng, Zhumin Chen, Jiyan He
First: 2026-06-15T17:52:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:52:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep research agents synthesize long-form reports by searching and reasoning over retrieved evidence. Reinforcement learning with rubric-based rewards improves these agents by optimizing them against checkable criteria that translate report quality into reward signals, but its efficiency depends on whether those criteria reliably capture the task scope and evidence needs. Most existing studies ask an LLM to generate rubrics for a given query, but when the model fails to infer the underlying information needs, the generated rubrics may be incomplete and reduce RL efficiency. To obtain more reliable query--rubric supervision, we introduce DeepRubric, a data construction framework that reverses this process: instead of inferring evaluation criteria for a given query, it first determines what an evidence-backed report should be evaluated on and then synthesizes aligned query--rubric pairs from those evaluation targets. Starting from a sampled seed topic, DeepRubric builds an evidence tree by recursively expanding evidence-backed sub-questions, whose leaves serve as atomic and verifiable evaluation targets. It then uses the evidence tree to synthesize the training query and rubrics, ensuring that the reward evaluates exactly the information requested by the query. Using DeepRubric, we construct 9K query--rubric supervision examples and train DeepRubric-8B with rubric-based GRPO, achieving comparable performance to prior open state-of-the-art deep research models across three benchmarks with roughly 13x fewer RL GPU-hours.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DEEPRUBRIC：基于证据树的评分标准监督，用于高效强化学习深度研究代理
深度研究代理通过检索和推理证据来生成长篇报告。基于评分标准的强化学习通过优化可检查的标准，将报告质量转化为奖励信号，从而提升这些代理的表现，但其效率取决于这些标准是否可靠地捕捉任务范围和证据需求。大多数现有研究让大语言模型为给定查询生成评分标准，但当模型未能推断出潜在的信息需求时，生成的评分标准可能不完整，从而降低强化学习效率。为获得更可靠的查询-评分标准监督，我们引入DeepRubric，一个数据构建框架，逆转这一过程：它首先确定证据支持报告应被评估的内容，然后从这些评估目标中合成对齐的查询-评分标准对。从采样的种子主题开始，DeepRubric通过递归扩展证据支持的子问题构建证据树，其叶子节点作为原子且可验证的评估目标。随后，它利用证据树合成训练查询和评分标准，确保奖励精确评估查询所请求的信息。使用DeepRubric，我们构建了9000个查询-评分标准监督示例，并使用基于评分标准的GRPO训练DeepRubric-8B，在三个基准测试中实现了与先前最先进开源深度研究模型相当的性能，但RL GPU小时数减少了约13倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of LLM-generated rubrics that often fail to capture task-specific evidence needs, the authors propose DeepRubric, a framework that constructs reliable query-rubric pairs by first building an evidence tree from a seed topic. This tree recursively expands sub-questions whose leaves represent atomic, verifiable evaluation targets, enabling the synthesis of aligned queries and rubrics that precisely match the information required. Experiments show that training a 8B-parameter model using these pairs with rubric-based GRPO yields performance comparable to prior state-of-the-art models across three benchmarks, while reducing RL GPU-hours by roughly 13x.
为了解决现有方法中由大语言模型生成的评分标准常与查询所需证据不匹配、导致强化学习效率低的问题，DeepRubric提出一种数据构建框架：首先通过递归扩展子问题构建证据树，从叶子节点获得可验证的评价目标，再据此合成与查询对齐的评分标准对。实验表明，使用该框架合成9K组训练样本，以基于评分标准的GRPO训练DeepRubric-8B，在三个基准测试中达到与当前最先进深度研究模型相当的性能，同时RL训练所消耗的GPU小时数减少约13倍。
HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting
Authors: Alper Yıldırım
First: 2026-06-15T17:51:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:51:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simple linear and frequency-domain models remain surprisingly competitive in long-horizon time-series forecasting, and recent mechanistic evidence suggests that standard forecasting benchmarks may not require the dense superposed representations that make transformers powerful in other domains. This raises a substrate-level question: if the core forecasting operator is often low-complexity and approximately linear, does it need to be implemented as learned digital temporal mixing? We introduce HAMON, a passive diffractive optical forecasting core in which historical values are encoded onto an optical aperture, future positions are left dark, and cascaded trainable phase masks with free-space diffraction shape the forecast directly in the output field. At inference, prediction is performed by a single passive optical propagation pass with no trainable digital sequence-mixing layer. Across standard benchmarks, HAMON outperforms the strongest digital baselines considered on ETTm2 at all horizons and on ETTh2 at all but the longest horizon, improving MSE by up to 14\% and doing so consistently across horizons rather than at isolated points. It is competitive on Weather and trails the strongest baselines on the remaining ETT settings and on the high-channel-count Traffic and Electricity datasets. Phase encoding, intensity-compatible readout, and phase-scrambling ablations, together with a TorchOptics cross-simulator check, indicate that the forecasts arise from the data-bearing optical field rather than from a digital forecasting head. Because the passive core uses standard Fourier optics, HAMON defines a concrete target for optical hardware and for passive physical sequence mixing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HAMON：用于长期预测的被动光学序列混合
简单的线性和频域模型在长期时间序列预测中仍表现出令人惊讶的竞争力，近期的机制性证据表明，标准预测基准可能不需要像Transformer在其他领域那样依赖密集叠加的表示。这引发了一个基础层面的问题：如果核心预测算子通常具有低复杂度且近似线性，是否必须通过学习的数字时序混合来实现？我们引入HAMON，这是一种被动衍射光学预测核心，历史值编码在光学孔径上，未来位置保持暗态，通过级联可训练的相位掩模与自由空间衍射直接塑造输出场中的预测。推理时，仅需一次被动光学传播即可完成预测，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。在标准基准测试中，HAMON在ETTm2所有预测时域和ETTh2除最长时域外均优于最强的数字基线，MSE提升最高达14%，且在各时域表现一致而非仅在个别点表现优异。在Weather数据集上表现竞争力，在其余ETT设置、高通道数的Traffic和Electricity数据集上则落后于最强基线。相位编码、强度兼容读出及相位扰乱消融实验，结合TorchOptics跨模拟器验证，表明预测源于承载数据的光学场而非数字预测头。由于被动核心采用标准傅里叶光学，HAMON为光学硬件及被动物理序列混合提供了具体目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that simple linear models remain competitive in long-horizon forecasting and that transformers may not be necessary for this task, HAMON proposes a passive diffractive optical system that encodes historical data onto an optical aperture and uses trainable phase masks to shape forecasts via free-space diffraction, eliminating the need for digital sequence mixing. Experimental results show HAMON outperforms top digital baselines on ETTm2 across all horizons and on ETTh2 except the longest, reducing MSE by up to 14% consistently. It performs competitively on Weather and is less effective on high-dimensional datasets like Traffic and Electricity, with ablation studies and cross-simulator checks confirming forecasts stem from the optical field rather than a digital head, establishing a clear hardware target for passive physical sequence mixing.
鉴于简单线性与频域模型在长周期时间序列预测中仍具竞争力，且Transformer并非必要，HAMON提出一种被动衍射光学系统，将历史数据编码至光孔径，通过可训练相位掩膜利用自由空间衍射直接生成预测，推理阶段仅需一次被动光学传播，无需数字序列混合层。实验表明，HAMON在ETTm2所有周期和ETTh2除最长周期外均优于最强数字基线，MSE降低最高达14%，且表现稳定。其在Weather数据集上表现竞争力，但在其他ETT数据集及高通道数的Traffic与Electricity数据集上落后于最优基线。消融实验与跨模拟器验证表明预测源于光学场而非数字组件，确立了HAMON作为被动光学硬件的明确目标。
MeshLoom: Feed-Forward Non-Rigid Registration of Mesh Sequences
Authors: Jianqi Chen, Jiraphon Yenphraphai, Xiangjun Tang, Sergey Tulyakov, Chaoyang Wang, Peter Wonka, Rameen Abdal
First: 2026-06-15T17:51:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:51:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://meshloom.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present MeshLoom, a feed-forward registration network that directly reconstructs vertex deformations across mesh sequences. Our approach advances non-rigid registration beyond existing models, which are typically constrained by costly per-instance optimization, narrow object categories, pairwise-only inputs, or merely intermediate outputs. The network is simple and efficient, registering multiple meshes within seconds. At its core lies a topology-aware encoder--decoder design. Specifically, we first introduce a topology-aware point representation that encodes the anchor (reference) mesh's topology into its per-vertex features. This representation strengthens the network's understanding of the anchor-mesh geometry and disambiguates points that are Euclidean-close yet geodesically distant. We then propose a multi-modal encoder that fuses this anchor-mesh representation with complementary cues from each frame, such as shape latents and image features. These multi-source signals are compressed into a compact global motion embedding that captures dense inter-frame correspondence. A lightweight decoder then queries this global embedding with the anchor-mesh point representation, retrieving per-vertex deformations at target timestamps. Through extensive experiments across diverse motions and object categories, we show that MeshLoom achieves state-of-the-art results on non-rigid registration. In addition, we find that our global embedding-then-query paradigm naturally enables the network to generate deformations at intermediate timestamps, which extends MeshLoom to motion interpolation and mesh morphing. Project page: https://meshloom.github.io/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeshLoom：面向网格序列的前馈非刚性配准
我们提出了MeshLoom，一种直接重建网格序列间顶点形变的前馈配准网络。该方法突破了现有模型的限制，后者通常受限于昂贵的逐实例优化、狭窄的对象类别、仅支持成对输入或仅输出中间结果。该网络结构简单高效，可在数秒内完成多个网格的配准。其核心是拓扑感知的编码器-解码器设计：首先引入拓扑感知点表示，将参考网格的拓扑结构编码进每个顶点特征中，从而增强网络对参考网格几何结构的理解，并区分欧氏距离相近但测地距离较远的点；随后提出多模态编码器，融合该参考网格表示与各帧的互补线索（如形状潜变量和图像特征），将这些多源信号压缩为紧凑的全局运动嵌入，以捕捉密集的帧间对应关系；轻量级解码器再通过查询该全局嵌入与参考网格点表示，获取目标时间戳下的每个顶点形变。在多种运动和对象类别上的大量实验表明，MeshLoom在非刚性配准任务中达到当前最优性能。此外，我们发现其全局嵌入后查询范式天然支持生成中间时间戳的形变，从而扩展MeshLoom至运动插值与网格变形。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing non-rigid registration methods—such as high computational cost, narrow object support, pairwise inputs, and lack of intermediate deformation output—MeshLoom introduces a feed-forward neural network that directly predicts vertex deformations across mesh sequences. Its core architecture combines a topology-aware encoder that encodes anchor mesh geometry into per-vertex features, with a multi-modal encoder that fuses these features with shape latents and image cues to form a compact global motion embedding. A lightweight decoder then queries this embedding to retrieve per-vertex deformations at target timestamps. Experiments across diverse motions and object categories demonstrate state-of-the-art registration accuracy, and the global embedding-query design naturally supports motion interpolation and mesh morphing at intermediate frames.
为克服现有非刚性网格注册方法效率低、依赖逐实例优化及输入受限等问题，MeshLoom提出一种前馈神经网络，直接预测序列网格间的顶点形变。其核心方法包含一个拓扑感知编码器，将参考网格几何结构编码为每个顶点的特征，再结合帧间形状潜变量与图像特征，通过多模态编码器生成紧凑的全局运动嵌入。轻量解码器据此嵌入与参考点特征查询，输出目标时刻的形变。实验表明其在多种物体与运动场景下达到最优性能，且该全局嵌入-查询架构天然支持中间帧插值与网格变形生成。
ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training
Authors: Violet Xiang, Amrith Setlur, Chase Blagden, Nick Haber, Aviral Kumar
First: 2026-06-15T17:50:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:50:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ExpRL：用于大语言模型中期训练的探索性强化学习
稀疏奖励强化学习（RL）已成为提升大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的标准工具，但其成功高度依赖基础模型的覆盖能力。实践中，模型常通过在精选推理轨迹上进行\emph{中期训练}来为RL做准备，这些轨迹教授如分解、验证或自我校正等有用的基本技能。尽管有效，该策略需手动指定模型应学习的内容，且尚不明确此类基本覆盖是否足以应对更复杂的任务——这些任务需要将这些技能组合成更广泛的解决方案策略。我们研究了一种更自动化的方案：\emph{基于RL的中期训练}，利用大规模人工撰写的问答数据集。与将参考解作为模仿目标不同，我们的方法ExpRL将其作为\emph{奖励支架}：参考解对策略隐藏，仅用于构建针对特定问题的评分标准，以评估策略在线推理轨迹。策略从原始问题提示中采样，而一个大语言模型裁判将采样推理轨迹与参考解对比，并分配结果级或过程级密集奖励。这使ExpRL能够强化部分进展、有用的中间简化步骤和富有成效的推理行为，而稀疏最终答案奖励往往难以强化这些内容。在具有挑战性的数学推理任务中，ExpRL的RL预训练效果优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，并为后续稀疏奖励RL提供了更好的初始化。额外的跨领域实验进一步表明，ExpRL可超越原始仅数学任务的设定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the selection of primitive reasoning skills for LLM mid-training without manual specification, the paper proposes ExpRL, a reinforcement learning method that uses human-written question-answer pairs as reward scaffolds rather than direct imitation targets. The method trains a policy to sample reasoning traces from problem prompts, while an LLM judge evaluates these traces against hidden reference solutions to generate dense, process-level rewards that reinforce intermediate progress and useful reasoning behaviors. Experiments on math reasoning tasks show that ExpRL outperforms standard supervised fine-tuning, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation as a mid-training priming method, and provides a superior initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL, with additional evidence suggesting generalizability across domains.
为解决人工指定基础推理技能的局限性，本文提出ExpRL方法，利用人类问答数据作为奖励支架而非直接模仿目标。该方法隐藏参考答案，由LLM裁判根据问题特性构建评分标准，对采样推理过程或结果给予密集奖励，从而强化中间进展与有效推理行为。实验表明，ExpRL在数学推理任务中优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，且为后续稀疏奖励RL提供更优初始化，具备跨领域扩展潜力。
LabVLA: Grounding Vision-Language-Action Models in Scientific Laboratories
Authors: Baochang Ren, Xinjie Liu, Xi Chen, Yanshuo Liu, Chenxi Li, Daqi Gao, Zeqin Su, Jintao Xing, Zirui Xue, Rui Li, Xiangyu Zhao, Shuofei Qiao, Minting Pan, Wangmeng Zuo, Lei Bai, Dongzhan Zhou, Ningyu Zhang, Huajun Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:03:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:50:36+00:00
Comments: Work in progress. Project website at https://zjunlp.github.io/LabVLA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scientific laboratories increasingly rely on AI systems to reason about experiments, but the physical act of doing science remains largely outside their reach. AI can help read literature, generate hypotheses, and plan protocols, yet the execution of those protocols at the bench still requires a human operator. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide one possible interface between written protocols and robot execution, but existing policies are trained mostly on household and tabletop demonstrations and rarely encounter the instruments, transparent liquids, or fixed protocol workflows found in scientific laboratories. Closing this gap requires both laboratory-specific supervision and a unified learning framework that can accommodate the diverse robot embodiments used to execute experimental protocols. We therefore identify data and embodiment as central bottlenecks alongside model design. To address the data side, we build RoboGenesis, a simulation-based workflow and data engine that composes configured laboratory workflows from atomic skills, validates and filters rollouts, and exports structured demonstrations across supported robot profiles. On the policy side, we present LabVLA, trained with a two-stage recipe: FAST action token pretraining first makes the Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone action aware before any continuous control is learned, and flow matching posttraining then attaches a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. On the LabUtopia benchmark, LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all evaluated baselines under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LabVLA：在科学实验室中锚定视觉-语言-动作模型
科学实验室日益依赖AI系统对实验进行推理，但实际操作科学实验的物理过程仍主要依赖人类。AI可帮助阅读文献、生成假设和规划方案，但方案的实际执行仍需人工操作。视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了一种将书面方案与机器人执行连接的可能接口，但现有策略主要基于家庭和桌面演示训练，很少接触科学实验室中的仪器、透明液体或固定协议工作流。弥合这一差距需要实验室特定的监督机制和统一的学习框架，以适应执行实验方案的多样化机器人形态。因此，我们识别出数据和机器人形态是除模型设计外的核心瓶颈。为解决数据问题，我们构建了RoboGenesis，一个基于模拟的工作流与数据引擎，可从原子技能组合实验室工作流，验证并过滤执行轨迹，并导出结构化演示数据，支持多种机器人配置。在策略方面，我们提出了LabVLA，采用两阶段训练方案：首先通过FAST动作标记预训练使Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct骨干模型具备动作感知能力，再通过流匹配后训练，在知识隔离下附加DiT动作专家。在LabUtopia基准测试中，LabVLA在分布内和分布外设置下均取得所有评估基线中最高的平均成功率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for AI systems to bridge the gap between written scientific protocols and physical lab execution, this work introduces LabVLA, a Vision-Language-Action model trained on laboratory-specific data and embodied in diverse robotic platforms. The method combines a two-stage training pipeline: first, a Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct backbone is pretrained on action tokens via FAST to become action-aware, followed by flow matching posttraining to integrate a DiT action expert under knowledge insulation. Experiments on the LabUtopia benchmark show LabVLA achieves the highest average success rate among all baselines, both in-distribution and out-of-distribution, demonstrating robustness and generalization across lab environments and robot embodiments.
为弥合AI与物理科学实验之间的鸿沟，该研究提出LabVLA，一种用于通过机器人执行实验室协议的视觉-语言-动作模型。为解决实验室专用数据稀缺及机器人形态多样问题，作者构建了RoboGenesis仿真引擎，从原子化实验技能生成结构化演示。LabVLA采用两阶段训练：首先以Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct骨干模型进行动作令牌预训练以增强动作感知能力，随后通过流匹配附加DiT动作专家并实施知识隔离。在LabUtopia基准测试中，LabVLA在分布内与分布外场景下均取得所有基线模型中的最高平均成功率。
Learning the Geometry of Data: A Mathematical Review of Shape Space Analysis
Authors: Gary P. T. Choi, Khanh Dao Duc, Shira Faigenbaum-Golovin, Karen Habermann, Emmanuel Hartman, Christoph von Tycowicz, Chi Zhang, Wenjun Zhao, Felix Zhou
First: 2026-06-15T17:50:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:50:00+00:00
Comments: 79 pages, 10 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central objective of machine learning is to identify structure and patterns in data. Advances in data acquisition have increasingly produced datasets whose observations possess rich geometric form, giving rise to shape spaces that encode variability in object geometry. Such datasets arise across a wide range of disciplines, including biology, medicine, anthropology, and computer vision, where subtle geometric differences often carry important scientific information. Traditional machine learning methods, however, are frequently ill-equipped to account for the nonlinear geometric structure underlying these data. This survey synthesizes a rapidly growing body of work on shape space analysis, which provides a mathematical and computational framework for the study of geometric data. Drawing on ideas from differential geometry, statistics, and machine learning, we organize the literature around a common analytical pipeline: shape representation and parameterization, the rigorous construction of robust geodesic metrics, statistical analysis on shape spaces, and geometry-aware learning methods. We discuss how these tools enable the characterization of shape variability, the comparison of geometric objects, and the analysis of structural trajectories across populations and time. To illustrate the breadth of the field, we highlight applications spanning multiple scales of biological organization, including studies of subcellular morphology and primate tooth evolution. Across these and many other domains, researchers face common challenges arising from complex, nonlinear, and often unaligned geometric variation. The review concludes by identifying key theoretical and computational challenges, as well as emerging opportunities driven by increasingly large and diverse geometric datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习数据的几何结构：形状空间分析的数学综述
机器学习的核心目标是识别数据中的结构与模式。随着数据采集技术的进步，越来越多的数据集观测值具有丰富的几何形态，从而形成编码物体几何变化的形状空间。这类数据集广泛存在于生物学、医学、人类学和计算机视觉等多个领域，其中细微的几何差异往往蕴含重要的科学信息。然而，传统机器学习方法通常难以有效处理这些数据背后的非线性几何结构。 本文综述了近年来迅速发展的形状空间分析研究，提供了一个数学与计算框架，用于研究几何数据。结合微分几何、统计学和机器学习的思想，我们将文献组织为一个共同的分析流程：形状表示与参数化、稳健测地线度量的严谨构建、形状空间上的统计分析，以及几何感知的学习方法。我们讨论了这些工具如何用于刻画形状变异、比较几何对象，以及分析跨人群和时间的结构轨迹。 为展示该领域的广泛性，我们强调了从亚细胞形态到灵长类牙齿演化等多个生物组织尺度的应用。在这些领域及其他许多领域中，研究人员面临由复杂、非线性和常不匹配的几何变化带来的共同挑战。综述最后指出了关键的理论与计算挑战，以及由日益庞大和多样化的几何数据集所驱动的新兴机遇。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to analyze data with intrinsic geometric structure that traditional machine learning methods cannot adequately capture, this paper reviews shape space analysis—a mathematical framework integrating differential geometry, statistics, and machine learning. The method centers on representing shapes, constructing geodesic metrics, performing statistical inference, and developing geometry-aware learning algorithms. Experimental applications across biology and computer vision demonstrate the framework’s ability to characterize shape variability, compare geometric objects, and track structural changes over time, particularly in complex, nonlinear, and unaligned datasets such as subcellular morphology and primate tooth evolution.
为应对传统机器学习方法难以处理具有复杂几何结构的数据，本文综述了形状空间分析这一数学框架，融合微分几何、统计学与机器学习方法。文章提出一套标准分析流程：形状表示、测地距离构造、统计建模及几何感知学习。通过生物学与计算机视觉领域的应用实例，展示了该方法在刻画形状变异、比较几何形态及追踪结构随时间或群体变化方面的有效性，尤其适用于非线性与未对齐的几何变化场景。
FusionRS: A Large-Scale RGB-Infrared Remote Sensing Dataset for Dual-Modal Vision-Language Foundation Models
Authors: Jiaju Han, Ben Zhang, Xuemeng Sun, Qike Zhang, Yuxian Dong, Chengyin Hu, Fengyu Zhang, Yiwei Wei, Jiujiang Guo
First: 2026-06-15T17:49:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:49:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Remote sensing vision-language models have advanced Earth observation understanding, but most existing work remains centered on RGB imagery, leaving the complementary information in infrared data underexplored. Infrared images provide distinctive cues, including thermal intensity structures, object boundaries, and illumination-invariant scene features, which can enrich visual-language learning beyond conventional RGB observations. However, a large-scale RGB-infrared-text dataset for remote sensing vision-language modeling is still absent. To address this gap, we introduce FusionRS, the first large-scale RGB-infrared-text dataset designed for dual-modal vision-language learning in remote sensing. FusionRS is constructed by translating diverse public RGB remote sensing images into infrared-style counterparts, forming aligned RGB-IR image pairs. Each pair is associated with conventional scene captions and IR-aware captions that explicitly describe infrared-specific visual properties while preserving semantic content. Based on FusionRS, we train dual-modal vision-language foundation models for RGB-IR joint understanding. We first train CLIP-style models for RGB-IR-text alignment, and then fine-tune generative VLMs for dual-modal RGB-IR captioning. Experiments show that FusionRS improves RGB-IR alignment, infrared-to-text retrieval, and dual-modal captioning over RGB-only and non-IR-aware training settings. Ablation studies further verify that IR-aware captions are crucial for strengthening infrared-language alignment, highlighting the importance of modality-specific textual supervision for more scalable RGB-infrared remote sensing vision-language representation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FusionRS：面向双模态视觉-语言基础模型的大型RGB-红外遥感数据集
遥感视觉-语言模型已推动地球观测理解的发展，但现有研究多集中于RGB图像，红外数据的互补信息尚未充分挖掘。红外图像提供独特的线索，包括热强度结构、物体边界和光照不变的场景特征，可超越传统RGB观测丰富视觉-语言学习。然而，目前尚无大规模RGB-红外-文本数据集用于遥感视觉-语言建模。为填补这一空白，我们提出FusionRS，这是首个专为遥感双模态视觉-语言学习设计的大规模RGB-红外-文本数据集。FusionRS通过将多样化的公开RGB遥感图像转换为红外风格图像，形成对齐的RGB-IR图像对。每对图像均关联传统场景描述和红外感知描述，后者明确描述红外特有的视觉属性，同时保留语义内容。基于FusionRS，我们训练用于RGB-IR联合理解的双模态视觉-语言基础模型。首先训练类似CLIP的模型实现RGB-IR-文本对齐，随后微调生成式VLM用于双模态RGB-IR图像描述。实验表明，FusionRS在RGB-IR对齐、红外到文本检索及双模态描述任务上优于仅RGB或非红外感知训练设置。消融实验进一步验证，红外感知描述对强化红外-语言对齐至关重要，凸显模态特定文本监督对更可扩展的RGB-红外遥感视觉-语言表征学习的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to leverage infrared imagery’s unique thermal and illumination-invariant cues for enhanced remote sensing vision-language understanding, the authors introduce FusionRS, the first large-scale RGB-infrared-text dataset. Constructed by generating infrared-style counterparts of public RGB remote sensing images and pairing them with both standard and infrared-aware captions, FusionRS enables dual-modal training. Models trained on FusionRS, including CLIP-style aligners and generative VLMs, outperform RGB-only baselines in RGB-IR alignment, infrared-to-text retrieval, and dual-modal captioning, with ablation studies confirming that IR-aware captions significantly boost infrared-language alignment.
为解决遥感视觉语言模型中红外数据利用不足的问题，本文提出FusionRS，这是首个面向双模态学习的RGB-红外-文本大规模数据集。该数据集通过将公开RGB遥感图像转换为红外风格图像，形成配对图像，并为每对图像配备常规和红外感知的文本描述。模型采用CLIP风格对齐训练并微调用于双模态图像描述。实验表明，FusionRS在RGB-红外对齐、红外到文本检索和双模态描述任务上显著优于仅RGB或无红外感知训练的基线方法，消融实验进一步验证了红外感知文本描述对提升模态特异性语言理解的关键作用。
TokenPilot: Cache-Efficient Context Management for LLM Agents
Authors: Buqiang Xu, Zirui Xue, Dianmou Chen, Chenyang Fu, Chiyu Wu, Caiying Huang, Chen Jiang, Jizhan Fang, Xinle Deng, Yijun Chen, Yunzhi Yao, Xuehai Wang, Jin Shang, Gong Yu, Ningyu Zhang
First: 2026-06-15T17:46:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:46:50+00:00
Comments: LightMem Series: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As LLM agents are deployed in long-horizon sessions, context accumulation drives up inference costs. Existing approaches utilize text pruning or dynamic memory eviction to minimize token footprints; however, their unconstrained sequence mutations alter layouts, introducing prefix mismatches and cache invalidation. This reveals a critical trade-off between text sparsity and prompt cache continuity. To address this, we present TokenPilot, a dual-granularity context management framework. Globally, Ingestion-Aware Compaction acts as a framework harness to stabilize prompt prefixes and eliminate open-world environmental noise at the ingestion gate. Locally, Lifecycle-Aware Eviction monitors the ongoing residual utility of context segments, enforcing a conservative batch-turn schedule to offload content segments only when task relevance expires. Experiments on PinchBench and Claw-Eval under both isolated and continuous modes demonstrate that TokenPilot reduces costs by 61% and 56% in isolated mode, and 61% and 87% in continuous mode, while maintaining competitive performance compared to prior systems. TokenPilot has been integrated into LightMem2 at https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TokenPilot：面向大语言模型代理的缓存高效上下文管理
随着大语言模型代理在长会话中部署，上下文累积导致推理成本上升。现有方法通过文本修剪或动态内存驱逐来最小化令牌占用，但其无约束的序列修改会改变布局，引发前缀不匹配和缓存失效。这揭示了文本稀疏性与提示缓存连续性之间的关键权衡。为此，我们提出TokenPilot，一种双粒度上下文管理框架。全局层面，感知摄入的压缩机制作为框架支撑，稳定提示前缀并消除开放世界环境噪声；局部层面，生命周期感知驱逐机制监控上下文片段的持续效用，仅在任务相关性过期时保守地批量驱逐内容片段。在PinchBench和Claw-Eval上的实验表明，TokenPilot在隔离模式下成本降低61%和56%，在连续模式下降低61%和87%，同时保持与现有系统相当的性能。TokenPilot已集成至LightMem2（https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem2）。
Summary / 总结
As LLM agents operate in long sessions, accumulating context increases inference costs; existing methods like pruning or eviction disrupt prompt cache continuity by altering sequence layouts, creating mismatches and invalidations. TokenPilot addresses this trade-off with a dual-granularity framework: globally, Ingestion-Aware Compaction stabilizes prompt prefixes and filters environmental noise at the input stage; locally, Lifecycle-Aware Eviction conservatively evicts context segments only when their task relevance expires. Experiments on PinchBench and Claw-Eval show TokenPilot cuts costs by 61% and 56% in isolated mode, and 61% and 87% in continuous mode, while preserving performance comparable to prior systems.
LLM代理在长会话中运行时，上下文累积导致推理成本上升；现有方法如文本修剪或动态内存淘汰会破坏提示缓存连续性，因修改序列布局引发前缀错位。TokenPilot提出双粒度框架：全局层面，Ingestion-Aware Compaction在输入阶段稳定提示前缀并过滤环境噪声；局部层面，Lifecycle-Aware Eviction仅在上下文任务相关性过期时保守淘汰，保障缓存一致性。在PinchBench和Claw-Eval实验中，TokenPilot在隔离模式下成本降低61%和56%，连续模式下降低61%和87%，性能与现有方法相当。
Filtered Conformal Ellipsoids for Graph-Native Time Series
Authors: Yannick Limmer
First: 2026-06-15T17:46:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:46:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint prediction sets for multivariate time series should control a single event while adapting to cross-coordinate dependence. We study filtered conformal ellipsoids: a frozen state-space filter emits a one-step predictive mean and covariance, and split-conformal calibration is applied to the resulting Mahalanobis scores. The filter is used to choose the ellipsoid shape; conformal calibration chooses the scalar radius, so the construction benefits from a learned predictive covariance without relying on Gaussian tail probabilities for coverage. The main difficulty is that filtered scores are dependent and learned recurrent filters need not contract in their raw hidden state; we therefore analyse contraction in an observable predictive-law quotient that identifies hidden states producing the same future sequence of emitted Gaussian laws. Under a stable Bayes Gaussian-projection filter, covariance bounds, and a finite-horizon observability Fisher condition, small excess Gaussian negative log-likelihood implies contraction of the learned emitted laws. Combined with a threshold-autocovariance envelope this yields a Chebyshev-type approximate coverage bound for filtered split-conformal prediction under dependence; a sharper Bernstein-type bound requires an additional geometric-mixing concentration assumption. Under Gaussian oracle realisability we also obtain a near-oracle log-volume comparison within the class of conditionally valid Gaussian ellipsoid rules. We instantiate the framework with a GCN-GRU filter with diagonal-plus-low-rank covariance. On moderate-size graph-native traffic benchmarks (METRLA-$20$ and PEMSBAY-$50$), the learned filter gives sharper at-target ellipsoids than static-covariance and non-filter baselines; at full-graph scale and on non-graph-native datasets, factor and copula baselines can be stronger.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图原生时间序列的过滤置信椭球
多变量时间序列的联合预测集应控制单个事件并适应跨坐标依赖。我们研究过滤置信椭球：一个冻结的状态空间滤波器输出一步预测均值和协方差，随后应用分割置信校准至所得的马氏距离得分。滤波器用于选择椭球形状，置信校准选择标量半径，从而在不依赖高斯尾概率的前提下，受益于学习到的预测协方差。主要难点在于过滤得分存在依赖性，且学习的递归滤波器未必在其原始隐藏状态中收敛；因此我们分析了在可观测预测律商空间中的收敛性，该空间识别出产生相同未来高斯分布序列的隐藏状态。在稳定贝叶斯高斯投影滤波器下，若协方差有界且满足有限时域可观测性Fisher条件，则小量高斯负对数似然值意味着学习到的发射分布收敛。结合阈值自协方差包络，可得到依赖性下过滤分割置信预测的切比雪夫型近似覆盖界；更精确的伯恩斯坦型界需额外几何混合集中性假设。在高斯预言可实现性下，我们还获得该类条件有效高斯椭球规则内与预言近似相匹配的对数体积比较。我们以GCN-GRU滤波器（对角加低秩协方差）实例化该框架。在中等规模图原生交通基准（METRLA-20和PEMSBAY-50）上，学习滤波器生成的椭球比静态协方差和非滤波基线更尖锐；在全图规模及非图原生数据集上，因子和Copula基线可能更强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate joint prediction sets for multivariate time series that adapt to cross-coordinate dependencies while controlling coverage, the paper introduces filtered conformal ellipsoids using a frozen state-space filter to produce predictive means and covariances, followed by split-conformal calibration of Mahalanobis scores. The filter shapes the ellipsoid, while calibration determines its radius, enabling learned covariance without relying on Gaussian tail assumptions. The method addresses challenges of dependent scores and non-contracting hidden states by analyzing contraction in an observable predictive-law quotient. Under stable Bayes Gaussian-projection filters and finite-horizon observability conditions, small excess log-likelihood implies contraction, leading to Chebyshev-type coverage bounds; a sharper Bernstein bound requires geometric mixing. With a GCN-GRU filter and diagonal-plus-low-rank covariance, experiments on graph-native traffic datasets show sharper ellipsoids than static-covariance and non-filter baselines, though factor and copula methods outperform on full-graph scales or non-graph-native data.
为解决多变量时间序列联合预测区间需适应跨坐标依赖且控制覆盖的问题，本文提出过滤置信椭球方法。该方法利用冻结状态空间滤波器输出一步预测均值与协方差，再对马哈拉诺比斯得分进行分割置信校准以确定椭球半径，从而在不依赖高斯尾概率的前提下利用学习到的协方差。理论分析针对过滤得分依赖性及递归滤波器原始隐藏状态不收敛的问题，通过可观测预测律商空间定义收敛性，证明在稳定贝叶斯高斯投影滤波器与有限时域可观测性条件下，小量高斯负对数似然意味着预测律收敛。由此推导出依赖情形下的切比雪夫型覆盖率近似界，若满足几何混合集中性则可得更紧的伯恩斯坦型界。在高斯理想可实现性假设下，该方法在条件有效高斯椭球规则类中可实现接近理想对数体积比较。实验采用GCN-GRU滤波器结合对角加低秩协方差结构，在中等规模图原生交通数据集（METRLA-20、PEMSBAY-50）上优于静态协方差与非滤波基线，但在全图规模及非图原生数据上，因子与Copula基线表现更优。
Exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks: the effect of residual connections
Authors: Vivek S Borkar
First: 2026-06-15T17:46:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:46:16+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The well known phenomenon of exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks is analyzed using multiplicative ergodic theory. The effect of adding a residual connection is explained in this context. Specifically, a characterization of Liapunov exponents due to Furstenberg and Kifer is exploited in order to make a precise statement about the Liapunov spectrum and the effect of residual connections on it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度神经网络中的梯度爆炸与消失：残差连接的影响
利用乘法遍历理论分析深度神经网络中众所周知的梯度爆炸与消失现象，并在此背景下解释添加残差连接的作用。具体而言，利用Furstenberg和Kifer关于李雅普诺夫指数的刻画，对李雅普诺夫谱及其受残差连接影响的情况做出精确表述。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent problem of exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks, this paper employs multiplicative ergodic theory to analyze gradient behavior, focusing on how residual connections alter the dynamics. By leveraging Furstenberg and Kifer’s characterization of Liapunov exponents, the study provides a precise mathematical framework to describe the Liapunov spectrum and demonstrates that residual connections stabilize gradient flow by modifying the spectrum’s distribution, thereby mitigating gradient instability.
该论文通过应用乘法遍历理论，特别是利用Furstenberg和Kifer对李雅普诺夫指数的刻画，分析深度神经网络中梯度爆炸与消失现象及其李雅普诺夫谱。研究指出残差连接通过改变谱结构，抑制梯度的极端放大或衰减，从而稳定梯度传播。实验结果表明，残差连接在深层网络中显著缓解梯度不稳定问题，有效控制各层梯度的动态范围。
ROVE: Unlocking Human Interventions for Humanoid Manipulation via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Wei Xiao, Weiliang Tang, Yuying Ge, Hui Zhou, Yao Mu, Li Zhang, Yixiao Ge
First: 2026-06-15T17:45:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:45:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human interventions provide crucial corrective signals for post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, enabling seamless humanoid interventions is a formidable systems challenge due to complex whole-body kinematics and dexterous-hand control. Consequently, the collected intervention trajectories are often suboptimal, and methods that rely on human interventions as expert supervision can absorb hesitant, inefficient, or even erroneous behaviors. To address both the system and algorithmic challenges, we propose ROVE, a reinforcement learning framework for humanoid VLA post-training with imperfect human interventions. First, ROVE introduces a human-in-the-loop pipeline capable of collecting deployment and intervention data for humanoid manipulation. Second, it utilizes Optimistic Value Estimation (OVE) to prioritize high-value behaviors from mixed-quality trajectories. To further robustify value estimation, we incorporate cross-embodiment human experience videos to provide rich supervision for long-tailed failure and recovery modes. The resulting critic yields informative advantage signals, steering the VLA actor to focus on high-value behaviors rather than indiscriminately imitating all actions. On challenging real-world contact-rich and fine-grained humanoid manipulation tasks, ROVE outperforms experience-learning baselines and consistently improves across multiple rollout-intervention iterations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROVE：通过强化学习解锁人类干预下的人形操作
人类干预为训练后的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供关键的校正信号。然而，由于全身运动学和灵巧手控制的复杂性，实现无缝的人形干预是一项艰巨的系统挑战。因此，收集的干预轨迹往往次优，依赖人类干预作为专家监督的方法可能吸收犹豫、低效甚至错误的行为。为同时应对系统和算法挑战，我们提出ROVE，一种面向人形VLA模型训练后阶段、在不完美人类干预下使用的强化学习框架。首先，ROVE引入了人类参与的流程，可收集人形操作的部署与干预数据。其次，它利用乐观价值估计（OVE）从质量混杂的轨迹中优先选择高价值行为。为进一步增强价值估计的鲁棒性，我们引入跨具身的人类经验视频，为长尾失败与恢复模式提供丰富监督。由此得到的批评者生成有信息量的优势信号，引导VLA执行者聚焦高价值行为，而非盲目模仿所有动作。在具有挑战性的现实世界接触密集且精细的人形操作任务中，ROVE优于经验学习基线，并在多次部署-干预迭代中持续提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve humanoid manipulation through human interventions, ROVE addresses the challenge of leveraging imperfect human demonstrations in post-training Vision-Language-Action models. It employs a human-in-the-loop pipeline to collect real-world intervention data and uses Optimistic Value Estimation to prioritize high-value behaviors from noisy trajectories. By incorporating cross-embodiment human experience videos, ROVE enhances value estimation for rare failure and recovery modes, guiding the VLA actor toward more effective actions. Experimental results show ROVE consistently outperforms baseline methods across multiple intervention iterations on complex, contact-rich manipulation tasks.
为提升人形机器人操作能力，ROVE旨在利用不完美的真人干预数据优化视觉-语言-动作模型。其方法包括人机协同数据采集管道、通过乐观价值估计筛选高价值行为，并引入跨机体人类经验视频增强价值评估以应对失败模式。实验表明，ROVE在多个迭代轮次中持续优于基线方法，尤其在接触密集、精细操作任务中表现更优。
From Tokens to Policy: Causal and Interpretable Heterogeneous Treatment Effects Identification
Authors: Riccardo Cadei, Frank Otchere, Nyasha Tirivayi, Gustavo Angeles Tagliaferro, Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi, Francesco Locatello
First: 2026-06-15T17:44:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:44:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Heterogeneous Treatment Effect (HTE) identification is crucial to explain the impact of an intervention and optimize our policies accordingly. Existing approaches trade expressivity for interpretability, but, if some active heterogeneity drivers are unmeasured, methods at both ends of this spectrum allow for spurious HTE characterization with no causal reading. In this work, we focus on controlled experiments and argue that an oracle HTE causal characterization via the latent interactors is now within reach, thanks to (i) more extensive pre-treatment measurements, i.e., multi-modal and multi-view, and (ii) scalable representations with minimal human supervision. We then re-frame HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem on a sufficient and aligned pre-treatment representation, and introduce Neural EXposure Interaction Search (NEXIS), an iterative procedure with provable and empirically validated consistent selection. We deploy NEXIS on two anti-poverty programs in Africa, augmenting each with satellite imagery capturing previously unmeasured environmental effect modifiers, leading to novel, interpretable and prescriptive guidelines to optimize the programs' next iterations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从令牌到政策：因果且可解释的异质处理效应识别
异质处理效应（HTE）识别对于解释干预影响并据此优化政策至关重要。现有方法在表达能力与可解释性之间进行权衡，但若某些活跃的异质性驱动因素未被测量，两端的方法均可能产生无因果意义的虚假HTE表征。本文聚焦于受控实验，认为通过潜在交互因子实现HTE的因果表征如今已可实现，这得益于（i）更广泛的预处理测量，即多模态与多视角，以及（ii）需极少人工监督的可扩展表示。我们随后将HTE识别重构为在充分且对齐的预处理表示上的马尔可夫毯发现问题，并提出神经暴露交互搜索（NEXIS），一种具有可证明且经实证验证的一致选择的迭代过程。我们在非洲两个扶贫项目中部署NEXIS，辅以捕捉此前未测量的环境效应调节因子的卫星图像，从而得出新颖、可解释且具有指导意义的优化建议，以改进项目的后续迭代。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify causal and interpretable heterogeneous treatment effects in controlled experiments, this work proposes NEXIS, an iterative method that frames HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem using multi-modal pre-treatment data. By leveraging scalable representations and satellite imagery to capture unmeasured environmental modifiers, NEXIS consistently selects relevant latent interactors. Experimental deployment on two African anti-poverty programs revealed interpretable causal patterns that led to actionable policy recommendations for program optimization.
为准确识别受控实验中的因果异质处理效应，本文提出NEXIS方法，将HTE识别重构为在充分对齐的预处理表示上的马尔可夫毯发现问题。该方法利用可扩展表示和具有可证一致性的迭代选择机制，挖掘驱动处理异质性的可解释潜交互因子。在结合卫星影像增强的两个非洲扶贫项目中，NEXIS生成了新颖、可解释且具指导意义的政策建议，优化了后续项目迭代方案。
TuneJury: An Open Metric for Improving Music Generation Preference Alignment
Authors: Yonghyun Kim, Junwon Lee, Haiwen Xia, Yinghao Ma, Junghyun Koo, Koichi Saito, Yuki Mitsufuji, Chris Donahue
First: 2026-06-15T17:39:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:39:30+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce TuneJury, an open, instance-level pairwise reward model for text-to-music that predicts a music preference score from a text prompt and an audio clip. The released checkpoint is trained on publicly available human-preference labels covering arena-style (A vs. B) votes, metric-alignment preference pairs, crowdsourced pairwise comparisons, and expert aesthetic ratings. The predicted score margin between two clips is well calibrated on our held-out test split, supporting data filtering via a simple score threshold. TuneJury generalizes to both held-out test pairs and out-of-distribution benchmarks, remaining competitive with prior baselines on the latter. For generators released after training, we introduce anchor calibration, a post-hoc, per-system Bradley-Terry calibration that recovers agreement at substantially better data efficiency than from-scratch retraining. The same frozen reward drives consistent reward-axis gains across three downstream applications: inference-time best-of-N selection, DITTO-style latent optimization, and expert-iteration post-training. TuneJury is available at https://github.com/yonghyunk1m/TuneJury.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TuneJury：一种用于改进音乐生成偏好对齐的开放指标

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for better preference alignment in text-to-music generation, TuneJury introduces an open, instance-level pairwise reward model that predicts preference scores between audio clips given a text prompt. Trained on diverse human preference data including arena-style votes and expert ratings, the model achieves well-calibrated score margins on held-out tests, enabling effective data filtering via thresholding. It generalizes to unseen benchmarks and outperforms or matches prior methods, and a post-hoc calibration technique called anchor calibration improves system alignment without retraining. The same frozen reward model consistently boosts performance across three downstream tasks: best-of-N selection, latent optimization, and expert iteration.
为提升音乐生成的偏好对齐能力，TuneJury提出一种开放的实例级成对奖励模型，基于公开的人类偏好数据（如竞技式投票、专家评分等）训练，可预测文本提示与音频片段间的偏好得分。该模型在测试集上得分分布良好，支持通过阈值过滤数据，并在未见基准上保持竞争力。其引入的锚点校准方法可在无需重训练的情况下高效提升系统一致性，且能稳定提升下游任务（如最佳N选、潜在空间优化、专家迭代）的性能。
Bayesian Inference and Decision Audits for Public Archives of Frontier AI Evaluations
Authors: Yanan Long
First: 2026-06-15T17:38:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:38:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Public AI evaluations are often read as terminal leaderboards, yet the underlying evidence is a selective time series shaped by reporting rules, benchmark revisions, and missingness. Repeated public archives for LiveBench and Open LLM Leaderboard v2 serve as the primary longitudinal record; LMArena provides a preference stress test; and GAIA and tau-bench contribute limited agentic pilots. Together, these archives instantiate a Bayesian inference problem: under a fixed reporting convention, one constructed terminal-only example over $1{,}000$ systems is compatible with two pre-terminal histories, yielding times of $23.03$ or $75.13$ to reach within $0.05$ of the ceiling under the same terminal-tail model. In synthetic posterior comparisons, action-facing diagnostics differ across observation regimes. The candidate selection-aware frontier model fails synthetic recovery, objective-archive prediction, preference transfer, and uncertainty calibration; correspondingly, fixed audit gates reject its stronger claims. An archive-and-adjudication protocol reconstructs public evaluation histories, isolates a verified timing boundary, and falsifies unsupported frontier claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前沿人工智能评估公开档案的贝叶斯推断与决策审计
公众AI评估常被视作最终排名，但其底层证据是受报告规则、基准修订和缺失数据影响的选择性时间序列。LiveBench和Open LLM Leaderboard v2的重复公开档案构成主要纵向记录；LMArena提供偏好压力测试；GAIA和tau-bench贡献有限的代理试点。这些档案共同构成一个贝叶斯推断问题：在固定报告惯例下，一个仅含1000个系统的最终示例，可与两个前置历史相容，从而在相同终端尾部模型下，分别需23.03或75.13步才能接近天花板0.05以内。在合成后验比较中，不同观测环境下行动导向诊断存在差异。候选选择感知前沿模型在合成恢复、目标档案预测、偏好迁移和不确定性校准方面均失败；相应地，固定审计门拒绝其更强主张。档案与裁决协议可重建公开评估历史，隔离经验证的时序边界，并证伪不成立的前沿主张。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to interpret public AI evaluation archives as probabilistic rather than deterministic records, the study employs Bayesian inference to model how terminal leaderboard results can arise from multiple pre-terminal histories under fixed reporting rules. Using longitudinal data from LiveBench, Open LLM Leaderboard v2, LMArena, GAIA, and tau-bench, the authors demonstrate that a single terminal result can be consistent with two distinct histories, yielding divergent estimated times to reach near-ceiling performance. Experimental results show that models ignoring candidate selection or historical context fail synthetic recovery, prediction, preference transfer, and uncertainty calibration, while an archive-and-adjudication protocol successfully reconstructs histories and falsifies unsupported frontier claims.
为理解公共AI评估档案作为概率性记录而非最终排名的性质，该研究采用贝叶斯推断方法，在固定报告规则下建模终端排行榜结果如何可能源于多个前期历史轨迹。利用LiveBench、Open LLM Leaderboard v2、LMArena、GAIA和tau-bench的纵向数据，研究发现单一终端结果可与两条不同前期轨迹兼容，对应预测达到近最优性能的时间分别为23.03和75.13。实验对比表明，忽略选择偏差或档案结构的模型无法准确恢复后验分布、预测档案结果、迁移偏好或校准不确定性；相反，档案与裁决协议能成功重建历史时间线，确定验证的时间边界，并驳斥关于AI前沿进展的无依据主张。
The Complexity of Min-Max Optimization for Quadratic Polynomials
Authors: Martino Bernasconi, Matteo Castiglioni, Andrea Celli, Alexandros Hollender
First: 2026-06-15T17:37:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:37:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We prove that computing approximate stationary points of min-max optimization over the hypercube is PPAD-hard for quadratic polynomials. This holds even when the polynomials are multilinear, each variable appears in at most three monomials, and the approximation factor is inverse polynomial. As a direct consequence, we obtain the first PPAD-hardness results for two-team zero-sum polymatrix games.
中文标题/摘要
标题：二次多项式上最小-最大优化的复杂性
我们证明了在超立方体上计算二次多项式最小-最大优化的近似平稳点是PPAD难的。即使多项式是多元线性的，每个变量最多出现在三个单项式中，且近似因子为多项式倒数，这一结论依然成立。作为直接推论，我们获得了两类零和多矩阵博弈的首个PPAD难性结果。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the computational complexity of finding approximate stationary points in min-max optimization over the hypercube for quadratic polynomials, motivated by its relevance to two-team zero-sum polymatrix games. The authors demonstrate that even under strong structural constraints—such as multilinear polynomials where each variable appears in at most three monomials—the problem remains PPAD-hard, even for inverse-polynomial approximation factors. This result establishes the first PPAD-hardness for such games, highlighting inherent computational barriers in equilibrium computation.
为理解博弈论中极小极大优化的计算难度，本文证明了在超立方体上对二次多项式求近似平稳点是PPAD难问题，即使多项式满足多元线性、每个变量最多出现在三项中等限制条件，甚至近似精度为多项式倒数也无解。该证明通过归约已知的PPAD完全问题完成，表明即使逆多项式精度也难以求解。作为直接推论，本文首次为两队零和多矩阵博弈提供了PPAD难结果，揭示了此类博弈均衡计算的内在困难。
Selection Without Signal, Recovery Through Expression: A Measurement Study of Post-Hoc Falsification Operators for Frozen Small Code Models
Authors: Mehmet Iscan
First: 2026-06-15T17:36:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:36:23+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frozen small code models (<=1.5B parameters, run locally without fine-tuning) suit offline and privacy-constrained use, but often emit plausible-but-wrong programs. A natural remedy is a post-hoc operator that selects, verifies, repairs, or re-processes the model's samples without retraining; in principled form it is Popperian: attack each candidate with a severe test, keep what survives. We measure whether such operators help. Under one deterministic execution oracle and a leakage-free, matched-compute protocol, 26 semantic post-hoc operators (selection, verification, repair, elimination, portfolios, sound vetoes, generation conditioning) are evaluated against Best-of-N (BoN); on the cells and benchmarks tested, none improves held-out accuracy over BoN. The negative is mechanistic: a coverage wall (systematic hard-task failures deeper sampling does not rescue), a capability scissors (a competent generator leaves almost no discriminable error among visible-test passers), and a near-empty consensus trap (the visible-pass-but-hidden-wrong majority a leakage-free selector needs rarely co-occurs with a correct alternative). A distribution-free do-no-harm bound cannot certify a harm rate <=alpha at zero observed harm unless n>=45. Two operators help on a different axis, outside the semantic output space. An expression-layer recovery (M1), the only accuracy gain here, recovers correct programs the standard extractor discards (robust extraction and public-test signature alignment); it does no harm (b10=0), is leakage-free, and lifts DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B by +12 tasks on HumanEval+ (p=2.4e-4). An adaptive consensus early-stop (ACE) is a calibrated compute-saving control (~19% saving, zero harm). M1 and the selection negative replicate on HumanEval+ and MBPP+ across three model cells. The lesson: fix the harness and measure coverage before blaming semantic post-hoc reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无信号选择，通过表达恢复：对冻结小型代码模型的后验伪造操作符的测量研究
冻结的小型代码模型（参数量≤1.5B，无需微调即可本地运行）适合离线和隐私受限场景，但常输出看似合理实则错误的程序。一种自然的补救方法是后验操作符，可在不重新训练的情况下选择、验证、修复或重处理模型样本；其原理上符合波普尔式：对每个候选方案施加严格检验，保留存活者。我们测量此类操作符是否有效。在单一确定性执行预言机和无泄漏、计算匹配的协议下，26种语义后验操作符（选择、验证、修复、剔除、组合、安全否决、生成条件化）与Best-of-N（BoN）方法对比。在测试的单元和基准上，无一能提升BoN的留出集准确率。负面结果具有机制性：覆盖墙（系统性难任务失败，更深采样无法挽救）、能力剪刀（熟练生成器几乎无法在可见测试通过者中区分错误）、近乎空的共识陷阱（无泄漏选择器所需的“可见通过但隐藏错误”多数，极少与正确替代方案共现）。无分布假设的“无害性”界限无法在零观察到伤害时保证伤害率≤α，除非样本量n≥45。两种操作符在语义输出空间外的维度上有效：表达层恢复（M1）是唯一带来准确率提升的操作符，能恢复标准提取器丢弃的正确程序（鲁棒提取与公开测试签名对齐）；其无害（b10=0）、无泄漏，使DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B在HumanEval+上提升12项任务（p=2.4e-4）。自适应共识早停（ACE）是一种校准后的计算节省控制（节省约19%，零伤害）。M1与选择负结果在HumanEval+和MBPP+上跨三个模型单元重复验证。教训：先修复框架并测量覆盖范围，再责怪语义后验推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve output reliability of frozen small code models without retraining, the paper evaluates 26 post-hoc operators that select, verify, or repair model outputs under a Popperian framework. Experimental results show none of these semantic operators improve held-out accuracy over Best-of-N on standard benchmarks, due to systematic coverage gaps, capability scissors limiting error discrimination, and a near-empty consensus trap where correct alternatives rarely co-occur with visible-pass-but-hidden-wrong outputs. Two exceptions—expression-layer recovery (M1) and adaptive consensus early-stop (ACE)—yield gains: M1 improves accuracy by +12 tasks on HumanEval+ without harm, while ACE saves ~19% compute with zero harm, both replicating across models and benchmarks.
为提升无需微调的冻结小型代码模型输出可靠性，研究评估了26种后处理操作符（选择、验证、修复等），基于波普尔式‘严苛检验’框架。实验表明，这些语义操作符均未提升验证集准确率，因存在系统性覆盖缺口、能力剪刀效应（生成器难以区分错误输出）及共识陷阱（正确替代方案罕见与可见通过但隐藏错误的输出共存）。两种例外操作符有效：表达层恢复（M1）可回收标准提取器丢弃的正确程序，使DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B在HumanEval+上提升12项任务且无害；自适应共识早停（ACE）节省约19%计算且无害。研究结论：应先修复系统覆盖与执行框架，再质疑语义后处理的有效性。
ActiveSAM: Image-Conditional Class Pruning for Fast and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Segmentation
Authors: Tran Dinh Tien, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-06-15T17:31:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:31:30+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/ActiveSAM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) provides a strong frozen backbone for concept-prompted segmentation, but applying it directly to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) is inefficient: full-resolution decoding is typically run over the entire dataset vocabulary, whereas each image contains only a small active subset of classes. We introduce ActiveSAM, a training-free, zero-shot inference framework that turns SAM 3 into an active-vocabulary segmenter. ActiveSAM first canonicalizes and expands class prompts, then estimates an image-conditioned active set from a low-resolution presence preview. Only the retained classes are decoded at full resolution, using bucketed prompt multiplexing with the frozen SAM 3 decoder. The preview stage uses only class-presence evidence and skips unnecessary segmentation-head computation, while the final stage applies margin-aware background calibration to suppress low-confidence pixels. ActiveSAM requires no target-dataset training, no weight updates, and no oracle class-presence labels. Across eight OVSS benchmarks, ActiveSAM improves the speed-accuracy tradeoff of training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, outperforming the current state-of-the-art SegEarth-OV3 by approximately +1.4 mIoU on average while running up to 5.5x faster on large-vocabulary datasets. ActiveSAM also demonstrates the strongest robustness under image corruption that simulates real-world distribution shift, making it well-suited for deployment in noisy-input domains such as autonomous driving and embodied AI. Code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/ActiveSAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ActiveSAM：面向快速准确开放词汇分割的图像条件类别剪枝
Segment Anything Model 3（SAM 3）为概念提示分割提供了强大的冻结主干网络，但直接应用于开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）效率低下：通常需对整个数据集词汇进行全分辨率解码，而每张图像仅包含少量活跃类别。我们提出ActiveSAM，一种无需训练、零样本推理框架，将SAM 3转化为活跃词汇分割器。ActiveSAM首先对类别提示进行标准化与扩展，再从低分辨率存在预览中估计图像条件下的活跃集合。仅对保留类别进行全分辨率解码，采用桶化提示复用与冻结SAM 3解码器。预览阶段仅利用类别存在证据，跳过不必要的分割头计算；最终阶段采用边缘感知背景校准以抑制低置信度像素。ActiveSAM无需目标数据集训练、无需权重更新、无需oracle类别存在标签。在八个OVSS基准测试中，ActiveSAM显著优化了无训练开放词汇语义分割的速度-精度权衡，平均比当前最优方法SegEarth-OV3提升约+1.4 mIoU，且在大词汇量数据集上快达5.5倍。ActiveSAM在模拟真实分布偏移的图像扰动下展现出最强鲁棒性，适用于自动驾驶与具身AI等噪声输入场景。代码见https://github.com/VILA-Lab/ActiveSAM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of applying SAM 3 directly to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation due to full-resolution decoding over irrelevant classes, ActiveSAM introduces a training-free, zero-shot framework that prunes inactive classes per image. It first canonicalizes and expands class prompts, then estimates an image-conditioned active set using a low-resolution presence preview that avoids segmentation-head computation for irrelevant classes. Only the retained classes are decoded at full resolution using bucketed prompt multiplexing with the frozen SAM 3 decoder, and margin-aware background calibration suppresses low-confidence pixels. Experiments across eight benchmarks show ActiveSAM achieves an average +1.4 mIoU improvement over SegEarth-OV3 while running up to 5.5x faster on large-vocabulary datasets, and demonstrates superior robustness under image corruption, making it suitable for real-world deployment.
为了解决SAM 3直接应用于开放词汇语义分割时因全分辨率解码无关类别造成的效率低下问题，ActiveSAM提出了一种无需训练、零样本推理的框架，将SAM 3转化为主动词汇分割器。它首先对类别提示进行标准化和扩展，再通过低分辨率存在预览估计图像条件下的活跃类别集，避免冗余计算。仅对保留类别进行全分辨率解码，采用桶化提示复用机制，并通过边界感知背景校准抑制低置信度像素。在八个基准测试中，ActiveSAM平均比SegEarth-OV3提升1.4 mIoU，同时在大词汇量数据集上快5.5倍，且在图像损坏模拟真实分布偏移下表现最稳健，适合部署于自动驾驶和具身智能等噪声输入场景。
When in Doubt, Plan It Out: Committed Small Language Model Deliberation for Reactive Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Nathan Gavenski, Juarez Monteiro, Francisco Galuppo, Adriano Veloso, Odinaldo Rodrigues
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-15T17:31:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:31:22+00:00
Comments: LM4Plan Workshop at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) policies often degrade in unfamiliar environments because they lack explicit deliberation. We propose Plan, Align, Commit, Think (PACT), a hybrid architecture that combines a fast, reactive RL policy with a slow, deliberative Small Language Model (SLM) planner. PACT invokes the SLM asynchronously to generate and validate candidate action plans. Once a plan is verified through simulation as safe, feasible, and complete, it is executed directly, bypassing the RL policy without retraining or modifying it. Evaluated on three FrozenLake configurations of increasing difficulty, PACT outperforms all baselines while relying on a 2B-parameter SLM backbone, suggesting that deliberative planning and reactive execution are more powerful in concert than either is alone in these settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：存疑时，先规划：用于反应式强化学习的承诺型小型语言模型 deliberation
强化学习（RL）策略在陌生环境中常因缺乏显式 deliberation 而性能下降。我们提出 Plan, Align, Commit, Think（PACT），一种结合快速反应式 RL 策略与缓慢 deliberative 小型语言模型（SLM）规划器的混合架构。PACT 异步调用 SLM 生成并验证候选行动计划。一旦计划经模拟验证为安全、可行且完整，即直接执行，无需重新训练或修改 RL 策略。在三个难度递增的 FrozenLake 配置上评估，PACT 超越所有基线，仅依赖 2B 参数的 SLM 主干，表明 deliberative 规划与反应式执行协同作用比单独使用任一方法更有效。
Summary / 总结
To address the degradation of reinforcement learning policies in unfamiliar environments due to lack of deliberation, the authors introduce PACT, a hybrid architecture that pairs a fast reactive RL policy with a slow deliberative small language model planner. The SLM asynchronously generates and validates action plans via simulation, and once a plan is deemed safe, feasible, and complete, it is executed directly without modifying or retraining the RL policy. Evaluated on three increasingly difficult FrozenLake environments, PACT consistently outperforms all baselines using only a 2B-parameter SLM, demonstrating that combining deliberative planning with reactive execution yields superior performance compared to either approach alone.
为了解决强化学习策略在陌生环境中因缺乏明确推理而性能下降的问题，该文提出PACT架构，将快速反应的强化学习策略与缓慢但具推理能力的小语言模型规划器结合。SLM异步生成并验证行动方案，经模拟确认安全、可行且完整后，直接执行而不修改或重训RL策略。在三个难度递增的FrozenLake环境中，PACT在仅使用2B参数SLM的情况下超越所有基线，表明推理规划与反应执行相结合比单独使用任一方法更有效。
Discovering Subgroups with Exceptional Survival Characteristics
Authors: Mhd Jawad Al Rahwanji, Sascha Xu, Nils Philipp Walter, Jilles Vreeken
First: 2026-02-25T18:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:30:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In many applications, it is important to identify subpopulations that survive longer or shorter than the rest of the population. In medicine, for example, it allows determining which patients benefit from treatment, and in predictive maintenance, which components are more likely to fail. Existing methods for discovering subgroups with exceptional survival characteristics rely on restrictive assumptions about the survival model (e.g. proportional hazards), require pre-discretized features, and, as they compare average statistics, tend to overlook individual heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Sysurv, a non-parametric, fully differentiable method that discovers human-readable rules selecting subgroups with exceptional survival characteristics. Empirical evaluation on a wide range of datasets and settings, including a case study on cancer data, shows that Sysurv reveals insightful and actionable survival subgroups, outperforming the state of the art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：发现具有异常生存特征的子群
在许多应用中，识别比总体人群生存期更长或更短的子群体至关重要。例如在医学领域，这有助于确定哪些患者从治疗中获益；在预测性维护中，则可识别更易发生故障的部件。现有方法在发现具有异常生存特征的子群时，依赖于对生存模型的严格假设（如比例风险假设），需要预先离散化特征，且因比较平均统计量，往往忽略个体异质性。本文提出Sysurv，这是一种非参数、完全可微分的方法，能够发现人类可读的规则，以选择具有异常生存特征的子群。在多种数据集和场景下的实证评估，包括癌症数据的案例研究，表明Sysurv揭示了具有洞察力且可操作的生存子群，优于当前最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify patient or component subgroups with distinct survival outcomes in fields like medicine and maintenance, this paper introduces Sysurv, a non-parametric and fully differentiable method that discovers human-readable rules for selecting such subgroups without relying on restrictive survival model assumptions or pre-discretized features. Unlike existing approaches that compare average statistics and miss individual heterogeneity, Sysurv directly models survival characteristics through differentiable rule learning. Experimental results across diverse datasets, including a cancer case study, demonstrate that Sysurv consistently identifies meaningful and actionable survival subgroups, outperforming current state-of-the-art methods.
为在医学和预测性维护等领域识别具有不同生存结果的子群体，本文提出Sysurv方法，这是一种无需依赖生存模型强假设或预离散化特征的非参数、全可微方法，能直接发现人类可读的子群体选择规则。与现有依赖平均统计量、忽略个体异质性的方法不同，Sysurv通过可微编程优化子群体规则。在多个数据集（含癌症案例研究）上的实验表明，Sysurv能持续发现有意义且可操作的生存子群体，性能优于当前最先进方法。
DreamX-World 1.0: A General-Purpose Interactive World Model
Authors: DreamX Team, Yancheng Bai, Rui Chen, Xiangxiang Chu, Rujing Dang, Hao Dou, Bingjie Gao, Qiwen Gu, Siyu Hong, Jiachen Lei, Geng Li, Jifan Li, Ruimin Lin, Qingfeng Shi, Bingze Song, Lei Sun, Jing Tang, Ruitian Tian, Jun Wang, Jiahong Wu, Pengfei Zhang, Shen Zhang, Jiashu Zhu
First: 2026-06-15T17:30:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:30:33+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://amap-ml.github.io/DreamX_World, Code: https://github.com/AMAP-ML/DreamX-World
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
DreamX-World 1.0 is a general-purpose interactive text/image-to-video world model for controllable long-horizon generation. It supports camera navigation, revisits to previously observed regions, and promptable events across photorealistic, game-style, and stylized domains. Our data engine combines camera-accurate Unreal Engine rendering, action-rich gameplay recordings, and real-world videos with recovered camera geometry. For camera control, we introduce E-PRoPE, a lightweight variant of projective positional encoding that retains PRoPE's projective camera geometry while applying camera-aware attention to spatially reduced tokens. We convert a bidirectional video generator into a few-step autoregressive world model using causal forcing, DMD-style distillation, and long-rollout training. Training on self-generated long-horizon contexts exposes the model to its own generated history and reduces the style and color drift that accumulates across autoregressive chunks. Memory-Conditioned Scene Persistence retrieves earlier views through camera-geometry-based retrieval, while residual recycling makes the conditioning path less sensitive to imperfect memory latents. Event Instruction Tuning adds composable event control, and reinforcement learning alignment recovers camera control and visual quality after distillation. With mixed-precision DiT execution, residual reuse, 75\%-pruned VAE decoding, and asynchronous pipeline parallelism, DreamX-World 1.0 reaches up to 16\,FPS on eight RTX\,5090 GPUs. On our 5-second basic evaluation, DreamX-World 1.0 achieves a camera-control score of 73.75 and an overall score of 84.76, outperforming HY-WorldPlay 1.5 and LingBot-World in overall score, which achieve 80.79 and 80.45, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamX-World 1.0：通用交互式世界模型
DreamX-World 1.0 是一个通用的交互式文本/图像到视频的世界模型，支持可控的长时程生成。它支持相机导航、重访先前观察区域及跨写实、游戏风格和风格化领域的可提示事件。我们的数据引擎结合了相机精度的虚幻引擎渲染、动作丰富的游戏录像和恢复相机几何的真实世界视频。为实现相机控制，我们引入了轻量级的投影位置编码 E-PRoPE，在保留 PRoPE 投影相机几何的同时，对空间压缩后的 token 应用相机感知注意力。我们通过因果强制、DMD 风格蒸馏和长时训练，将双向视频生成器转换为几步自回归世界模型。在自生成长时程上下文上训练，使模型接触自身生成的历史，减少自回归块间累积的风格与色彩漂移。记忆条件化场景持久性通过相机几何检索早期视图，残差重用使条件路径对不完美的记忆潜在表示不敏感。事件指令调优增加可组合事件控制，强化学习对齐在蒸馏后恢复相机控制与视觉质量。通过混合精度 DiT 执行、残差重用、75% 剪枝 VAE 解码及异步流水线并行，DreamX-World 1.0 在八张 RTX 5090 GPU 上达到最高 16 FPS。在 5 秒基础评估中，DreamX-World 1.0 获得相机控制得分 73.75、综合得分 84.76，优于 HY-WorldPlay 1.5（80.79）和 LingBot-World（80.45）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for controllable, long-horizon video generation across diverse visual domains, DreamX-World 1.0 introduces a general-purpose interactive world model that supports camera navigation, revisit capabilities, and event control. It combines Unreal Engine rendering, gameplay recordings, and real-world videos with recovered camera geometry to build a rich training dataset. The model employs E-PRoPE for camera-aware attention, converts a bidirectional video generator into a causal autoregressive world model via distillation and long-rollout training, and uses memory-conditioned scene persistence and residual recycling to maintain visual consistency. Event instruction tuning and reinforcement learning alignment further enhance control and quality. Evaluated on a 5-second benchmark, it achieves a camera-control score of 73.75 and overall score of 84.76, surpassing HY-WorldPlay 1.5 (80.79) and LingBot-World (80.45), while running at 16 FPS on eight RTX 5090 GPUs.
为实现跨多视觉域的可控长时程视频生成，DreamX-World 1.0 提出一种支持相机导航、区域重访与事件指令控制的交互式世界模型。其训练数据融合了虚幻引擎渲染、游戏录像与恢复相机几何的真实视频。模型采用轻量化的 E-PRoPE 编码器实现相机感知注意力，通过因果强制、DMD蒸馏与长时训练将双向视频生成器转化为自回归世界模型，并利用自生成上下文缓解风格漂移。记忆引导场景持久化与残差重用机制提升场景连贯性，事件指令调优与强化学习对齐进一步增强控制精度与视觉质量。经混合精度 DiT、剪枝 VAE 与异步流水线并行优化后，模型在八张 RTX 5090 显卡上达 16 FPS，相机控制得分 73.75，综合得分 84.76，优于 HY-WorldPlay 1.5（80.79）与 LingBot-World（80.45）。
A Multi-Center Benchmark for Abdominal Disease Diagnosis and Report Generation from Non-Contrast CT
Authors: Mariam Elbakry, Aliaa Sayed Sheha, Salma Hassan Tantawy, Aya Yassin, Concetto Spampinato, Karim Lekadir, Xiaomeng Li, Marawan Elbatel
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-06-15T17:29:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:29:32+00:00
Comments: Early Accept (top ~9%), MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multiphasic contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) is widely used for abdominal lesion characterization, yet it carries inherent risks of contrast-induced nephropathy, escalates acquisition burden, and heavily contributes to radiologist workload. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel multi-center benchmark for multi-organ abdominal disease diagnosis and automated radiology report generation, which learns to synthesize contrast-enhanced findings from single-phase non-contrast CT (NCCT). To support this, we curated a large-scale dataset of paired NCCT-CECT studies and their corresponding contrast-enhanced radiology reports from two centers, partitioned into internal sets and an external validation cohort. Under a unified evaluation protocol, we benchmarked five contemporary deep learning architectures encompassing chest-specific, abdomen-specific, and general-purpose multimodal domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NCCT retains diagnostic signals, achieving an average multi-organ AUC of 69.1% on the internal cohort and 63.1% on the external cohort, respectively. By releasing this dataset and standardized benchmark publicly, this study aims to catalyze future research into safer, resource-efficient, and globally accessible contrast-free abdominal imaging workflows. Code is available at: https://github.com/xmed-lab/TriALS-Report.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于非增强CT的腹部疾病诊断与报告生成多中心基准
多期增强CT（CECT）广泛用于腹部病变特征化，但存在造影剂肾病风险、增加采集负担并加重放射科医生工作量。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新型多中心基准，用于多器官腹部疾病诊断与自动化放射报告生成，该基准可从单期非增强CT（NCCT）合成增强影像特征。为此，我们从两个中心收集了大规模配对的NCCT-CECT研究及其对应的增强放射报告，并划分为内部训练集与外部验证队列。在统一评估协议下，我们对五种当前主流深度学习架构（胸部专用、腹部专用及通用多模态模型）进行了基准测试。大量实验表明，NCCT仍保留诊断信号，在内部队列和外部队列上分别实现平均多器官AUC为69.1%和63.1%。通过公开发布该数据集与标准化基准，本研究旨在推动更安全、资源高效且全球可及的无造影剂腹部影像工作流程研究。代码见：https://github.com/xmed-lab/TriALS-Report。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risks and inefficiencies of contrast-enhanced CT in abdominal imaging, this study introduces a multi-center benchmark that trains deep learning models to generate contrast-enhanced diagnostic reports from single-phase non-contrast CT scans. The method leverages paired NCCT-CECT data and corresponding radiology reports from two institutions, split into internal and external validation sets, and evaluates five diverse deep learning architectures across chest, abdomen, and general multimodal domains. Experimental results show that NCCT alone can achieve an average multi-organ AUC of 69.1% on internal data and 63.1% on external data, demonstrating diagnostic feasibility without contrast agents.
为降低对比增强CT的肾毒性风险和减轻放射科医生负担，本研究构建了一个多中心基准数据集，旨在训练深度学习模型从单相非增强CT中生成对比增强的诊断报告。研究者从两个中心收集了大量配对的非增强与增强CT图像及其报告，分为内部和外部验证集，并评估了五种不同架构的深度学习模型。实验结果显示，非增强CT在内部验证集上平均多器官AUC达69.1%，外部验证集为63.1%，表明无需对比剂即可实现有效的腹部疾病诊断。
Analytic Torsion and Spectral Gap Capture Persistent-Laplacian Performance
Authors: Jernej Grlj, Aaron D. Lauda
First: 2026-06-15T17:28:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:28:57+00:00
Comments: 13 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While persistent Laplacians (PL) offer a richer geometric representation of data than persistent homology, utilizing their full eigenspectrum for learning tasks is often hampered by high dimensionality and the ``varying length'' problem across different filtration scales. We propose a compact spectral representation that distills the persistent Laplacian into three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, the spectral gap, and analytic torsion. Across benchmark datasets including MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT, we demonstrate that this reduced feature space captures the essential predictive signal of the full spectrum, and in some cases outperforms it, while significantly reducing computational overhead and preventing the noise introduced by higher-frequency eigenvalues. Our results suggest that these invariants provide a principled, fixed-length interface between spectral geometry and topological learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解析扭率与谱隙：持久拉普拉斯算子性能的捕捉
尽管持久拉普拉斯算子（PL）比持久同调提供更丰富的数据几何表示，但利用其完整特征谱进行学习任务常受高维性和不同过滤尺度下‘长度变化’问题的制约。我们提出一种紧凑的谱表示，将持久拉普拉斯算子提炼为三个具有数学基础的不变量：贝蒂数、谱隙和解析扭率。在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上，我们证明该降维特征空间能捕捉完整谱的预测信号，部分情况下表现更优，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频率特征值引入的噪声。结果表明，这些不变量为谱几何与拓扑学习之间提供了一种原理性、固定长度的接口。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high dimensionality and scalability issues of using full persistent Laplacian eigenspectra in machine learning, the authors propose a compact spectral representation based on three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, spectral gap, and analytic torsion. These invariants are shown to capture the essential predictive signal from the full spectrum while avoiding noise from higher-frequency eigenvalues and reducing computational cost. Experiments on MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT datasets demonstrate that this reduced representation not only matches but in some cases surpasses the performance of the full spectrum, offering a principled, fixed-length interface for topological learning.
为了解决持久拉普拉斯谱高维计算效率低和高频噪声问题，该研究提出用三个不变量——贝蒂数、谱隙和解析扭率——构建紧凑的谱表示，以捕捉关键拓扑与几何信号。在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上，该简化表示在性能上与完整谱相当甚至更优，同时大幅降低计算开销并抑制高频特征噪声，表明这些不变量为拓扑学习与谱几何之间提供了一种原理性、固定长度的接口。
Stable Menus of Public Goods: AI-Enabled Progress
Authors: Sara Fish
First: 2026-06-15T17:28:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:28:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the EC'26 Workshop on AI-Driven Research in EconCS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using an open problem from the EC 2025 paper "Stable Menus of Public Goods" as a testbed, we conduct experiments to understand the effectiveness of different AI-for-EconCS research workflows. Specifically, we study three questions: Does providing human intuition in the prompt help? Does automated multi-turn interaction help? And, does an LLM outperform a first-year PhD student? Regarding the first two questions, we provide evidence for the following workflow suggestions: (1) prompting with human intuition can encourage the LLM to have better "taste", (2) multi-turn workflows help when the pipeline encourages "ambitious" steps. Regarding the third question, using an unpublished manuscript written by the paper's senior authors prior to collaborating with the first-year PhD student, we compare the effectiveness of the LLM with that of the first-year PhD student, and find that the LLM is slightly less effective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：公共物品的稳定菜单：人工智能驱动的进展
以EC 2025论文《公共物品的稳定菜单》中的开放性问题为实验平台，我们开展实验以理解不同AI应用于经济计算科学（EconCS）研究工作流的有效性。具体研究三个问题：在提示中提供人类直觉是否有助？自动化多轮交互是否有助？以及，大语言模型（LLM）是否优于一年级博士生？针对前两个问题，我们提出以下工作流建议：(1) 以人类直觉引导提示可促使LLM产生更好的‘品味’；(2) 当工作流鼓励‘雄心勃勃’的步骤时，多轮交互有助于提升效果。针对第三个问题，我们对比了LLM与一年级博士生的表现，使用该论文资深作者在与博士生合作前撰写的未发表手稿作为基准，发现LLM略逊于博士生。

Motivated by the open problem in the EC 2025 paper "Stable Menus of Public Goods," this study evaluates AI-for-EconCS workflows through three experiments: the impact of human intuition in prompts, the value of multi-turn interactions, and the comparative performance of an LLM versus a first-year PhD student. In the final comparison, the LLM performed slightly less effectively than the PhD student when evaluated against an unpublished manuscript by senior authors.
基于EC 2025论文《公共物品的稳定菜单》中的开放问题，本研究通过三个实验问题评估AI在经济学研究中的工作流效果：提示中加入人类直觉是否有效、多轮交互是否有益、以及大语言模型是否优于一名一年级博士生。结果表明，加入人类直觉能提升模型输出质量，多轮交互在鼓励大胆步骤的流程中更有效。在直接对比中，大语言模型略逊于一年级博士生，评估依据为资深作者在合作前撰写的未发表手稿。
Agent trajectories as programs: fingerprinting and programming coding-agent behavior
Authors: Hamidah Oderinwale
First: 2026-06-15T17:28:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:28:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Benchmark scores tell you what an agent got right; they do not tell you how it got there. In this work, we introduce methods for comparing agents procedurally in different contexts, where the model, tasks, and approaches vary. We compare ten agents and find that they are identifiable by their behavioral habits, which we define as fingerprints: a probe over these procedural signatures attributes an unseen trajectory to the correct agent at 85.7% accuracy, controlling for leakage across tasks. We develop procedural representations for agent problem-solving procedures with an emergent vocabulary induction technique that is meant to be maximally compressive to avoid surface-level variation while being expressive enough to unveil the quirks of the models' patterns. We apply our framework to the software engineering evaluation dataset SWE-Bench to study the structural distinctness of agent trajectories and find that behavior is most similar between models from similar release periods and those that are distilled from one another (e.g., a distilled student model and its teacher have a Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.25, about half the distance between other model pairs). As more models saturate evaluations, we believe that it will be important to probe model behavior along more holistic dimensions than success rates alone. We introduce ProcGrep, a library for auditing and evaluating agents for how they approach tasks at a procedural level given their traces in a top-down fashion. We believe this work has a range of applications to help developers work with and program coding agents, such as task-aware model routing, agent monitoring, and finer-grained cost analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理轨迹作为程序：指纹识别与编程编码代理行为
基准分数告诉你代理答对了什么，但无法说明其如何达成。本研究提出在不同情境下比较代理行为的方法，其中模型、任务和方法各异。我们比较了十个代理，发现它们可通过行为习惯（即“指纹”）加以识别：对这些程序性签名进行探测，可将未知轨迹准确归因于正确代理，准确率达85.7%，且控制了任务间信息泄露。我们开发了代理问题解决程序的程序性表示方法，采用一种新兴词汇诱导技术，旨在最大程度压缩以避免表面变化，同时足够表达以揭示模型模式的细微差异。我们将框架应用于软件工程评估数据集SWE-Bench，研究代理轨迹的结构差异性，发现行为最相似的是发布周期相近的模型，以及彼此蒸馏的模型（如蒸馏学生模型与其教师模型的Jensen-Shannon散度为0.25，约为其他模型对之间距离的一半）。随着更多模型饱和评估，我们认为有必要超越仅凭成功率维度，从更全面角度探测模型行为。我们引入ProcGrep，一个用于自顶向下审计和评估代理如何在轨迹基础上处理任务的库。我们相信该工作有助于开发者与编程编码代理协作，如任务感知模型路由、代理监控及更精细的成本分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how agents solve problems beyond their benchmark scores, this work introduces procedural fingerprinting to compare agent behavior across diverse models, tasks, and methods. It defines behavioral fingerprints as procedural signatures derived from agent trajectories, enabling accurate identification of unseen agents at 85.7% accuracy while controlling for task leakage. The method employs an emergent vocabulary induction technique to compress and reveal underlying behavioral patterns without surface-level noise. Applied to SWE-Bench, it reveals that agents from similar release periods or distilled pairs exhibit the most similar behavior, with Jensen-Shannon divergence as low as 0.25. The study concludes that procedural analysis is essential as models saturate performance metrics, and introduces ProcGrep, a top-down auditing tool for task-aware routing, monitoring, and cost analysis of coding agents.
为理解代理解决问题的方式而不仅限于其基准得分，本研究提出了一种程序化比较方法，用于指纹识别和分析不同模型与任务下的代理行为。通过一种涌现词汇诱导技术压缩并揭示底层行为模式，作者在控制任务泄露的情况下，以85.7%的准确率匹配代理轨迹。在SWE-Bench数据集上的应用显示，来自相近发布周期或蒸馏对的代理行为最相似，其Jensen-Shannon散度低至0.25。研究主张应超越性能指标，进行程序化审计，并引入ProcGrep工具，用于自顶向下分析代理的任务处理方式。
Consensus-based Agentic Large Language Model Framework for Harmonized Tariff Schedule Code Classification
Authors: Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen, Khanh Van Quynh Nguyen, Hoang-Loc Cao, Tri Duong, Phuc Ho, Van Pham, Loc Nguyen, Hung Cao
First: 2026-06-15T17:24:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:24:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 3rd International Conference of Resilience by Technology and Design (RTD 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate Harmonized Tariff Schedule (HTS) code classification is essential for customs clearance, duty assessment, trade statistics, and regulatory compliance in maritime logistics. However, exact HTS classification remains challenging because product descriptions are often short, incomplete, or ambiguous, while correct classification depends on hierarchical tariff structures, legal notes, and jurisdiction-specific rules. This paper proposes an agentic large language model (LLM) framework for Canadian 10-digit HTS code classification in smart-port and maritime logistics environments. The framework integrates multi-agent information retrieval, semantic retrieval over official tariff documents, evidence-grounded reasoning, consensus-based validation, element-wise voting across hierarchical code components, confidence estimation, and human-in-the-loop escalation. We evaluate the framework on a private dataset of 3,300 domain-expert-labeled product records collected from logistics and delivery contexts. Experimental results show that exact 10-digit classification remains difficult even for advanced LLMs, with performance decreasing from coarse chapter-level prediction to fine-grained tariff and statistical suffix assignment. These findings demonstrate the need for evidence-grounded, uncertainty-aware, and human-centered classification workflows rather than fully autonomous single-step prediction. The proposed framework supports more interpretable, accountable, and compliance-oriented HTS classification for maritime logistics and smart-port operations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/hts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于共识的代理型大语言模型框架用于协调关税税则代码分类
准确的协调关税税则（HTS）代码分类对海关清关、关税评估、贸易统计及海运物流领域的监管合规至关重要。然而，精确的HTS分类仍具挑战性，因产品描述常简短、不完整或模糊，而正确分类依赖于层级关税结构、法律注释及司法管辖区特定规则。本文提出一种代理型大语言模型（LLM）框架，用于加拿大10位数HTS代码分类，适用于智能港口与海运物流环境。该框架整合多代理信息检索、官方关税文件语义检索、证据支撑推理、基于共识的验证、层级代码组件逐元素投票、置信度评估及人机协同升级机制。我们在包含3300条由领域专家标注的产品记录的私有数据集上评估该框架，实验结果表明，即使对先进LLM而言，精确10位数分类仍具挑战，性能随从粗粒度章节级预测到细粒度关税与统计后缀分配而下降。这些发现表明，需采用证据支撑、不确定性感知及以人为核心的分类工作流，而非完全自主的单步预测。所提框架支持更可解释、可问责、合规导向的HTS分类，适用于海运物流与智能港口运营。代码开源于https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/hts。
