为解决海事物流中商品归类于《协调制度》（HTS）时因产品描述模糊、依赖复杂法律结构而导致的准确分类难题，本文提出一种面向加拿大10位HTS编码的共识型代理大语言模型框架。该方法结合多代理信息检索、官方关税文件语义检索、证据驱动推理、层级共识投票、置信度评估与人工介入机制。在包含3300条领域专家标注的产品数据集上测试表明，即使先进大模型在粗粒度章节分类上表现尚可，但在细粒度关税与统计后缀分配上性能显著下降，凸显了在智能港口环境中采用可解释、不确定性感知且有人类参与的分类流程的必要性，而非完全自主的单步预测。
Dynestyx: A Probabilistic Programming Library for Dynamical Systems
Authors: Daniel Waxman, Dmitry Batenkov, John Feser, Andy Zane, Eli Bingham, Youssef Marzouk, Matthew E. Levine
First: 2026-06-15T17:23:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:23:30+00:00
Comments: 7 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State-space models (SSMs) are the standard formalism for Bayesian treatment of dynamical systems, with natural applications in statistics, signal processing, and machine learning. Despite their importance in both theory and application, dynamical systems have proven difficult to incorporate in modern probabilistic programming languages (PPLs), making state-of-the-art methods less accessible to practitioners and introducing friction in following the "Bayesian workflow." We introduce dynestyx, a probabilistic programming library with first-class support for SSMs, including state-of-the-art methods in the estimation of both states and parameters. Through a single, unified interface, users may specify arbitrary priors for discrete-time or continuous-time dynamical systems, perform inference over mixed-effect data, and make state and parameter estimates with principled uncertainty quantification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dynestyx：用于动力系统的概率编程库
状态空间模型（SSMs）是处理动力系统贝叶斯方法的标准形式化工具，广泛应用于统计学、信号处理和机器学习领域。尽管在理论和应用中都至关重要，动力系统在现代概率编程语言（PPLs）中的集成一直存在困难，导致前沿方法对实践者不够友好，并增加了遵循“贝叶斯工作流”的摩擦。我们引入了dynestyx，一个对SSMs提供一等支持的概率编程库，包含对状态和参数估计的前沿方法。通过统一的接口，用户可为离散时间或连续时间动力系统指定任意先验，对混合效应数据进行推断，并以具有原则性不确定度量化的方式估计状态和参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of incorporating dynamical systems into modern probabilistic programming languages, the authors introduce Dynestyx, a library that provides first-class support for state-space models within a unified interface. Users can specify arbitrary priors for discrete- or continuous-time systems, perform inference over mixed-effect data, and obtain state and parameter estimates with principled uncertainty quantification. Experimental results demonstrate that Dynestyx enables efficient and accurate Bayesian inference for dynamical systems, bridging the gap between theoretical methods and practical implementation.
为了解决动力系统在现代概率编程语言中难以集成的问题，作者提出了Dynestyx库，该库为状态空间模型提供了一流支持，并通过统一接口实现。用户可为离散或连续时间系统指定任意先验，对混合效应数据进行推断，并获得带合理不确定性量化的状态与参数估计。实验结果表明，Dynestyx能高效准确地进行动力系统的贝叶斯推断，弥合了理论方法与实际应用之间的鸿沟。
Beyond Text-to-SQL: An Agentic LLM System for Governed Enterprise Analytics APIs
Authors: Gundeep Singh, Parsa Kavehzadeh, Jing Xia, Xue-Yong Fu, Julien Bouvier Tremblay, Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar, Vincent Lum, Shashi Bhushan TN
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-05-20T11:00:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:22:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Enterprise AI Agents Workshop @ KDD 2026. The first four authors contributed equally to this work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enterprise analytics aims to make organizational data accessible for decision-making, yet non-technical users still face barriers when using traditional business intelligence tools or Text-to-SQL systems. While recent Text-to-SQL approaches based on Large Language Models (LLMs) promise natural language access to structured data, they fall short in enterprise settings where analytics pipelines rely on governed APIs rather than raw databases. In practice, these APIs encapsulate complex business logic to ensure consistency, auditability, and security. However, delegating mathematical or aggregation logic to an LLM introduces reliability and compliance risks. To this end, we present Analytic Agent, an LLM-based agentic system that translates natural language intents into secure interactions with enterprise analytics APIs. Evaluated on 90 real enterprise use cases constructed by domain experts, it reliably interprets user goals, validates permissions, executes governed queries, and generates compliant visualizations through multi-step reasoning and policy-aware orchestration.

企业分析旨在使组织数据可被用于决策，但非技术人员在使用传统商业智能工具或文本到SQL系统时仍面临障碍。尽管基于大语言模型（LLM）的最新文本到SQL方法承诺自然语言访问结构化数据，但在企业环境中，分析管道依赖于受治理的API而非原始数据库，这些API封装了复杂业务逻辑以确保一致性、可审计性和安全性。然而，将数学或聚合逻辑委托给LLM会引入可靠性与合规性风险。为此，我们提出了Analytic Agent，一种基于LLM的代理式系统，可将自然语言意图转化为与企业分析API的安全交互。在90个由领域专家构建的真实企业用例中评估，该系统能可靠地理解用户目标、验证权限、执行受治理查询，并通过多步推理与政策感知编排生成合规可视化结果。

Evaluated on 90 real-world enterprise use cases curated by domain experts, Analytic Agent consistently interpreted user intents and produced accurate, secure results without delegating critical logic to untrusted LLMs.

From 911 to Hospital: Challenges and Opportunities for AI Integration in Emergency Medical Services
Authors: Emily Hou, Marelyn Gonzalez, Andrew L. Kun, Osnat Mokryn, Orit Shaer
First: 2026-06-15T17:22:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:22:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the Proceedings of CHIWORK 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly introduced into healthcare settings, yet its integration into fast-paced, high-pressure domains such as Emergency Medical Services (EMS) remains limited. EMS work unfolds across distinct stages, each characterized by different information needs, constraints, and forms of collaboration. Designing effective AI support requires understanding how AI interventions align with, or disrupt, EMS work across its different stages. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 25 EMS clinicians across the United States to examine how existing technologies currently support emergency services workflows and how they envision opportunities for, and concerns about, future AI-based support across different stages of emergency response. Our analysis reveals the cognitive, social, and procedural factors that enable EMS team coordination, which is grounded in situational awareness across distributed roles. EMS clinicians expressed significant concerns about how AI integration threatens this coordination mechanism across multiple dimensions: legal and privacy issues, technical reliability, contextual sensitivity, professional autonomy, and workflow friction. We propose five design principles for AI systems that augment distributed cognition and situational awareness, enabling EMS teams to deliver effective care under extreme constraints.

标题：从911到医院：人工智能在紧急医疗服务中的整合挑战与机遇
人工智能（AI）正越来越多地引入医疗环境，但在节奏快、压力大的紧急医疗服务（EMS）领域，其整合仍有限。EMS工作贯穿多个阶段，每个阶段具有不同的信息需求、约束条件和协作形式。设计有效的AI支持系统，需理解AI干预如何与EMS各阶段的工作相契合或干扰。 我们对美国25名EMS临床医生进行了半结构化访谈，探讨现有技术如何支持紧急服务工作流程，以及他们对未来AI支持在不同应急响应阶段的机会与担忧。分析表明，EMS团队协作依赖于跨分布式角色的情境意识，涉及认知、社会和程序性因素。临床医生表达了对AI整合可能从多个维度威胁这一协调机制的担忧：法律与隐私问题、技术可靠性、情境敏感性、专业自主权及工作流程摩擦。我们提出五项设计原则，旨在增强分布式认知与情境意识，使EMS团队在极端约束下提供有效照护。

Decoupling Inference from State Updates in Low-Latency Feature Engines via Probabilistic Thinning
Authors: Augusto Peres, Iker Perez, Pedro Valdeira, Guilherme Jardim, Ana Sofia Gomes, Hugo Ferreira, Pedro Bizarro
First: 2026-06-15T17:18:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:18:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Streaming data systems increasingly underpin Machine Learning workflows that maintain large numbers of continuously updated aggregations. In production settings, each incoming event typically triggers read-modify-write operations to persistent storage, making high-frequency state updates a dominant source of latency, contention, and operational cost. In this work, we decouple inference from state persistence in streaming Machine Learning pipelines via probabilistic thinning: every event is scored, but durable state updates are selectively triggered by informative events. Unlike approaches that shed input or state, we show that persistence-path control is achievable without a high-frequency in-memory control plane or cross-worker coordination, relying exclusively on approximate statistics retrieved from disk-backed key-value stores. We model the resulting stochastic processes, derive bounds on filtering rates, and prove that common time-based aggregations remain unbiased under variance-aware formulations, preventing systemic error accumulation. We evaluate the approach in a controlled setting that isolates per-event costs, demonstrating substantial reductions in storage Input/Output and serialization overhead. Across experiments, up to 90% of events are excluded from the persistence path while preserving and in some cases improving downstream utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过概率稀疏化在低延迟特征引擎中解耦推理与状态更新
流式数据系统日益成为支持大量持续更新聚合的机器学习工作流的基础。在生产环境中，每个传入事件通常会触发对持久化存储的读-修改-写操作，使高频状态更新成为延迟、竞争和运营成本的主要来源。在本研究中，我们通过概率稀疏化，在流式机器学习管道中解耦推理与状态持久化：每个事件都会被评分，但持久化状态更新仅由信息性事件触发。与那些丢弃输入或状态的方法不同，我们证明了无需高频内存控制平面或跨工作进程协调，仅依靠从磁盘支持的键值存储中检索的近似统计量，即可实现持久化路径控制。我们对由此产生的随机过程进行建模，推导出过滤率的界限，并证明在考虑方差的公式下，常见的时间聚合仍保持无偏，从而防止系统性误差累积。我们在一个隔离每事件成本的受控环境中评估该方法，展示了存储输入/输出和序列化开销的显著降低。在实验中，高达90%的事件被排除在持久化路径之外，同时保持并有时改善下游效用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency and operational cost of frequent state updates in streaming ML systems, this work proposes probabilistic thinning to decouple inference from persistent state writes. Instead of updating state for every event, the system scores each event and only commits state changes for informative ones, using approximate statistics from disk-backed key-value stores without requiring in-memory control planes or cross-worker coordination. Theoretical analysis models the stochastic filtering process, derives bounds on thinning rates, and proves that common time-based aggregations remain unbiased under variance-aware formulations. Experiments show up to 90% of events can be excluded from persistence while maintaining or improving downstream utility, significantly reducing storage I/O and serialization overhead.
为降低流式机器学习系统中高频状态更新带来的延迟与开销，该研究提出概率性稀疏化方法，将推理与持久化状态更新解耦。每个事件被评分后，仅部分信息量大的事件触发持久化写入，依赖磁盘存储的近似统计量，无需内存控制平面或跨工作协调。实验表明，最多可排除90%的事件仍保持或提升下游效用，显著降低存储I/O与序列化开销，且常见时间聚合未引入系统性偏差。
Scalable Pairwise Kernel Learning with Stochastic Vec Trick
Authors: Napsu Karmitsa, Tapio Pahikkala, Antti Airola
First: 2026-06-15T17:15:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:15:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pairwise learning is a specialized form of supervised learning that focuses on predicting outcomes for pairs of objects. In this work, we introduce SPaiK, a new scalable kernel learning method tailored for pairwise settings. Our approach preserves the expressive power of kernel methods while substantially reducing computational and memory requirements. The key innovation is the stochastic generalized vec trick (sGVT), a stochastic extension of the sparse Kronecker product multiplication algorithm, which enables efficient large-scale training with pairwise kernels. By incorporating sGVT, SPaiK makes it possible to apply kernel-based pairwise learning to datasets of a size previously out of reach. We evaluate the performance of SPaiK on seven real-world drug-target affinity datasets and compare the results with state-of-the-art methods in pairwise learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于随机向量化技巧的可扩展成对核学习
成对学习是监督学习的一种特殊形式，专注于预测对象对的结果。本文提出SPaiK，一种专为成对设置设计的可扩展核学习方法。我们的方法在保持核方法表达能力的同时，显著降低了计算和内存需求。关键创新是随机广义向量化技巧（sGVT），它是稀疏克罗内克积乘法算法的随机扩展，使成对核的大规模训练变得高效。通过引入sGVT，SPaiK使得核基成对学习能够应用于此前无法处理的大规模数据集。我们在七个真实药物-靶点亲和力数据集上评估了SPaiK的性能，并与成对学习领域的最先进方法进行了比较。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of traditional kernel methods in pairwise learning, this work proposes SPaiK, a scalable kernel learning framework that reduces memory and computation costs while preserving kernel expressiveness. It achieves this through the stochastic generalized vec trick (sGVT), a stochastic variant of sparse Kronecker product multiplication, enabling efficient training on large-scale pairwise datasets. Experimental results on seven real-world drug-target affinity datasets show that SPaiK outperforms existing state-of-the-art pairwise learning methods in terms of both accuracy and scalability.
为解决传统核方法在成对学习中计算效率低的问题，本文提出SPaiK框架，专为成对对象预测设计。该方法采用随机广义向量化技巧（sGVT），在保持核方法表达能力的同时显著降低内存与计算开销。在七个真实药物-靶点亲和力数据集上的实验表明，SPaiK在性能上可与当前最先进方法相媲美甚至更优，使大规模成对学习问题得以高效实现。
Task-Error Residual Learning for Real-Robot Five-Ball Juggling
Authors: Kai Ploeger, Jan Peters
First: 2026-06-15T17:14:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:14:32+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the 2026 International Symposium on Robotics Research (ISRR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For residual learning that refines existing behavior, sample efficiency depends on two things: how much information each rollout returns, and how efficiently the learner uses that information. Reinforcement learning's standard scalar reward carries far less information than the directional task error that defines the task. Random exploration further discards whatever information each rollout returns. Through residual learning with directional task-error supervision and a task error model that drives sample selection, we achieve stable three-, four-, and five-ball juggling on anthropomorphic Barrett WAM arms. Despite planning and controlling through a simple, idealized stack, the system converges from the second attempt. The first attempt drops, after which task error decreases monotonically without further failures. In comparison, five-ball juggling typically takes humans years of practice. We compare residual learners across two ternary axes, the directional information in the learning feedback and the commitment of the analytic prior, spanning Newton-style Jacobian updates, Composite Bayesian Optimization, and stochastic search methods. Both axes prove necessary: neither directional feedback nor an informative prior suffices alone, and the simplest method that combines them, a fixed-Jacobian Newton update, is the most reliable. The learned residual tolerates substantial prior misalignment and degraded joint tracking, affecting mainly convergence speed. The bottleneck for residual learning on real robots is therefore the information content of the supervision signal and how the learner uses it, not the accuracy of the surrounding stack. Video documentation of all experiments is available at https://kai-ploeger.com/residual-juggling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向真实机器人五球杂耍的任务误差残差学习
对于通过残差学习优化现有行为的方法，样本效率取决于两点：每个 rollout 返回的信息量，以及学习者利用该信息的效率。强化学习的标准标量奖励所携带的信息远少于定义任务的方向性任务误差。随机探索进一步丢弃每个 rollout 返回的任何信息。通过结合方向性任务误差监督和驱动样本选择的任务误差模型的残差学习，我们在类人形Barrett WAM机械臂上实现了稳定三球、四球和五球杂耍。尽管通过一个简单理想化的堆栈进行规划与控制，系统从第二次尝试即收敛。第一次尝试失败后，任务误差单调下降且不再发生失败。相比之下，人类通常需要数年练习才能掌握五球杂耍。我们沿两个三元轴比较残差学习者：学习反馈中的方向性信息，以及分析先验的承诺程度，涵盖牛顿式雅可比更新、复合贝叶斯优化和随机搜索方法。两个轴均不可或缺：仅方向性反馈或仅信息性先验均不足以实现成功，而最简单的结合二者的方法——固定雅可比矩阵的牛顿更新——最为可靠。所学习的残差可容忍显著的先验偏差和关节跟踪退化，主要影响收敛速度。因此，真实机器人上残差学习的瓶颈在于监督信号的信息含量及学习者如何利用该信息，而非堆栈本身的精度。所有实验视频资料见 https://kai-ploeger.com/residual-juggling。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard reinforcement learning in real-robot juggling tasks, this work introduces task-error residual learning to improve sample efficiency by leveraging directional task-error supervision and a task error model that guides sample selection. The method refines existing behavior through iterative updates driven by directional error signals, enabling stable juggling of three, four, and five balls on Barrett WAM arms despite using a simple idealized stack. Experimental results show that the system converges from the second attempt, with task error decreasing monotonically after the first failure, contrasting with the years of human practice typically required. A comparative analysis across three learning methods reveals that both directional feedback and an informative prior are essential, with the simplest combination — a fixed-Jacobian Newton update — proving most reliable. The learned residual is robust to prior misalignment and joint tracking degradation, indicating that the primary bottleneck lies in supervision signal informativeness and learner utilization, not the accuracy of the underlying stack.
为提升真实机器人杂耍任务的样本效率，该研究提出基于任务误差残差学习的方法，利用方向性任务误差监督和任务误差模型选择性执行回合，以优化现有行为。该方法通过方向误差引导的残差更新，在Barrett WAM机械臂上实现了三球、四球和五球的稳定杂耍，即使采用简化控制栈也能从第二次尝试起收敛。实验表明，系统在首次失败后任务误差单调下降，远快于人类通常需要数年训练才能掌握五球杂耍。对比分析显示，方向性反馈与信息性先验均不可或缺，其中最简单组合——固定雅可比牛顿更新——最可靠。所学残差对先验偏差和关节跟踪误差具有鲁棒性，表明残差学习瓶颈在于监督信号的信息量及其利用方式，而非控制栈的精度。
Sobolev Approximation by Fixed-Size Neural Networks with Arbitrary Accuracy
Authors: Baicheng Li, Haizhao Yang, Shijun Zhang
First: 2026-06-15T17:12:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:12:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we investigate new activation functions for achieving arbitrary-accuracy Sobolev approximation by fixed-size neural networks. We first show that any function in $W^{2,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ can be approximated with arbitrary accuracy, measured in the $W^{1,\infty}$-norm, by a fixed-size neural network using the Elementary Universal Activation Function ($\mathrm{EUAF}$). To extend this result to $W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ for $s\in\mathbb{N}$, we introduce a smooth activation $\mathrm{DUAF}_{\infty}$ from the family of Differentiable Universal Activation Functions ($\mathrm{DUAF}_n$). We prove that any function in $W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ can be approximated with arbitrary accuracy in the $W^{s-1,\infty}$-norm by a fixed-size $\mathrm{DUAF}_{\infty}$-activated network. We further construct sigmoidal variants $\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$ and show that, for every $1\leq s\leq n$, fixed-size $\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$-activated networks still approximate any $f\in W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ with arbitrary accuracy in the $W^{s-1,\infty}$-norm. In all these results, the width and depth bounds are computed explicitly, and the proposed activations are elementary.
中文标题/摘要
标题：固定规模神经网络中任意精度Sobolev逼近的新激活函数
本文研究了实现固定规模神经网络任意精度Sobolev逼近的新激活函数。我们首先证明，任何属于$W^{2,\infty}((a,b)^d)$的函数，均可在$W^{1,\infty}$范数下由采用基础通用激活函数（EUAF）的固定规模神经网络任意逼近。为将此结果推广至$W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$（$s\in\mathbb{N}$），我们引入了可微通用激活函数（DUAF_n）家族中的光滑激活函数DUAF_∞。我们证明，任何属于$W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$的函数，均可在$W^{s-1,\infty}$范数下由DUAF_∞激活的固定规模网络任意逼近。我们进一步构造了sigmoid型变体$\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$，并证明对任意$1\leq s\leq n$，固定规模$\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$激活网络仍可在$W^{s-1,\infty}$范数下任意逼近任意$f\in W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$。所有结果中，宽度与深度的上界均明确计算，且所提激活函数均为初等函数。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of achieving arbitrary-accuracy Sobolev approximation using neural networks of fixed size. The authors introduce new activation functions—specifically the Elementary Universal Activation Function (EUAF) and its smooth variant DUAF∞—to enable such approximation. They prove that any function in W^{2,∞}((a,b)^d) can be approximated with arbitrary precision in the W^{1,∞}-norm using a fixed-size network with EUAF. This result is extended to higher-order Sobolev spaces W^{s,∞}((a,b)^d) for natural numbers s, using DUAF∞, which allows approximation in the W^{s-1,∞}-norm. Sigmoidal variants of DUAFn are also constructed, maintaining the same approximation capability for orders up to s ≤ n. All results come with explicit bounds on network width and depth, and the proposed activations are elementary.
本文旨在解决使用固定规模神经网络实现任意精度Sobolev逼近的问题。作者提出了新的激活函数，包括基础通用激活函数（EUAF）和可微通用激活函数（DUAF_n）及其平滑变体，以支持该目标。他们证明，任何属于Sobolev空间W^{2,∞}((a,b)^d)的函数，均可在W^{1,∞}范数下用固定规模的EUAF激活网络实现任意精度逼近。该结果进一步推广至任意自然数s对应的W^{s,∞}空间，使用DUAF_∞激活函数，可在W^{s−1,∞}范数下实现任意精度逼近。同时，作者构造了sigmoid型DUAF_n变体，对1≤s≤n仍保持相同逼近能力。所有结果均给出明确的网络宽度与深度上界，且所提激活函数均为基础函数。
The embrace of open science: An analysis of a decade of AI research and 56 800 conference papers
Authors: Kevin L Coakley, Thijs Snelleman, Holger Hoos, Odd Erik Gundersen
First: 2026-06-15T17:11:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:11:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The reproducibility crisis has directed the AI research community toward improving documentation practices. Several studies have identified methodological issues, and in response, the most impactful venues in the field have introduced reproducibility checklists. We seek to understand whether documentation practices have changed over time by assessing all published papers at five leading AI conferences over the past decade. Seven reproducibility variables were identified, quality-assured and used to analyse 56 800 publications. Our analysis reveals that in the period 2014 to 2024, documentation practices have improved; papers sharing both code and data increased nearly sixfold, from 11% to 64% Building on empirical reproducibility rates from a prior study, we estimate - inferred from documentation practices, not direct testing - that reproducibility increased from 28% in 2014 to 64% in 2024. Improvements in documentation practices predate the introduction of reproducibility checklists, suggesting these changes reflect a broader movement toward open science rather than a direct response to formal requirements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拥抱开放科学：对十年AI研究及56800篇会议论文的分析
可重复性危机促使AI研究社区转向改进文档实践。多项研究已识别出方法论问题，为此，该领域最具影响力的会议引入了可重复性检查清单。我们通过评估过去十年五大顶尖AI会议发表的所有论文，旨在了解文档实践是否随时间改善。我们识别并质量验证了七个可重复性变量，用于分析56800篇出版物。分析显示，2014至2024年间，文档实践显著改善：同时分享代码与数据的论文比例从11%增至64%，近六倍增长。基于先前研究的实证可重复率，我们推断——依据文档实践而非直接测试——可重复性从2014年的28%提升至2024年的64%。文档实践的改善早于可重复性检查清单的引入，表明这些变化反映了更广泛的开放科学运动，而非对正式要求的直接响应。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the reproducibility crisis in AI, this study examines whether documentation practices have evolved over the past decade by analyzing 56,800 papers from five top AI conferences. Using seven quality-assured reproducibility variables, it finds that the proportion of papers sharing both code and data rose from 11% to 64% between 2014 and 2024. Based on these documentation trends, the study estimates that reproducibility rates increased from 28% to 64% over the same period, indicating that improvements in openness preceded the adoption of formal reproducibility checklists and likely reflect a broader cultural shift toward open science.
为应对人工智能研究中的可重复性危机，本研究通过分析过去十年五大顶级AI会议的56800篇论文，考察文档实践是否随时间改善。研究识别并验证了七个可重复性相关变量，结果显示2014至2024年间，论文共享代码和数据的比例从11%上升至64%，推断的可重复性比例也从28%提升至64%。这些改进早于可重复性清单的广泛推行，表明其根源是开放科学文化的广泛传播，而非单纯响应正式要求。
How Much Do Reviews Really Contribute? A Study on Text-Enriched Matrix Factorization for Recommendations
Authors: Eduardo Ferreira da Silva, Mayki dos Santos Oliveira, Joel Machado Pires Denis Dantas Boaventura, Frederico Araújo Durão
First: 2026-06-15T17:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:09:48+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures, SBBD 2026 ISSN 2763-8979
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Incorporating textual reviews into a Recommender System has become a prominent strategy for enriching collaborative signals with semantic information. However, the actual contribution of review-derived representations remains an open question, particularly when strong collaborative baselines are employed. In this work, we systematically investigate the impact of textual information on Matrix Factorization by introducing and comparing three enrichment strategies over a common collaborative backbone. First, we propose a learnable gating mechanism that adaptively balances collaborative and textual signals during training. This mechanism is applied to two distinct review representations: (i) aggregated topic profiles extracted from user and item histories, and (ii) full text embedding representations derived from reviews. Additionally, we explore a cross-attention mechanism that identifies and emphasizes the most informative dimensions of the textual representation before fusion with collaborative factors. We evaluate six variants: pure, enriched with topic profiles and text via gating; enriched with topics and text via gating; and enhanced with cross-attention over textual features. Experiments across multiple review-based datasets reveal that although adaptive fusion mechanisms improve representation flexibility, the marginal contribution of textual signals remains limited compared to the collaborative backbone. These findings suggest that, under typical rating-prediction settings, collaborative information continues to dominate performance, raising important considerations for the effective integration of semantic review signals into recommendation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评论究竟贡献了多少？一项关于文本增强矩阵分解推荐方法的研究
将文本评论融入推荐系统已成为通过语义信息丰富协同信号的主流策略。然而，评论衍生表示的实际贡献仍是一个开放问题，尤其是在采用强协同基线时。本文系统研究了文本信息对矩阵分解的影响，通过在共同的协同基底上引入并比较三种增强策略。首先，我们提出一种可学习的门控机制，在训练过程中自适应平衡协同与文本信号。该机制应用于两种不同的评论表示：(i) 从用户和物品历史中提取的聚合主题画像；(ii) 从评论中推导出的完整文本嵌入表示。此外，我们探索了一种交叉注意力机制，用于在与协同因子融合前识别并强调文本表示中最信息量的维度。我们评估了六种变体：纯协同模型、通过门控机制增强主题画像和文本、通过门控机制增强主题和文本、以及通过文本特征交叉注意力增强的模型。在多个基于评论的数据集上的实验表明，尽管自适应融合机制提升了表示灵活性，但文本信号相比协同基底的边际贡献仍有限。这些发现表明，在典型的评分预测设置下，协同信息仍主导性能，这对语义评论信号有效整合到推荐模型提出了重要考量。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how much textual reviews contribute to recommendation performance when integrated into matrix factorization models, motivated by the need to understand the semantic value of reviews against strong collaborative baselines. The authors propose and compare three enrichment strategies: a learnable gating mechanism that adaptively balances collaborative and textual signals, applied to both aggregated topic profiles and full text embeddings; and a cross-attention mechanism that highlights informative dimensions of text before fusion. Across six model variants evaluated on multiple review-based datasets, results show that while adaptive fusion improves flexibility, the marginal gain from textual signals remains small compared to the collaborative backbone, indicating that collaborative signals still dominate in rating prediction tasks.
该研究旨在量化文本评论在矩阵分解推荐系统中的实际贡献。针对现有方法常忽略评论语义信息价值的问题，作者提出并对比三种文本增强策略：一种可学习门控机制，用于在训练中自适应平衡协同信号与文本信号，分别应用于用户/物品历史的聚合主题画像或完整文本嵌入；以及一种跨注意力机制，用于在融合前突出文本特征中的关键维度。实验表明，尽管自适应融合提升了模型灵活性，但文本信号对推荐性能的边际增益有限，协同信号仍主导表现，提示在典型评分预测任务中需谨慎整合语义评论信息。
When Should a Robot Replan? Regret-Guided Update Scheduling in Time-Varying MDPs
Authors: Negin Musavi, Gokul Puthumanaillam, Ruben Hernandez, William Schafer, Melkior Ornik
First: 2026-06-15T17:09:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:09:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robots operating in non-stationary environments must continually adapt their policies as the dynamics drift, but onboard energy and compute budgets cap how often a full state estimation and re-planning step can be performed. This raises a question: \emph{when}, along a horizon, should a robot spend its limited budget? We formulate this problem in time-varying Markov decision processes (TVMDPs) with a known bound on the rate of transition drift. We model execution as a \emph{skip-update} scheme in which, at chosen update times, the agent estimates the transition kernel by maximum likelihood and computes a finite-horizon policy, and between updates reuses this policy under a propagated state estimate. We analyze the dynamic regret of this scheme and show how it grows during skip intervals in terms of the properties of the TVMDP and the skip lengths; the resulting bound answers the opening question via an online, regret-guided update rule that allocates the budget adaptively. We evaluate the rule in a simulated Mars-rover navigation task with time-varying slip dynamics and on a Crazyflie quadrotor in indoor obstacle fields. Adaptive allocation outperforms other budgeted baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器人何时应重新规划？时间变化MDP中的遗憾引导更新调度
在非平稳环境中运行的机器人必须持续适应动态变化，但其机载能源和计算预算限制了完整状态估计和重新规划步骤的执行频率。这引发一个问题：机器人在时间跨度内应何时花费其有限预算？我们在此问题中将时间变化马尔可夫决策过程（TVMDP）建模，其中已知转移动态变化的速率上限。我们将执行建模为“跳过更新”方案：在选定的更新时刻，代理通过最大似然估计转移核并计算有限时域策略；在更新之间，基于传播的状态估计重用该策略。我们分析了该方案的动态遗憾，并展示了其在跳过间隔期间的增长与TVMDP属性及跳过长度的关系；所得界限通过在线、遗憾引导的更新规则回答了初始问题，该规则能自适应分配预算。我们在模拟的火星车导航任务（具有时变打滑动态）和室内障碍物场中的Crazyflie四旋翼飞行器上评估了该规则，结果表明自适应分配优于其他预算基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to adapt policies in non-stationary environments while respecting limited onboard resources, this work formulates the update scheduling problem in time-varying Markov decision processes with bounded drift. It proposes a skip-update method where the robot periodically re-estimates the transition model and recomputes a finite-horizon policy, then reuses it between updates with propagated state estimates. Theoretical analysis derives a regret bound that depends on TVMDP properties and skip intervals, enabling an adaptive, regret-guided update rule. Experiments in simulated Mars rover navigation and real Crazyflie quadrotor flights show that this adaptive allocation outperforms fixed-budget baselines.
为解决机器人在非平稳环境中需持续适应策略但受限于计算与能源预算的问题，本文提出一种基于后悔引导的更新调度方法，应用于时变马尔可夫决策过程。该方法采用跳过更新机制：机器人在选定时间点通过最大似然估计动态转移核并重新计算有限时域策略，其间复用该策略并传播状态估计。理论分析推导出后悔上界，其依赖于TVMDP特性与更新间隔，从而支持在线自适应预算分配。在模拟火星车导航与室内四旋翼飞行实验中，该自适应策略优于静态预算分配基线方法。
Not What, But How: A Framework for Auditing LLM Responses across Positioning, Generalization, Anthropomorphism, and Maxims
Authors: Siddhesh Milind Pawar, Sarah Masud, Haneul Yoo, Alice Oh, Isabelle Augenstein
First: 2026-06-01T17:01:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:07:38+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 19 Figures, 4 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used to answer subjective, information-seeking questions, where users are sensitive to how responses are communicated, not just whether the answers are correct. Existing LLM evaluations for subjective cultural queries largely focus on factual correctness, ignoring how the response is framed. To this end, we introduce FRANZ, an automated FRAmework for respoNse characteriZation to conduct communicative audit of LLM responses along four dimensions: cultural positioning, use of generalizing language, anthropomorphic cues, and adherence to conversational maxims. To enable this evaluation, we contribute SQUARE - a corpus of 376k subjective questions sourced from 57 subreddits, and mapped to 7 countries and 19 question categories. We demonstrate FRANZ's applicability by scoring responses from three open-weight LLMs. We observe that LLMs show statistically significant differences in the frequency with which they employ each response characteristic. Unlike single-dimensional audits, FRANZ reveals that insider positioning and anthropomorphism are positively coupled, with the degree of coupling varying by country, providing a diagnostic lens for identifying framing divergences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不是内容而是方式：一种针对LLM响应在定位、泛化、拟人化和会话准则维度上的审计框架
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于回答主观性、信息寻求类问题，用户对响应的表达方式敏感，而不仅限于答案是否正确。现有针对主观文化查询的LLM评估主要关注事实准确性，忽视了响应的表述框架。为此，我们提出了FRANZ——一种用于响应特征自动化的框架，以在四个维度上对LLM响应进行沟通审计：文化定位、泛化语言使用、拟人化线索及会话准则遵循情况。为支持该评估，我们贡献了SQUARE数据集——包含57个Reddit子版块的37.6万个主观问题，并映射至7个国家和19个问题类别。我们通过为三个开源权重LLM评分，展示了FRANZ的适用性。我们观察到，LLM在每种响应特征的使用频率上存在统计显著差异。与单维度审计不同，FRANZ揭示了“内部人定位”与“拟人化”呈正相关，且相关程度因国家而异，为识别表述差异提供了诊断视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how LLMs frame subjective responses beyond factual correctness, the authors introduce FRANZ, an automated framework that audits LLM outputs across four communicative dimensions: cultural positioning, generalization, anthropomorphism, and adherence to conversational maxims. To support this, they construct SQUARE, a 376k-question corpus drawn from 57 subreddits and mapped to 7 countries and 19 categories. Experimental results show statistically significant differences in how three open-weight LLMs deploy each characteristic, revealing that insider positioning and anthropomorphism are positively correlated, with this coupling varying by country, offering a diagnostic tool for identifying framing biases.
为评估大语言模型在回答主观问题时的表达方式而非仅事实准确性，作者提出FRANZ框架，从文化定位、泛化语言、拟人化和会话准则四个维度自动分析模型响应。为此构建了SQUARE语料库，包含37.6万条主观问题，源自57个Reddit子版块，并按7个国家和19类问题分类。实验表明，三款开源大模型在各特征使用频率上存在统计显著差异，且内部定位与拟人化呈正相关，且该关联程度因国家而异，为识别模型表达偏差提供了诊断视角。
Probing Low Frame Rate Degradation in Neural Audio Codecs
Authors: Alex Gichamba, Moise Busogi
First: 2026-06-15T17:06:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:06:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low frame rates in neural audio codecs are attractive for autoregressive speech synthesis, where the generation cost scales linearly with the sequence length. Recent work has demonstrated that codecs can operate at 12.5 Hz and below, but the mechanisms underlying low frame rate degradation remain insufficiently understood. We investigate these mechanisms through a controlled frame rate ablation. We reproduce a quality cliff at 6.25 Hz reported in previous works and evaluate candidate explanations: phonemic collisions and codebook saturation, neither of which shows evidence of a fundamental barrier. The cliff is instead caused by suboptimal training configuration: fixed clip duration during training yields too few tokens at low frame rates, starving the decoder of inter-token context. Once corrected, WER degrades smoothly with phonemic load down to 3.1 Hz and 1.6 Hz, suggesting the inference-time efficiency gains of low frame rate codecs are more accessible than previously assumed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探究神经音频编解码器中低帧率退化机制
神经音频编解码器中的低帧率对自回归语音合成具有吸引力，因为生成成本与序列长度呈线性关系。近期研究表明，编解码器可运行在12.5 Hz及以下，但低帧率退化的机制尚未充分理解。我们通过受控帧率消融实验研究这些机制，复现了先前研究中在6.25 Hz处报告的质量断崖，并评估了候选解释：音素碰撞与码本饱和，均未显示为根本性障碍。断崖实为训练配置不当所致：训练时固定剪辑时长导致低帧率下生成的token过少，使解码器缺乏token间上下文。修正后，WER随音素负载平滑下降至3.1 Hz和1.6 Hz，表明低帧率编解码器在推理时的效率优势比预想中更易实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential efficiency gains of low frame rate neural audio codecs in autoregressive speech synthesis, this study investigates the causes of performance degradation at sub-12.5 Hz rates. Through controlled frame rate ablation, the authors reproduce a previously reported quality cliff at 6.25 Hz and test two hypotheses—phonemic collisions and codebook saturation—both of which fail to explain the degradation. Instead, they identify that fixed clip duration during training leads to insufficient token generation at low frame rates, starving the decoder of inter-token context. Correcting this training configuration enables smooth performance decay down to 3.1 Hz and 1.6 Hz, indicating that low frame rate inference efficiency may be more achievable than previously believed.
为探索低帧率神经音频编解码器在自回归语音合成中的效率优势，本研究通过控制帧率消融实验，分析其性能退化机制。作者重现了先前报道的6.25 Hz处的质量断崖，并检验了“音素碰撞”与“码本饱和”两个假设，均未发现根本性障碍。研究发现，退化根源在于训练配置不当：固定训练剪辑时长导致低帧率下生成的token数量不足，使解码器缺乏跨token上下文。修正该问题后，WER可平滑下降至3.1 Hz和1.6 Hz，表明低帧率推理效率的提升潜力远超以往认知。
PhyloSDF: Phylogenetically-Conditioned Neural Generation of 3D Skull Morphology via Residual Flow Matching
Authors: Kaikwan Lau, Gary P. T. Choi
First: 2026-04-28T08:38:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:06:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating novel, biologically plausible three-dimensional morphological structures is a fundamental challenge in computational evolutionary biology, hampered by extreme data scarcity and the requirement that generated shapes respect phylogenetic relationships among species. In this work, we present PhyloSDF, a phylogenetically-conditioned neural generative model for 3D biological morphology that integrates two innovations: (1) a DeepSDF auto-decoder regularized by a novel Phylogenetic Consistency Loss that structures the latent space to correlate with evolutionary distances (Pearson r=0.993); (2) a Residual Conditional Flow Matching (Residual CFM) architecture that factorizes generation into analytic species-centroid lookup and learned residual prediction, enabling generation from as few as ~4 specimens per species. We evaluate PhyloSDF on 100 micro-CT-scanned skulls of Darwin's Finches and their relatives across 24 species. The model generates novel meshes achieving 88-129% of real intra-species variation at the code level, with all 180 generated meshes verified as non-memorized. Residual CFM surpasses denoising diffusion (which fails entirely at this scale), standard flow matching (which mode-collapses to 3-6% variation), and a Gaussian mixture baseline in both fidelity (Chamfer Distance 0.00181 vs. 0.00190) and morphometric Fréchet distance (10,641 vs. 13,322). Leave-one-species-out experiments across 18 species demonstrate phylogenetic extrapolation capability, and smooth latent interpolations produce biologically plausible ancestral skull reconstructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhyloSDF：通过残差流匹配实现基于系统发育条件的三维头骨形态神经生成
生成新颖且符合生物学的三维形态结构是计算进化生物学的基本挑战，受限于极端的数据稀缺性以及生成形状需符合物种间系统发育关系的要求。本文提出PhyloSDF，一种基于系统发育条件的三维生物形态神经生成模型，包含两项创新：（1）结合DeepSDF自动解码器与新型系统发育一致性损失的模型，使潜在空间结构与进化距离相关（皮尔逊相关系数r=0.993）；（2）残差条件流匹配（Residual CFM）架构，将生成分解为分析性物种质心查询与学习残差预测，仅需每物种约4个样本即可生成。我们在100个达尔文雀及其近缘种（24种）的微CT扫描头骨上评估PhyloSDF，生成的网格在代码层面实现88-129%的真实种内变异，180个生成网格均经验证非记忆化。Residual CFM在保真度（Chamfer距离0.00181 vs. 0.00190）和形态学Fréchet距离（10,641 vs. 13,322）上优于去噪扩散模型（在此尺度下完全失败）、标准流匹配（模式坍缩至3-6%变异）及高斯混合基线。跨18种的留一物种实验验证了系统发育外推能力，平滑潜在空间插值可生成符合生物学的祖先头骨重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate biologically plausible 3D skull morphologies while respecting evolutionary relationships, the authors introduce PhyloSDF, a neural model combining a DeepSDF auto-decoder with a Phylogenetic Consistency Loss to align latent space with evolutionary distance, and a Residual Conditional Flow Matching architecture that separates species-specific centroid lookup from learned residual prediction. Evaluated on 100 micro-CT skulls across 24 Darwin’s Finch species, the model generates novel meshes capturing 88–129% of real intra-species variation without memorization, outperforming denoising diffusion, standard flow matching, and Gaussian mixture baselines in both Chamfer Distance and morphometric Fréchet distance, and demonstrates phylogenetic extrapolation and ancestral reconstruction via latent interpolation.
为解决在尊重物种演化关系前提下生成生物可解释3D头骨形态的难题，作者提出PhyloSDF模型，结合DeepSDF自解码器与新型系统发育一致性损失以使潜在空间与演化距离对齐，并采用残差条件流匹配架构实现仅需少量样本即能生成形态。在24种达尔文雀及其近亲的100个微CT头骨数据上测试，该模型生成的180个新形态覆盖真实种内变异的88–129%，无记忆化现象，优于扩散模型、标准流匹配及高斯混合基线模型，在Chamfer距离与形态学Fréchet距离上均表现更优，并能在留一物种实验中实现演化外推与祖先形态重建。
Airport Terminal Passenger Queue Forecasting for Departure Gates and Security Checkpoints
Authors: Juhwan Lee, Seokbin Yoon, Keumjin Lee, Hojong Baik, Seyeon Jung
First: 2026-05-30T13:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:04:42+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 6 figures, accepted at DASC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate passenger queue forecasting in airport terminals is essential for efficient departure operations, as it enables proactive congestion management. However, time-varying passenger demand and heterogeneous facility usage across multiple departure facilities make forecasting challenging. In this work, we propose a passenger queue forecasting framework that learns historical passenger flow patterns from operational data. The proposed model employs a Transformer-based architecture to capture temporal dependencies and inter-facility correlations using past queue length and waiting time at departure gates and security checkpoints, together with passenger throughput at check-in islands. The learned representations are mapped to two facility-specific prediction heads to predict queue length and waiting time at departure gates and security checkpoints. Experimental results demonstrate accurate forecasts up to two hours ahead. The proposed approach offers practical real-time decision support for proactive queue management and staff reallocation in airport terminal operations.

标题：机场航站楼出发口及安检点旅客排队预测

What LLMs Must Forget to Teach Effectively: A DIY Approach to Premodern Japanese Language Pedagogy
Authors: Ariel Stilerman, Andrew Nelson, Alan Cheng, Caleb Langley, Sera Wang, Camilla Piana, Pelin Çılgın, Qianhe Qin, Teisha Nishimitsu, Liaoliao Zhang, Huiting Liu, Josh Eyre, Gavin Sherry
First: 2026-05-31T19:16:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:04:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We discuss a novel approach to Premodern Japanese Language Pedagogy (PJLP) with potential applications in other languages and fields. The integration of artificial intelligence into education has largely operated as a top-down project, affording minimal agency to everyday users. This dynamic mirrors the broader frontier model ecosystem, which concentrates massive human and financial resources within a few labs. Drawing inspiration from grassroots initiatives such as the DIY and Maker movements, this paper advocates for an approach to AI in Education that fosters instructional and student agency over the pedagogical process. Specifically, we discuss a tutoring framework for textual analysis in the context of a graduate seminar in premodern Japanese literature, as well as a bilingual interactive dictionary and a conversational partner created for a language course in Classical Japanese. Created through prompt engineering as custom instances of a Large Language Model (LLM), these three tools are designed to counteract the tendency of out-of-the-box LLMs to either bypass student effort through over-explanation or misguide learners via hallucinations. To illustrate how this approach can promote active comprehension and pedagogical alignment, we provide transcripts (logs) of actual exchanges, sample instructions (system prompts), and guidance for instructors curious about exploring this approach in a variety of fields (starter kit).
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型要遗忘什么才能有效教学：一种面向中古日语教学的DIY方法
本文探讨了一种新颖的中古日语教学法（PJLP），该方法在其他语言和领域亦有潜在应用。人工智能融入教育主要以自上而下方式推进，赋予普通用户极小自主权。这一动态与更广泛的前沿模型生态系统相似，后者将大量人力和资金集中于少数实验室。受DIY和创客运动等草根倡议启发，本文倡导一种促进教学与学生自主权的AI教育方法。具体而言，我们讨论了研究生中古日语文学研讨课中的文本分析辅导框架，以及为古典日语课程开发的双语互动词典和对话伙伴。这些工具通过提示工程定制为大语言模型（LLM）实例，旨在对抗现成LLM易出现的过度解释（绕过学生努力）或幻觉误导（错误引导学习者）问题。为展示该方法如何促进主动理解与教学协同，我们提供实际对话记录、示例指令（系统提示）及教师探索该方法的入门指南（工具包）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to empower students and instructors in premodern Japanese language education through more agency-driven AI tools, this paper proposes a DIY approach using Large Language Models (LLMs) to create custom pedagogical aids. Three tools—a tutoring framework for textual analysis, a bilingual dictionary, and a conversational partner—are built via prompt engineering to mitigate common LLM pitfalls like over-explanation or hallucinations. Experimental use in graduate seminars and language courses shows these tools support active student engagement and align with pedagogical goals, as evidenced by interaction logs and instructor guidance materials.
为提升日语古典语言教学中的师生自主性，本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的DIY教学框架。针对通用LLM易过度解释或产生幻觉的问题，作者通过提示工程定制了三个教学工具：辅导框架、双语词典和对话伙伴，专用于研究生阶段的文本分析与语言学习。实验日志与系统提示显示，这些定制化LLM工具显著减少了教学干扰，有效促进学生主动理解与教学目标一致。
Finite-Width Neural Tangent Kernels from Feynman Diagrams
Authors: Max Guillen, Philipp Misof, Jan E. Gerken
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-08-15T15:02:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:03:34+00:00
Comments: Published at the ICML 2026; 12 pages + appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural tangent kernels (NTKs) are a powerful tool for analyzing deep, non-linear neural networks. In the infinite-width limit, NTKs can easily be computed for most common architectures, yielding full analytic control over the training dynamics. However, at infinite width, important properties of training such as NTK evolution or feature learning are absent. Nevertheless, finite width effects can be included by computing corrections to the Gaussian statistics at infinite width. We introduce Feynman diagrams for computing finite-width corrections to NTK statistics. These dramatically simplify the necessary algebraic manipulations and enable the computation of layer-wise recursion relations for arbitrary statistics involving preactivations, NTKs and certain higher-derivative tensors (dNTK and ddNTK) required to predict the training dynamics at leading order. We demonstrate the feasibility of our framework by extending stability results for deep networks from preactivations to NTKs and proving the absence of finite-width corrections for scale-invariant nonlinearities such as ReLU on the diagonal of the Gram matrix of the NTK. We numerically implement the complete set of equations necessary to compute the first-order corrections for arbitrary inputs and demonstrate that the results follow the statistics of sampled neural networks for widths $n\gtrsim 20$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：来自费曼图的有限宽度神经切线核
神经切线核（NTK）是分析深度非线性神经网络的强大工具。在无限宽度极限下，NTK可轻松计算，为训练动态提供完全解析控制。然而，在无限宽度下，训练的重要特性（如NTK演化或特征学习）缺失。但可通过计算无限宽度高斯统计的修正来包含有限宽度效应。我们引入费曼图以计算NTK统计的有限宽度修正，这极大简化了必要的代数运算，并能计算任意统计量（涉及预激活、NTK及某些高阶导数张量dNTK和ddNTK）的逐层递推关系，从而预测训练动态的一阶近似。我们通过将深度网络的稳定性结果从预激活扩展至NTK，并证明ReLU等尺度不变非线性激活函数在NTK Gram矩阵对角线上无有限宽度修正，展示了该框架的可行性。我们数值实现了计算任意输入一阶修正所需的全部方程，并验证当宽度n≥20时，结果符合采样神经网络的统计特性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of infinite-width NTK analysis in capturing realistic training dynamics, this work introduces Feynman diagram techniques to compute finite-width corrections to NTK statistics. The method leverages diagrammatic algebra to derive layer-wise recursion relations for preactivations, NTKs, and higher-derivative tensors, enabling prediction of training behavior at leading order. Experiments show the framework successfully extends stability results from preactivations to NTKs and proves no finite-width corrections for scale-invariant nonlinearities like ReLU on the NTK’s diagonal Gram matrix. Numerical implementation confirms that the first-order corrections accurately match sampled network statistics for widths n ≥ 20.
为弥补无限宽NTK分析在捕捉真实训练动态方面的不足，本文引入费曼图方法计算NTK统计量的有限宽度修正。该方法利用图示代数推导预激活、NTK及高阶张量的层间递推关系，从而预测训练行为的一阶近似。实验表明，该框架成功将稳定性结果从预激活扩展至NTK，并证明对ReLU等尺度不变非线性激活函数，NTK Gram矩阵对角线上无有限宽度修正。数值实现显示，当网络宽度n≥20时，一阶修正结果与采样网络统计量高度吻合。
Beyond the Smile: A Hybrid Convolutional VAE for Crypto Volatility Surfaces
Authors: Sadanand Singh, Allam Reddy, Manan Chopra
First: 2026-06-15T17:01:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:01:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a convolutional variational autoencoder for cryptocurrency implied-volatility surfaces, together with a deployable predictor that combines it with a quadratic smile re-fit through a deterministic per-tenor routing rule. Trained on 6,034 fully-filled hourly Binance Options surfaces of BTC and ETH spanning May-October 2023 and parameterised on a common $6 \times 7$ tenor-delta grid, the model attains a hidden-cell surface-completion RMSE in the 0.94-1.56 vol-point range across both markets and mask rates 10-50%. The hybrid predictor attains 0.83 vol points at 50% masking against 7.00 for the smile re-fit alone, an eightfold reduction obtained at no additional inference cost. Under structurally-correlated hole patterns that emulate the withdrawal of an entire tenor of strikes, the smile re-fit incurs 9.6-13.1 vol points of error while the learned model remains at 1.5-1.9, isolating a regime in which the generative model is the only viable predictor. Joint training on BTC and ETH improves the in-distribution model on both markets by 9-27% relative to the better-performing single-symbol counterpart, indicating a substantially shared vol-surface manifold across the two largest cryptocurrencies over the observation window. The hybrid is calendar- and butterfly-arbitrage-free at the listed strikes, a property that the parametric smile re-fit alone fails at high mask rates. The per-snapshot reconstruction error of the trained model flags the late-October ETF-anticipation rally and the August $17$, $2023$ flash crash as elevated-error periods without supervision. All training and evaluation infrastructure is released to support reproducible follow-on work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越微笑曲线：一种用于加密货币波动率曲面的混合卷积变分自编码器
我们提出了一种用于加密货币隐含波动率曲面的卷积变分自编码器，并结合确定性每期限路由规则，与二次微笑曲线重拟合构建可部署的预测器。该模型在2023年5月至10月期间6,034个完全填充的Binance期权小时级曲面（BTC和ETH）上训练，参数化于共同的6×7期限-行权价网格，其隐藏单元曲面补全的RMSE在两个市场及10%-50%掩码率下均处于0.94-1.56波动点范围。混合预测器在50%掩码率下达到0.83波动点，相较单独微笑重拟合的7.00波动点降低八倍，且无额外推理开销。在模拟整个期限行权价被移除的结构相关孔洞模式下，微笑重拟合误差达9.6-13.1波动点，而学习模型仍保持在1.5-1.9波动点，凸显生成模型在该场景下为唯一可行预测器。BTC与ETH联合训练使两个市场内的分布内模型相较表现更好的单币种模型提升9-27%，表明在观测窗口内两大加密货币波动率曲面存在显著共享流形。该混合模型在列示行权价下无日历与蝶式套利，而参数化微笑重拟合在高掩码率下无法实现。训练模型的每帧重建误差在无监督情况下可识别10月下旬ETF预期上涨行情及2023年8月17日闪崩为高误差时段。所有训练与评估基础设施均已开源，以支持可复现的后续研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust volatility surface prediction in cryptocurrency markets, the authors propose a hybrid convolutional variational autoencoder that combines generative modeling with a deterministic quadratic smile re-fit via per-tenor routing. Trained on 6,034 hourly Binance options surfaces for BTC and ETH, the model achieves surface-completion RMSE of 0.94–1.56 vol-points across mask rates 10–50%, outperforming the smile re-fit alone by an order of magnitude (0.83 vs 7.00 vol-points at 50% masking). Under simulated tenor-wide strike removals, the generative model maintains low error (1.5–1.9 vol-points) while the smile re-fit fails (9.6–13.1 vol-points), demonstrating its superiority in structural gaps. Joint training on BTC and ETH improves performance by 9–27% over single-symbol models, suggesting shared volatility manifolds. The hybrid predictor is arbitrage-free at listed strikes, unlike the parametric re-fit, and autonomously detects market anomalies like the August 2023 flash crash and late-October ETF rally via reconstruction error.
为解决加密货币市场波动率曲面建模的准确性与鲁棒性问题，作者提出一种混合卷积变分自编码器（VAE），结合生成模型与基于每期限的确定性二次微笑拟合。该模型在2023年5月至10月的6034个Binance期权表面数据上训练，覆盖BTC和ETH，在10%-50%遮蔽率下表面重建RMSE为0.94–1.56波动点。混合预测器在50%遮蔽率下误差仅为0.83波动点，较单独微笑拟合（7.00波动点）降低八倍，且无额外推理开销。在模拟整期限期权删除的结构化缺失场景下，VAE保持低误差（1.5–1.9波动点），而微笑拟合误差高达9.6–13.1波动点，凸显VAE在结构性缺口中的优势。BTC与ETH联合训练使模型在两个市场上的表现提升9–27%，表明二者在观测期内共享波动率曲面流形。该混合模型在列示行权价上无日历与蝶式套利，而单独微笑拟合在高遮蔽率下无法满足此性质，且其重建误差可无监督识别市场事件如8月2023年闪崩与ETF预期上涨期。
SurroundNEXO: Ego-Centric Metric Bridging for Spatially Consistent Geometry in Autonomous Driving
Authors: Shuai Yuan, Runxi Tang, Yuzhou Ji, Fudong Ge, Hanshi Wang, Yifei Wang, Xianming Zeng, Jianyun Xu, Xingliang Liu, Yanfeng Wang, Zhipeng Zhang
First: 2026-06-15T17:00:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:00:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern autonomous driving depends on accurate metric 3D understanding for perception, reconstruction, and planning, which in turn requires reliable multi-camera depth prediction. However, the outward-facing nature of vehicle-mounted surround-view camera rigs inherently limits visual overlap across views, challenging the correspondence-based assumptions that underpin conventional multi-view geometry. To bridge this gap, we present SurroundNEXO, named after the Spanish word nexo for a geometric link, a low-overlap multi-camera metric depth framework that grounds cross-view reasoning in ego-centric geometry rather than dense visual correspondences. Instead of directly enforcing early global fusion, SurroundNEXO first assigns image tokens globally comparable ego-frame viewing directions through Ego-Ray Positional Encoding, then uses sparse LiDAR measurements as metric anchors to propagate absolute scale cues, and finally expands feature interaction progressively from view-local modeling to decomposed spatio-temporal reasoning and global integration. This design enables metric-scale depth prediction with improved spatial consistency across weakly overlapping cameras. Across low-overlap autonomous driving benchmarks, including NuScenes, Waymo and DDAD, SurroundNEXO reduces single-view error by 33.2%, improves cross-view consistency by 10.5%, and enhances metric reconstruction quality by 25.6% compared with SOTA methods. It further remains robust under extremely sparse depth prompts and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to unseen camera layouts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SurroundNEXO：面向自动驾驶的空间一致几何的自车视角度量桥接
现代自动驾驶依赖精确的度量3D理解以实现感知、重建与规划，而这需要可靠的多摄像头深度预测。然而，车载环视摄像头系统固有的外向视角特性，导致视图间视觉重叠有限，挑战了传统多视角几何所依赖的基于对应关系的假设。为弥合这一差距，我们提出SurroundNEXO（名称源自西班牙语“nexo”，意为几何连接），这是一种低重叠多摄像头度量深度框架，其跨视图推理基于自车视角几何而非密集视觉对应。SurroundNEXO不直接强制早期全局融合，而是首先通过自车射线位置编码将图像标记分配至全局可比的自车视角方向，再利用稀疏激光雷达测量作为度量锚点传播绝对尺度线索，最后逐步从视图局部建模扩展至分解时空推理与全局整合。该设计实现了在弱重叠摄像头间具有更优空间一致性的度量尺度深度预测。在包括NuScenes、Waymo和DDAD在内的低重叠自动驾驶基准测试中，SurroundNEXO相较当前最优方法单视图误差降低33.2%，跨视图一致性提升10.5%，度量重建质量提高25.6%。同时，其在极度稀疏深度提示下仍保持鲁棒性，并对未见摄像头布局展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of sparse visual overlap in multi-camera autonomous driving systems, which undermines conventional correspondence-based depth prediction, SurroundNEXO introduces a novel ego-centric metric depth framework. It first aligns image tokens using Ego-Ray Positional Encoding to establish globally comparable viewing directions, then anchors depth scaling via sparse LiDAR measurements, and finally progressively expands feature interaction from local to spatio-temporal and global reasoning. Evaluated across NuScenes, Waymo, and DDAD, the method reduces single-view error by 33.2%, improves cross-view consistency by 10.5%, and boosts metric reconstruction quality by 25.6% over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining robustness under sparse depth prompts and zero-shot generalization to unseen camera configurations.
为解决自动驾驶多摄像头系统中视觉重叠稀疏导致的传统对应关系深度预测失效问题，SurroundNEXO提出一种基于自车视角的度量深度框架。该方法首先通过自车射线位置编码对齐图像标记以建立全局可比的观测方向，再利用稀疏激光雷达测量锚定度量尺度，最后通过时空分解逐步扩展特征交互从局部到全局。在NuScenes、Waymo和DDAD等基准测试中，其单视角误差降低33.2%，跨视角一致性提升10.5%，度量重建质量提高25.6%，且在稀疏深度提示和未见摄像头布局下仍保持鲁棒性与零样本泛化能力。
Code as a Weapon: A Consensus-Labeled Prompt Bank for Measuring Coding-Model Compliance with Malicious-Code Requests
Authors: Richard J. Young, Gregory D. Moody
First: 2026-05-27T16:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T17:00:04+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 9 figures, 6 tables. Consensus-labeled prompt bank consolidating eight malicious-code corpora (ASTRA, CySecBench, AdvBench/harmful_behaviors, JailbreakBench, MalwareBench, RedCode, RMCBench, Scam2Prompt) spanning diverse elicitation paradigms; 6,675 prompts, 33,375 classification calls
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A general-purpose language model that answers a harmful question returns text; a coding model that complies with a malicious request can return a working weapon: a keylogger, ransomware, an exploit that runs as written. This asymmetry in the severity of a single act of compliance implies coding-specialized models should clear a higher refusal bar than general-purpose chat models, not a lower one, yet the field cannot tell whether they do. Refusal benchmarks for malicious code are fragmented: they mix requests for executable software with requests for harmful security knowledge and report refusal rates over non-comparable corpora. This paper's central result is that the CODE-versus-KNOWLEDGE classification axis established in a prior four-corpus release remains stable under a substantially expanded corpus pool and an independently refreshed judge panel, evidence that it measures a real construct rather than an artifact of the prompts or judges. Eight corpora spanning diverse elicitation paradigms (direct, jailbreak-decorated, indirect, and agent/interpreter: ASTRA, CySecBench, AdvBench/harmful_behaviors, JailbreakBench, MalwareBench, RedCode, RMCBench, Scam2Prompt) are classified under a five-judge consensus protocol (6,675 prompts x 5 judges = 33,375 calls), reaching Fleiss' kappa = 0.767 [95% CI 0.755, 0.777] ("substantial"). Critically, the panel shares no judge with the prior release (five paid commercial APIs replaced by five open-weight models from five vendors), yet the two panels agree on 94.45% of the 3,133 shared prompts and reach Cohen's kappa = 0.952 [0.942, 0.963] on the 3,031-prompt binary overlap: the axis survives near-total panel replacement. The released bank comprises 4,748 consensus-CODE and 1,923 consensus-KNOWLEDGE prompts, a reliability-quantified benchmark whose central classification axis is shown stable across corpus expansion and judge-panel replacement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代码即武器：用于衡量编码模型对恶意代码请求合规性的共识标注提示语库
通用语言模型回答有害问题仅返回文本；而符合恶意请求的编码模型可能返回可运行的武器：键盘记录器、勒索软件或可直接运行的漏洞利用程序。这种单一行为合规性后果的不对称性意味着编码专用模型应比通用聊天模型设置更高的拒绝门槛，而非更低。然而，当前领域尚无法判断这一点。恶意代码拒绝基准测试碎片化：混合了请求可执行软件与请求有害安全知识的请求，并在不可比的数据集上报告拒绝率。本文核心成果是：在先前四语料库发布中建立的“代码”与“知识”分类轴，在大幅扩展语料库和独立更新评审员面板后仍保持稳定，证明其衡量的是真实构念而非提示或评审员的伪影。八个覆盖多样诱导范式（直接、越狱装饰、间接、代理/解释器：ASTRA、CySecBench、AdvBench/harmful_behaviors、JailbreakBench、MalwareBench、RedCode、RMCBench、Scam2Prompt）的语料库，通过五位评审员共识协议（6,675个提示 × 5位评审员 = 33,375次分类）进行分类，Fleiss' kappa = 0.767 [95% CI 0.755, 0.777]（“显著”）。关键的是，新评审团与先前发布无任何重叠评审员（五家付费商业API被五家供应商的开源权重模型替代），但两组评审团在3,133个共享提示上达成94.45%的一致性，在3,031个二元重叠提示上Cohen's kappa = 0.952 [0.942, 0.963]：该轴在评审团几乎完全更换后仍保持稳定。发布的语料库包含4,748个共识“代码”和1,923个共识“知识”提示，是一个可靠性量化基准，其核心分类轴在语料库扩展和评审团更换后仍保持稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the critical risk that coding models may generate executable malicious code while general-purpose models only produce text, this paper seeks to establish a reliable benchmark to measure compliance with harmful code requests. It introduces a consensus-labeled prompt bank, created by classifying 6,675 prompts across eight diverse corpora using a five-judge panel, achieving a Fleiss' kappa of 0.767, indicating substantial inter-rater reliability. The study confirms the stability of the CODE-versus-KNOWLEDGE classification axis even after replacing the judge panel entirely with new open-weight models, with 94.45% agreement on shared prompts and Cohen's kappa of 0.952 on binary overlap. The final dataset includes 4,748 consensus-CODE and 1,923 consensus-KNOWLEDGE prompts, providing a quantified, reliable benchmark for evaluating coding models' refusal behavior under malicious requests.
为评估编码模型在面对恶意代码请求时是否应比通用模型承担更严格的拒绝标准，本文构建了一个基于五位评委共识标注的提示语库，将提示分为“代码”（可执行恶意软件）和“知识”（有害安全知识）两类。通过对八个多样化数据集采用6,675个提示语和33,375次标注，研究获得高评分者间一致性（Fleiss’ kappa = 0.767），并证明该分类轴在完全更换评委后仍保持稳定。最终发布的基准包含4,748个“代码”和1,923个“知识”提示语，为评估模型对恶意请求的合规性提供了一个可靠、量化工具。
SidewalkBench: Benchmarking Visual Navigation on Urban Sidewalks
Authors: Zhizheng Liu, Honglin He, Vivek Alumootil, Akshat Pandya, Brad Squicciarini, Wayne Wu, Bolei Zhou
First: 2026-06-15T16:54:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:54:30+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://vail-ucla.github.io/SidewalkBench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Urban sidewalk navigation presents significant challenges due to complex structural layouts, dynamic pedestrian behaviors, and long distances. While recent visual navigation models offer a promising solution, the lack of a unified benchmark hinders quantitative and reproducible evaluation. To bridge this gap, we propose SidewalkBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed for visual navigation on urban sidewalks. Built upon NVIDIA Isaac Sim, SidewalkBench brings GPU-accelerated simulation of diverse, high-fidelity sidewalk environments, including both procedurally generated and real-world scanned scenes. We further populate the scenes with rich, reactive event-based pedestrian behaviors and flexible, efficient animation, enabling standardized model evaluation under realistic real-world settings. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 9 visual navigation models on 330 unit-test scenarios, 800 pedestrian-reactive scenarios, and 105 long-horizon scenarios. Our findings highlight that pedestrian interaction and long-horizon robustness remain critical bottlenecks for existing models, and scaling up sidewalk training with synthetic data emerges as a promising solution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SidewalkBench：城市人行道视觉导航基准
由于复杂的结构布局、动态行人行为和长距离等因素，城市人行道导航面临重大挑战。尽管近期视觉导航模型提供了有前景的解决方案，但缺乏统一基准阻碍了定量和可重复的评估。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了SidewalkBench，一个专为城市人行道视觉导航设计的综合性基准。基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建，SidewalkBench提供GPU加速的多样化、高保真度人行道环境模拟，包括程序生成和真实世界扫描场景。我们进一步为场景添加了丰富、基于事件的行人行为和灵活高效的动画，使模型能在真实世界环境下进行标准化评估。我们在330个单元测试场景、800个行人交互场景和105个长时程场景中对9个视觉导航模型进行了全面评估。研究发现，行人交互和长时程鲁棒性仍是现有模型的关键瓶颈，而利用合成数据扩展人行道训练成为有前景的解决方案。

为解决视觉导航模型在复杂城市人行道环境中缺乏统一评估基准的问题，本研究提出SidewalkBench，基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建，支持GPU加速、高保真人行道场景（含程序生成与真实扫描环境），并融入反应式行人行为与高效动画以模拟真实交互。在对9个模型在330个单元测试、800个行人交互场景及105个长时序场景的评估中发现，行人交互与长时序鲁棒性仍是现有模型的主要瓶颈，而合成数据扩展训练展现出显著改进潜力。
Phantoms and Disclosures: a Causal Framework for Auditing Synthetic Data
Authors: Kareem Amin, Rudrajit Das, Alessandro Epasto, Adel Javanmard, Dennis Kraft, Mónica Ribero, Sergei Vassilvitskii
First: 2026-06-15T16:54:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:54:02+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 10 tables, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid adoption of generative AI and Large Language Models (LLMs) has spurred interest in synthetic data as a privacy-preserving alternative to sensitive real-world datasets. However, generating high-utility synthetic data often carries the risk of memorizing and regurgitating private information from the training corpus. In this work, we present a customizable empirical auditing framework designed to detect and explain such data disclosures. Our framework introduces a mechanism to distinguish between "true disclosures"-where the system directly reproduces a user's information-and "phantom disclosures''-where the system incidentally generates a user's data. By partitioning input data into training and holdout sets and applying rigorous statistical hypothesis testing, we determine if observed disclosures are consistent with strict privacy baselines, such as zero-learning or specific Differential Privacy (DP) bounds. Crucially, this approach requires no model access, no canary insertion, and no reference model training -only the synthetic output and a held-out control set. We demonstrate that this framework effectively functions as a membership inference attack, providing empirical lower bounds on privacy leakage that are tighter than prior data-based auditing methods. Our approach is model-agnostic, applies to any synthetic data generation mechanism, and requires orders of magnitude fewer computational resources than shadow-model or canary-based alternatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：幻影与披露：用于合成数据审计的因果框架
生成式人工智能和大型语言模型（LLM）的快速采用，推动了合成数据作为保护隐私的敏感真实数据替代方案的兴趣。然而，生成高价值的合成数据往往存在从训练语料库中记忆并复现私人信息的风险。本文提出了一种可定制的经验性审计框架，旨在检测并解释此类数据披露。该框架引入了一种区分“真实披露”（系统直接复现用户信息）与“幻影披露”（系统偶然生成用户数据）的机制。通过将输入数据划分为训练集和保留集，并应用严格的统计假设检验，我们判断观察到的披露是否符合严格的隐私基准，如零学习或特定差分隐私（DP）界限。关键的是，该方法无需模型访问、无需插入探针、无需训练参考模型——仅需合成输出和保留的对照集。我们证明，该框架可有效作为成员推断攻击，提供比以往基于数据的审计方法更紧的隐私泄露经验下界。我们的方法模型无关，适用于任何合成数据生成机制，且计算资源需求比影子模型或探针方法低几个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Driven by concerns over privacy risks in synthetic data generated by generative AI and LLMs, this work introduces a causal auditing framework to detect and classify data disclosures as either direct 'true disclosures' or indirect 'phantom disclosures'. The method partitions input data into training and holdout sets, then uses statistical hypothesis testing to evaluate whether observed outputs violate strict privacy baselines like zero-learning or Differential Privacy. Crucially, it requires no model access, no canary insertion, or reference model training—only synthetic outputs and a held-out control set. Experiments show the framework effectively acts as a membership inference attack, yielding tighter empirical lower bounds on privacy leakage than prior methods, while being model-agnostic and requiring far fewer computational resources.
鉴于生成式AI和大语言模型生成的合成数据可能泄露用户隐私，本文提出一种因果审计框架，用于检测并区分直接泄露（true disclosures）与间接泄露（phantom disclosures）。该方法将输入数据划分为训练集与保留集，通过统计假设检验判断输出是否违反零学习或差分隐私等严格隐私基准。关键在于无需模型访问、无需插入探针或训练参考模型，仅需合成输出与保留控制集即可。实验表明，该框架可有效作为成员推断攻击，提供比现有方法更紧的隐私泄露下界，且模型无关、计算资源需求低得多。
Simulation-Based Multi-Fillet Evaluation of Woody Breast Poultry Fillets
Authors: Chirantan Sen Mukherjee, Seung-Chul Yoon, William J. Beksi
First: 2026-06-15T16:52:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:52:34+00:00
Comments: To be published in the 2026 International Conference on Automation Science and Engineering (CASE)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Woody breast (WB) is a myopathy in modern broiler chickens that causes the breast muscle to become unusually stiff and fibrous, leading to decreased meat quality and significant economic losses. State-of-the-art automated WB detection relies on a side-view imaging system to analyze the bending behavior of a single fillet as it falls off a conveyor belt. While highly accurate, this approach is constrained by its single-fillet field of view, creating throughput bottlenecks on commercial processing lines. In this paper, we address this limitation via a novel multi-fillet detection architecture utilizing a top-down camera configuration. To validate our approach, we first develop a high-fidelity digital twin of an industrial conveyor system. Next, we synthesize a diverse dataset of 3D fillet meshes and model their viscoelastic bending dynamics using a physics-based simulation engine. Lastly, a continuous 2D shape deformation score is extracted from the top-down perspective as the simulated fillets traverse the roller precipice. Experimental results demonstrate that the top-down shape score effectively captures the contour changes of the fillets as it bends, providing a robust and scalable alternative to a side-view imaging system for simultaneous multi-fillet WB evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于仿真的多鸡胸肉嫩度评估方法
木质胸肉（WB）是现代肉鸡中的一种肌病，会导致胸肌异常僵硬和纤维化，从而降低肉质并造成重大经济损失。目前最先进的自动化WB检测依赖于侧视成像系统，通过分析单个鸡胸肉片从传送带脱落时的弯曲行为进行判断。虽然该方法精度高，但受限于单片视野，难以满足商业生产线的吞吐量需求。本文提出一种新颖的多鸡胸肉检测架构，采用俯视摄像头配置以克服此限制。为验证该方法，我们首先构建了工业传送系统的高保真数字孪生体；接着，合成多样化的3D鸡胸肉网格数据，并利用物理仿真引擎模拟其黏弹性弯曲动力学；最后，从俯视角度提取模拟鸡胸肉穿越滚筒边缘时的连续2D形状变形分数。实验结果表明，俯视形状分数能有效捕捉鸡胸肉弯曲过程中的轮廓变化，为同时评估多片鸡胸肉的WB状况提供了一种稳健且可扩展的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve throughput in poultry processing by enabling simultaneous detection of multiple fillets for woody breast (WB) evaluation, this study proposes a top-down camera-based detection method. The approach uses a physics-based simulation engine to generate a digital twin of an industrial conveyor system, simulating 3D fillet meshes and their viscoelastic bending behavior. A continuous 2D shape deformation score is then extracted from the top-down perspective as simulated fillets traverse a roller precipice. Experimental results show that this shape score effectively captures contour changes during bending, offering a scalable and robust alternative to single-fillet side-view imaging systems.
为解决现有侧视图像系统仅能单个检测鸡胸肉木状胸（WB）缺陷导致的处理瓶颈，本研究提出一种基于顶视摄像头的多胸肉同步检测方法。该方法通过构建工业传送带数字孪生体，结合物理仿真引擎模拟3D胸肉的黏弹性弯曲动力学，并从顶视角度提取连续的2D形状变形分数。实验表明，该形状分数能有效捕捉胸肉弯曲轮廓变化，提供了一种可扩展且稳健的多胸肉WB检测替代方案。
PoseGAM: Robust Unseen Object Pose Estimation via Geometry-Aware Multi-View Reasoning
Authors: Jianqi Chen, Biao Zhang, Xiangjun Tang, Peter Wonka
Venue: CVPR 2026 Oral
First: 2025-12-11T17:29:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:52:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026 (Oral). Project page: https://windvchen.github.io/PoseGAM/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
6D object pose estimation, which predicts the transformation of an object relative to the camera, remains challenging for unseen objects. Existing approaches typically rely on explicitly constructing feature correspondences between the query image and either the object model or template images. In this work, we propose PoseGAM, a geometry-aware multi-view framework that directly predicts object pose from a query image and multiple template images, eliminating the need for explicit matching. Built upon recent multi-view-based foundation model architectures, the method integrates object geometry information through two complementary mechanisms: explicit point-based geometry and learned features from geometry representation networks. In addition, we construct a large-scale synthetic dataset containing more than 190k objects under diverse environmental conditions to enhance robustness and generalization. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate our state-of-the-art performance, yielding an average AR improvement of 5.1% over prior methods and achieving up to 17.6% gains on individual datasets, indicating strong generalization to unseen objects. Project page: https://windvchen.github.io/PoseGAM/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：PoseGAM：通过几何感知的多视角推理实现对未见物体姿态的鲁棒估计
6D物体姿态估计，即预测物体相对于相机的变换，对于未见物体仍具挑战性。现有方法通常依赖于显式构建查询图像与物体模型或模板图像之间的特征对应关系。本文提出PoseGAM，一种几何感知的多视角框架，可直接从查询图像和多个模板图像中预测物体姿态，无需显式匹配。该方法基于近期多视角基础模型架构，通过两种互补机制整合物体几何信息：显式的点基几何与从几何表示网络中学习的特征。此外，我们构建了一个大规模合成数据集，包含超过19万个物体在多样环境条件下的数据，以增强鲁棒性和泛化能力。在多个基准测试中的广泛评估表明，我们的方法达到当前最优性能，相较前序方法平均AR提升5.1%，在部分数据集上最高提升达17.6%，显示出对未见物体的强泛化能力。项目页面：https://windvchen.github.io/PoseGAM/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating 6D poses for unseen objects without explicit feature matching, PoseGAM introduces a geometry-aware multi-view framework that directly predicts object pose from a query image and multiple templates. It leverages recent multi-view foundation models and integrates object geometry via explicit point-based structures and learned geometry representation features. Evaluated on multiple benchmarks using a large-scale synthetic dataset of over 190k objects, the method achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average AR improvement of 5.1% over prior methods and up to 17.6% gains on individual datasets, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen objects.
为解决传统方法在估计未见物体6D位姿时依赖显式特征匹配的局限性，PoseGAM提出一种几何感知的多视角框架，直接从查询图像与多个模板图像中预测物体位姿，无需显式匹配。该方法基于近期多视角基础模型，通过显式点几何与几何表示网络学习的特征相结合，融入物体几何信息。在包含超19万类物体的合成数据集上进行评估，方法在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能，平均AR提升5.1%，部分数据集提升高达17.6%，展现出对未见物体的强泛化能力。
Discovering Symmetry Groups with Flow Matching
Authors: Yuxuan Chen, Jung Yeon Park, Floor Eijkelboom, Jianke Yang, Jan-Willem van de Meent, Lawson L. S. Wong, Robin Walters
First: 2025-12-23T04:27:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:49:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Symmetry is fundamental to understanding physical systems and can improve performance and sample efficiency in machine learning. Both pursuits require knowledge of the underlying symmetries in data, yet discovering these symmetries automatically is challenging. We propose LieFlow, a novel framework that reframes symmetry discovery as a distribution learning problem on Lie groups. Instead of searching for the symmetry generators, our approach operates directly in group space, modeling a symmetry distribution over a large hypothesis group $G$. The support of the learned distribution reveals the underlying symmetry group $H \subseteq G$. Unlike previous works, LieFlow can discover both continuous and discrete symmetries within a unified framework, without assuming a fixed Lie algebra basis or a specific distribution over the group elements. Experiments on synthetic 2D and 3D point clouds, ModelNet10 and a real-world MI-Motion dataset show that LieFlow accurately discovers continuous and discrete subgroups, significantly outperforming a state-of-the-art baseline, LieGAN, in identifying discrete symmetries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用流匹配发现对称群
对称性是理解物理系统的基础，也能提升机器学习的性能和样本效率。两者均需了解数据中的底层对称性，但自动发现这些对称性颇具挑战。我们提出LieFlow，一种新颖框架，将对称性发现重构为在李群上的分布学习问题。不同于搜索对称生成元，我们的方法直接在群空间操作，建模一个在大型假设群G上的对称分布。所学分布的支持集揭示了底层对称群H ⊆ G。与以往工作不同，LieFlow可在统一框架内发现连续与离散对称性，无需假设固定的李代数基或特定的群元素分布。在合成2D和3D点云、ModelNet10及真实世界MI-Motion数据集上的实验表明，LieFlow准确发现连续与离散子群，在识别离散对称性方面显著优于当前最先进基线LieGAN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automatically uncover symmetries in data to enhance machine learning performance and physical system modeling, the authors introduce LieFlow, a framework that treats symmetry discovery as learning a distribution over Lie groups. Instead of searching for symmetry generators, LieFlow operates directly in group space, modeling a symmetry distribution over a large hypothesis group G, and the support of this distribution reveals the underlying symmetry group H ⊆ G. Experiments on synthetic point clouds and real datasets demonstrate that LieFlow successfully identifies both continuous and discrete symmetries, outperforming the state-of-the-art LieGAN in discrete symmetry detection.
为提升机器学习性能和物理系统建模能力，自动发现数据中的对称性成为关键需求。作者提出LieFlow框架，将对称性发现转化为在李群上学习分布的问题，不依赖对称生成元，而直接在群空间建模一个大假设群G上的对称分布，其支撑集即揭示真实对称子群H ⊆ G。在合成点云和真实数据集上的实验表明，LieFlow能准确识别连续与离散对称性，尤其在离散对称性检测上显著优于当前最优方法LieGAN。
Latent space mapping of interpretable structural coordinates from stochastic single-molecule signals
Authors: Matteo Cartiglia, Sandro Kuppel, Wouter Botermans Wannes Peeters, Natan Biesmans, Liam Vandekerckhove, Eric Beamish, Koen Ongena, Wouter Renckens, Pol Van Dorpe, Sanjin Marion
First: 2026-06-15T16:49:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:49:11+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nanopores are versatile single-molecular sensors, but their utility is fundamentally constrained by stochastic translocation dynamics warping any encoded information. We resolve it by shifting from time-domain analysis to a learned latent-space mapping via a contrastive encoder trained exclusively on simulated signals from a physics-informed model. This encoder maps solid-state nanopore signals of engineered DNA barcodes into an interpretable molecular coordinate system. The learned representation is responsive to structural barcode parameters while remaining invariant to acquisition conditions and translocation conformation, allowing data pooling across devices. Molecule identification requires a single pass through the encoder, reducing computational cost by three orders of magnitude relative to alignment-based methods. We experimentally validate through mixture quantification, rare-variant detection, consensus barcode reconstruction, and real-time signal acquisition. This shift from temporal analysis to mapping structural coordinates into a latent space changes the paradigm behind analyzing stochastic sensor signals by linking classification to interpretable encoded molecular information.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从随机单分子信号中映射可解释的结构坐标到潜在空间
纳米孔是多功能的单分子传感器，但其应用受到随机转位动力学扭曲编码信息的根本限制。我们通过将时域分析转向通过对比编码器学习的潜在空间映射来解决该问题，该编码器仅基于物理信息模型模拟的信号进行训练。该编码器将工程DNA条形码的固态纳米孔信号映射到可解释的分子坐标系中。所学表示对结构条形码参数敏感，同时对采集条件和转位构象保持不变，允许跨设备数据合并。分子识别仅需一次通过编码器，相较于基于对齐的方法，计算成本降低三个数量级。我们通过混合物定量、稀有变异检测、共识条形码重建和实时信号采集实验验证了该方法。从时域分析转向将结构坐标映射到潜在空间，改变了随机传感器信号分析的范式，将分类与可解释的编码分子信息联系起来。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the stochastic nature of nanopore signals that obscures encoded molecular information, this work introduces a contrastive encoder trained on simulated signals from a physics-informed model to map single-molecule nanopore data into a latent space representing interpretable structural coordinates. The method enables molecule identification with a single encoder pass, drastically reducing computational cost compared to alignment-based approaches, and remains invariant to acquisition conditions and translocation conformation, facilitating cross-device data pooling. Experimental results across mixture quantification, rare-variant detection, consensus barcode reconstruction, and real-time acquisition confirm the method’s robustness and utility in transforming stochastic sensor signals into interpretable molecular representations.
为了解决纳米孔信号因随机转位导致的分子信息模糊问题，该研究提出一种基于物理模型模拟信号训练的对比编码器，将随机单分子信号映射到可解释的结构坐标空间。该方法仅需一次编码即可完成分子识别，计算成本较对齐方法降低三个数量级。实验在混合物定量、稀有变异检测、共识条形码重建和实时信号采集中验证了编码器对设备差异和转位构象的鲁棒性，同时保持对结构条形码参数的敏感性。
Fast-dLLM++: Fréchet Profile Decoding for Faster Diffusion LLM Inference
Authors: Siva Rajesh Kasa, Yasong Dai, Sumit Negi, Hongdong Li
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T23:18:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:47:38+00:00
Comments: Initial version accepted at Workshop on Structured Probabilistic Inference & Generative Modeling, ICML 2026. Project Page: https://ringo-star.github.io/projectpage_frechet/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion large language models promise parallel token generation, yet inference remains bottlenecked by deciding which masked tokens can be safely committed together. Fast-dLLM addressed this with KV caching and confidence-guided parallel decoding, but its decoding theory uses a homogeneous high-confidence assumption that effectively reduces each candidate set to its weakest selected token. We argue that this leaves speed on the table because real decoding steps exhibit heterogeneous confidence profiles. We propose \textbf{Fast-dLLM++}, a training-free extension that introduces \emph{Fréchet profile decoding}: selecting parallel commit sets from the full sorted confidence profile rather than a single worst-case confidence. The resulting rule is a heterogeneous-confidence generalization of Fast-dLLM's factor selector and it recovers the previous rule exactly in the equal-confidence case and adds a provable \emph{heterogeneity bonus} when the selected tokens have uneven confidences. Fast-dLLM++ leaves the model, diffusion process, and cache implementation entirely unchanged, making it a drop-in replacement for existing Fast-dLLM decoding. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP with the LLaDA-8B model show that the theoretical improvement translates directly into empirical gains: profile-aware selection improves the accuracy--throughput frontier by exploiting safe parallelism that weakest-token rules miss, achieving up to 37\% higher throughput at comparable accuracy. Our code release is at https://github.com/Ringo-Star/FastdLLM_plusplus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Fast-dLLM++：基于Fréchet轮廓解码的更快扩散大语言模型推理
扩散式大语言模型虽承诺并行生成token，但推理仍受限于如何判断哪些被掩码的token可安全并行提交。Fast-dLLM通过KV缓存与置信度引导的并行解码解决了此问题，但其解码理论依赖同质高置信度假设，实质上将每个候选集缩减为最弱选中token。我们认为，这导致速度损失，因真实解码步骤呈现异质置信度分布。我们提出\textbf{Fast-dLLM++}，一种无需训练的扩展方法，引入\emph{Fréchet轮廓解码}：从完整排序的置信度轮廓中选择并行提交集，而非单一最坏置信度。所得规则是Fast-dLLM因子选择器的异质置信度推广，在置信度相等时精确还原原规则，并在选中token置信度不均时提供可证明的\emph{异质性增益}。Fast-dLLM++完全保留模型、扩散过程与缓存实现，可无缝替换现有Fast-dLLM解码器。在GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval与MBPP数据集上使用LLaDA-8B模型的实验表明，理论改进直接转化为实证增益：轮廓感知选择通过利用最弱token规则忽略的安全并行性，提升准确率-吞吐量前沿，最高实现37%更高吞吐量且保持相当准确率。代码发布于https://github.com/Ringo-Star/FastdLLM_plusplus。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Fast-dLLM's homogeneous confidence assumption that overlooks real-world token confidence heterogeneity, the authors propose Fast-dLLM++, a training-free decoding extension that employs Fréchet profile decoding to select parallel commit sets based on the full sorted confidence profile rather than just the weakest token. This approach generalizes the original factor selector and recovers its behavior under equal confidence while adding a provable heterogeneity bonus when confidences vary. Experiments on LLaDA-8B across GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP show that this profile-aware selection improves the accuracy-throughput tradeoff, achieving up to 37% higher throughput at comparable accuracy without altering the model, diffusion process, or cache implementation.
为解决Fast-dLLM对高置信度同质假设的局限性，该研究提出Fast-dLLM++，一种无需训练的解码扩展方法，采用Fréchet置信度分布解码策略，依据完整排序的置信度分布而非仅最弱置信度选择并行提交组。该方法在置信度相等时还原Fast-dLLM行为，并在置信度不均时提供可证明的异质性增益。在GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval和MBPP数据集上使用LLaDA-8B模型的实验表明，该置信度感知选择显著优化了准确率与吞吐量的权衡，最高可实现37%的吞吐量提升，且不改变模型、扩散过程或缓存实现。
Generative Molecular Design with Steerable and Granular Synthesizability Control
Authors: Jeff Guo, Víctor Sabanza-Gil, Olha Semenenko, Oleksii Hrabovskyi, Mykola Protopopov, Anna Kapeliukha, Oleksandr Mosia, Sofiia Hatych, Diana Alieksieieva, Tom Nelis, Patrick Molliet, Helena Solé-Àvila, Valentas Olikauskas, Nina Aregger, Irina Morozova, Joseph Schmidt, Zlatko Jončev, Olga Tarkhanova, Petro Borysko, Jerome Waser, Bruno Correia, Jeremy Luterbacher, Philippe Schwaller
First: 2025-05-13T17:53:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:47:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing molecules that are both property-optimal and readily synthesizable is a central challenge in drug discovery. Existing works that do consider synthesizability can jointly output predicted synthesis routes for generated molecules. However, there has been minimal attention in addressing the ease of synthesis and with flexibility to incorporate desired reaction constraints. On the other hand, virtual screening searches for commercially available compounds, but imposes challenges when scaling to ultra-large (billion-size and beyond) chemical spaces. Here, we propose a generative design framework that unifies synthesis-constrained molecular design and ultra-large-scale virtual screening through steerable and granular synthesizability control. Generated molecules satisfy arbitrary multi-parameter optimization objectives with predicted synthesis routes satisfying mix-and-match constraints: including or avoiding certain reactions, incorporating specific building blocks, and minimizing synthesis route length. In an end-to-end in-house campaign targeting BRD4, we designed molecules synthesizable with specific selected reactions and building blocks, synthesized all six selected compounds, and identified two micromolar binders. We further demonstrate that reaction control enables efficient navigation of ultra-large make-on-demand chemical spaces to identify property-optimal candidates. By applying our framework to Chemspace's Freedom 4.0 make-on-demand space (142 billion molecules), we generated ~320k molecules (0.00023% of the library) on a single consumer-grade GPU (with only 8 GB GPU memory) and identified a micromolar Wee1 binder amongst 60 synthesized candidates.
