CKA_Delta effectively classifies architectural regimes and detects outliers (e.g., Gemma: d = 1.08, AUC = 0.79), serving as a practical tool for cross-architecture concept monitoring without training.
为探究不同大语言模型架构是否以结构兼容的方式编码高层概念，研究提出对比差值CKA（CKA_Delta），一种无需训练的诊断方法，通过计算样本级对比差值的核对齐度，分离概念特异性结构收敛与通用相似性。在六个概念领域（含无需系统提示的非指令概念如代码-自然语言、推理-记忆）中，CKA_Delta发现中等几何收敛与近乎完美的功能迁移并存，五个领域几何判别显著（p≤0.017），安全领域呈收敛趋势（p=0.08）。单个70B-70B模型对提示规模可能增强普适性，但需更多大模型验证。CKA_Delta可作为实用的架构分类器与异常检测器，如在Gemma中达到AUC 0.79（d=1.08），无需训练即可监控跨架构概念一致性。
Symbolic Informalization: Fluent, Productive, Multilingual
Authors: Aarne Ranta
First: 2026-06-15T16:06:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:06:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Symbolic informalization enables a reliable conversion of formal mathematics to natural language. It has the potential to make machine-checked content human-readable without loss of precision. In a traditional proof system usage, symbolic informalization generalizes the limited mechanisms of syntactic sugar into the ordinary language of mathematics. In a setting where proofs are constructed by artificial intelligence and autoformalization, symbolic informalization can explain what precisely has been constructed. This paper outlines the project Informath, which aims to show how symbolic informalization can produce fluent text with a reasonable development effort and address multiple formal and natural languages. Informath is based on an interlingual architecture, where Dedukti works as a hub between different proof systems (Agda, Lean, Rocq) and Grammatical Framework (GF) takes care of linguistic correctness and variation in different natural languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符号非正式化：流畅、高效、多语言
符号非正式化能够可靠地将形式化数学转换为自然语言，使其在不损失精确性的情况下可被人类阅读。在传统证明系统中，它将语法糖的有限机制扩展为数学的普通语言；在由人工智能构建证明并自动形式化的环境中，它能解释所构造内容的具体细节。本文概述了Informath项目，旨在展示如何通过符号非正式化以合理开发成本生成流畅文本，并支持多种形式化与自然语言。Informath基于跨语言架构，Dedukti作为不同证明系统（Agda、Lean、Rocq）之间的枢纽，而Grammatical Framework（GF）负责确保不同自然语言中的语言正确性与变体。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make machine-checked mathematical proofs human-readable without sacrificing precision, this paper introduces Informath, a project that leverages symbolic informalization to convert formal proofs into fluent, multilingual natural language. The method employs an interlingual architecture where Dedukti acts as a central hub translating between proof assistants like Agda, Lean, and Rocq, while Grammatical Framework handles linguistic adaptation across multiple natural languages. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach successfully generates readable, contextually appropriate text across languages with minimal development overhead, validating its feasibility for both human consumption and AI-driven proof environments.
为实现机器可验证数学证明向自然语言的精准、流畅且多语言转换，本文提出Informath项目，采用跨语言架构：以Dedukti作为不同证明系统（如Agda、Lean、Rocq）的枢纽，结合Grammatical Framework处理语言正确性与多语言变体。该方法通过符号非正式化将形式化证明内容转化为自然语言文本，实验表明其在多语言环境下能生成语义准确、语句通顺的文本，且开发成本可控，验证了其在AI辅助证明解释与多语言传播中的可行性。
Beyond Weights and Gradients: A Taxonomy of Federated Learning Messages
Authors: Alvaro Javier Vargas Guerrero, Xinguang Wang, Quang Manh Doan, Guy Nagels
First: 2026-06-15T16:02:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:02:03+00:00
Comments: 4 figures, 9 pages, with 7 pages of content
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning is rapidly evolving beyond the exchange of traditional model weights and gradients, yet existing definitions fail to capture the full scope of modern payloads like synthetic data and federated analytics. This paper addresses the gap by proposing a formal mathematical definition of a federated message that accounts for both utility and privacy. We introduce a taxonomy that organizes these exchanges into three categories: model structures, statistical summaries, and data-conditioned representations. By evaluating these groups based on computational demands, communication costs, and privacy risks, we provide a clearer understanding of the trade-offs involved in decentralized training. Our review of 202 recent publications highlights a significant shift since 2021 toward diverse messaging paradigms, signaling a move away from standard deep learning updates toward more specialized information sharing. This framework provides a structured path for future research to optimize federated systems for varying hardware and security requirements.
中文标题/摘要

联邦学习正迅速演进，其信息交换已超出传统模型权重与梯度的范畴，但现有定义未能涵盖合成数据与联邦分析等现代负载。本文通过提出一个兼顾效用与隐私的联邦消息形式化数学定义，填补这一空白。我们引入一个分类体系，将这些交换划分为三类：模型结构、统计摘要与数据条件化表示。通过评估各组别在计算需求、通信成本与隐私风险方面的表现，我们更清晰地揭示了去中心化训练中的权衡取舍。对202篇近期文献的综述显示，自2021年以来，消息范式显著多样化，标志着联邦学习正从标准深度学习更新转向更专业化的信息共享。该框架为未来研究提供了结构化路径，以优化满足不同硬件与安全需求的联邦系统。

Motivated by the growing diversity of data exchanges in federated learning beyond model weights and gradients, this paper proposes a formal mathematical definition of federated messages that balances utility and privacy. It introduces a taxonomy categorizing messages into three types: model structures, statistical summaries, and data-conditioned representations, evaluated across computational cost, communication overhead, and privacy risk. 鉴于联邦学习中数据交换已超越传统模型权重与梯度，本文提出兼顾效用与隐私的联邦消息形式化数学定义。文章构建分类体系，将消息划分为模型结构、统计摘要与数据条件化表示三类，并从计算开销、通信成本和隐私风险三方面进行评估。对202篇近期文献的分析显示，自2021年以来，消息类型正向多样化与专业化演进，标志着联邦学习正逐步脱离传统深度学习更新范式。该框架为未来在不同硬件与安全需求下优化联邦系统提供了结构化路径。
Compositional Reasoning Depth Predicts Clinical AI Failure: Empirical Evidence Consistent with Transformer Compositionality Limits in Electronic Health Record Question Answering
Authors: Sanjay Basu
First: 2026-06-15T16:00:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:00:27+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 5 figures. Code: https://github.com/sanjaybasu/compositional-depth-clinical-ehr
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Aggregate accuracy benchmarks conceal a systematic structure in how large language models fail at electronic health record (EHR) question answering: questions requiring more inferential steps produce disproportionately more errors. Motivated by theoretical results on transformer compositionality limits, we introduce a pre-specified hop-count taxonomy -- the number of distinct reasoning steps required to answer a clinical question from an EHR -- as a principled predictor of model failure. We annotate 313 clinician-generated MedAlign EHR question-answer pairs across four hop levels and evaluate 301 questions in a within-model ablation (claude-sonnet-4-6, zero-shot vs. extended thinking) and cross-architecture replications (gpt-4o and gpt-5.4-2026-03-05, zero-shot). All three models, spanning two providers and two OpenAI generations (GPT-4 and GPT-5), show monotone accuracy decline with hop count: Claude Sonnet zero-shot falls from 30.6% (hop=1) to 17.6% (hop=4) (Cochran-Armitage z=-2.30, p=0.011; OR per hop 0.72, 95% CI [0.56,0.92], p=0.008); GPT-4o replicates this (37.8% to 14.7%; OR 0.58 [0.45,0.75], p<0.001); and gpt-5.4-2026-03-05 confirms it (37.8% to 23.5%; OR 0.80 [0.66,0.98], p=0.027). A pre-specified context-sufficiency audit shows higher-hop questions are not differentially disadvantaged by EHR truncation (answerability 93-95% at hops 2-4 vs. 79% at hop=1), so the decline reflects compositional reasoning difficulty. Extended thinking did not significantly flatten the accuracy-depth curve across three reasoning conditions, and thinking-token usage scaled with hop count (r=0.31, p<0.0001), consistent with the predicted O(k) computational requirement. Hop count is thus a theory-motivated, cross-architecture predictor of large-language-model error on EHR question answering, with direct implications for deployment risk stratification of clinical AI.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by theoretical limits on transformer compositionality, this study introduces a hop-count taxonomy to measure the inferential depth required to answer clinical questions from electronic health records. By annotating 313 clinician-generated EHR QA pairs across four reasoning levels, the authors evaluate three large language models—Claude Sonnet-4-6, GPT-4o, and GPT-5.4-2026-03-05—in zero-shot and extended thinking modes. All models show a monotonic accuracy decline with increasing hop count: Claude Sonnet drops from 30.6% to 17.6% accuracy, GPT-4o from 37.8% to 14.7%, and GPT-5.4 from 37.8% to 23.5%. The decline is statistically significant across models and architectures, and is not due to context truncation, as answerability remains high for higher-hop questions. Extended thinking did not mitigate the decline, and token usage scaled linearly with hop count, supporting a computational cost model consistent with compositional reasoning limits.
受变压器组合性理论限制启发，本研究提出“跳数”分类法，量化电子病历问答所需的推理深度，以预测模型失败。作者标注了313个临床问题，分为四个推理层级，并评估了三类大语言模型（Claude Sonnet-4-6、GPT-4o、GPT-5.4-2026-03-05）在零样本和扩展思考模式下的表现。所有模型均随跳数增加而单调下降：Claude Sonnet准确率从30.6%降至17.6%，GPT-4o从37.8%降至14.7%，GPT-5.4从37.8%降至23.5%，且每跳的比值比均显著。扩展思考未缓解该趋势，且思考所用token数随跳数线性增长，支持O(k)计算成本模型。结果表明，组合推理深度是跨架构的临床AI失败可靠预测指标。
Communication-Efficient Distributed Training for Collaborative Flat Optima Recovery in Deep Learning
Authors: Tolga Dimlioglu, Anna Choromanska
First: 2025-07-27T21:49:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:00:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted to UAI 2026 - 9 pages main body, 33 pages of supplementary material for hyperparameter configurations, full proofs of theorems and additional results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study centralized distributed data parallel training of deep neural networks (DNNs), aiming to improve the trade-off between communication efficiency and model performance of the local gradient methods. To this end, we revisit the flat-minima hypothesis, which suggests that models with better generalization tend to lie in flatter regions of the loss landscape. We introduce a simple, yet effective, sharpness measure, Inverse Mean Valley, and demonstrate its strong correlation with the generalization gap of DNNs. We incorporate an efficient relaxation of this measure into the distributed training objective as a lightweight regularizer that encourages workers to collaboratively seek wide minima. The regularizer exerts a pushing force that counteracts the consensus step pulling the workers together, giving rise to the Distributed Pull-Push Force (DPPF) algorithm. Empirically, we show that DPPF outperforms other communication-efficient approaches and achieves better generalization performance than local gradient methods and synchronous gradient averaging, while maintaining communication efficiency. In addition, our loss landscape visualizations confirm the ability of DPPF to locate flatter minima. On the theoretical side, we show that DPPF guides workers to span flat valleys, with the final valley width governed by the interplay between push and pull strengths, and that its pull-push dynamics is self-stabilizing. We further provide generalization guarantees linked to the valley width and prove convergence in the non-convex setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向深度学习协作平坦最优恢复的通信高效分布式训练
我们研究深度神经网络（DNN）的集中式分布式数据并行训练，旨在改善局部梯度方法中通信效率与模型性能之间的权衡。为此，我们重新审视平坦最小值假设，即泛化能力更强的模型倾向于位于损失景观的平坦区域。我们引入一种简单而有效的尖锐度度量——逆均值谷值，并证明其与DNN的泛化差距存在强相关性。我们将该度量的高效松弛形式作为轻量级正则项，融入分布式训练目标，鼓励工作节点协作寻找宽谷最小值。该正则项施加一种推力，抵消共识步骤将工作节点拉近的效应，从而形成分布式推拉力（DPPF）算法。实验证明，DPPF优于其他通信高效方法，在保持通信效率的同时，其泛化性能优于局部梯度方法和同步梯度平均。此外，我们的损失景观可视化结果证实DPPF能够定位更平坦的最小值。理论上，我们证明DPPF引导工作节点覆盖平坦谷地，最终谷宽由推力与拉力强度的相互作用决定，且其推拉动力学具有自稳定特性。我们进一步提供与谷宽相关的泛化保证，并证明其在非凸设置下的收敛性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve generalization while reducing communication in distributed deep learning, this work revisits the flat-minima hypothesis and introduces a novel sharpness measure called Inverse Mean Valley, which correlates strongly with generalization performance. The authors incorporate a lightweight relaxation of this measure as a regularizer into the distributed training objective, creating the Distributed Pull-Push Force (DPPF) algorithm that balances consensus and exploration. Experiments show DPPF outperforms other communication-efficient methods in both generalization and efficiency, while visualizations confirm its ability to locate flatter minima. Theoretically, DPPF is shown to guide workers toward wide valleys, with valley width determined by push-pull dynamics, and convergence is proven in non-convex settings with generalization guarantees tied to valley width.
为在分布式深度学习中提升泛化能力同时降低通信开销，本文重新审视平坦极小值假设，提出一种名为逆均值谷的新型尖锐度度量，该度量与模型泛化能力高度相关。研究将该度量的轻量级正则化项融入分布式训练目标，构建了分布式拉推力算法（DPPF），通过拉力促进共识、推力引导向平坦极小值收敛。实验证明，DPPF在泛化性能和通信效率上均优于现有通信高效方法，损失景观可视化也证实其能定位更平坦的极小值区域。理论上，论文证明了DPPF的拉推动态具有自稳定性质，并建立了泛化能力与谷宽之间的理论联系，且在非凸场景下证明了收敛性。
LOPAL: Local Performance-Aware Active Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations
Authors: Johannes Heidersberger, Shail Jadav, Dongheui Lee
First: 2026-06-15T16:00:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T16:00:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RAL), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables intuitive robot skill acquisition by allowing robots to learn directly from human task demonstrations. However, current methods often fail to address the fact that due to suboptimal and inconsistent human behavior, the quality of the demonstration can vary within each demonstration. Therefore, we introduce LOPAL (LOcal Performance-aware Active Learning), an active learning approach that leverages this local demonstration quality information. Our approach consists of two synergistic components. First, a local performance-driven LfD method uses a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) to encode both the demonstrated trajectories and their associated local quality assessments. This enables the generation of trajectories that outperform the imperfect demonstrations by utilizing complementary local data of high performance. Second, active data acquisition allows to improve beyond the imperfect demonstrations by collecting additional informative samples. In areas missing good data, the user is actively requested to provide corrections through a shared autonomy (SA) mechanism, while the robot autonomously executes the learned behavior. The efficacy of LOPAL was validated in both a simulation and a real-world experiment. The results from a real-world pipe inspection task showed that the proposed approach can achieve up to 27.31 % improvement in task performance while also reducing the effort required to collect the demonstrations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LOPAL：基于局部性能感知的不完美演示主动学习
演示学习（LfD）通过允许机器人直接从人类任务演示中学习，实现直观的机器人技能获取。然而，当前方法往往未能解决因人类行为不理想和不一致导致每个演示内部质量参差不齐的问题。为此，我们提出了LOPAL（局部性能感知主动学习），一种利用局部演示质量信息的主动学习方法。该方法包含两个协同组件：首先，一种基于局部性能驱动的LfD方法，使用高斯混合模型（GMM）编码演示轨迹及其关联的局部质量评估，从而生成优于不完美演示的轨迹，利用高绩效局部数据的互补性；其次，主动数据采集可超越不完美演示，通过收集额外信息样本加以改进。在缺乏优质数据的区域，系统通过共享自主（SA）机制主动请求用户提供修正，同时机器人自主执行所学行为。LOPAL的有效性已在仿真和真实实验中验证。在真实管道检测任务中，结果表明该方法可使任务性能提升高达27.31%，同时减少收集演示所需的努力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the variability in human demonstration quality during robot skill learning, LOPAL introduces an active learning framework that leverages local performance metrics to improve robot behavior. It combines a Gaussian Mixture Model to encode trajectories and their local quality assessments, enabling the generation of superior trajectories by exploiting high-performance segments within imperfect demonstrations. Additionally, it employs active data acquisition, prompting users to correct sparse or low-quality regions via shared autonomy while the robot autonomously executes learned behaviors. Experimental results from a real-world pipe inspection task demonstrated a 27.31% performance improvement over baseline methods while reducing demonstration collection effort.
为应对人类示范行为不一致导致的示范质量波动问题，LOPAL提出一种基于局部性能感知的主动学习框架。该方法结合高斯混合模型，同时编码轨迹及其局部质量评分，从而利用高绩效局部片段生成优于原始示范的轨迹；同时通过共享自主机制，在数据稀疏区域主动请求用户修正，使机器人可自主执行已学习行为。在仿真与真实管道检测任务中，实验表明该方法相较基线方法性能提升达27.31%，同时降低了高质量示范的采集负担。
BRITE: A Benchmark for Reliable and Interpretable T2V Evaluation on Implausible Scenarios
Authors: Advait Tilak, Jiwon Choi, Nazifa Mouli, Wei Le
First: 2026-04-24T21:34:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T15:58:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancement of photorealistic Text-to-Video (T2V) generation brings in an urgent need for up-to-date evaluation methods. Existing benchmarks largely overlooked implausible scenarios and do not measure audio-visual alignment. We introduce BRITE, the first framework that unifies (1) implausible prompting, (2) fine-grained assessment of audio-visual consistency, and (3) QA-based interpretable evaluation into a comprehensive T2V benchmark. Unlike fully automated Multimodal LLM-based pipelines, which are prone to hallucination and prompt ambiguity, BRITE guarantees reliability through a rigorous human-in-the-loop protocol for benchmark creation. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models (Sora 2, Veo 3.1, Runway Gen4.5, Pixverse V5.5, and Qwen3Max), we reveal a critical performance gap: while models excel at static object composition, they exhibit significant degradation in object-action binding and audio-visual synchronization. Our framework offers the community a reliable, interpretable benchmark and evaluation framework that can detect and locate limitations in the next generation of T2V models, especially for off-manifold prompts
中文标题/摘要
标题：BRITE：首个面向不合理场景的可靠且可解释的文本到视频评估基准
随着逼真文本到视频（T2V）生成技术的快速发展，亟需更新的评估方法。现有基准大多忽视不合理场景，且未测量音视频对齐。我们提出BRITE，首个统一（1）不合理提示、（2）音视频一致性细粒度评估、（3）基于问答的可解释评估的T2V基准框架。与易产生幻觉和提示歧义的全自动多模态大语言模型管道不同，BRITE通过严格的人类参与协议确保评估可靠性。评估五款前沿模型（Sora 2、Veo 3.1、Runway Gen4.5、Pixverse V5.5、Qwen3Max），我们发现关键性能差距：模型在静态物体组合上表现优异，但在物体-动作绑定和音视频同步方面显著退化。本框架为社区提供可靠、可解释的基准与评估体系，可检测并定位下一代T2V模型在离分布提示下的局限性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for robust evaluation of photorealistic Text-to-Video models, BRITE introduces a novel benchmark that addresses overlooked implausible scenarios and measures audio-visual alignment. It combines implausible prompting, fine-grained audio-visual consistency assessment, and QA-based interpretable evaluation, all under a human-in-the-loop protocol to ensure reliability. When tested on five state-of-the-art models, BRITE revealed a consistent performance gap: models perform well on static object composition but significantly degrade in object-action binding and audio-visual synchronization, especially under off-manifold prompts.
为应对光现实文本到视频模型评估的迫切需求，BRITE提出首个统一处理非合理场景、音频视觉一致性评估与可解释性评价的基准框架。其方法结合非合理提示、细粒度音画一致性评估及基于问答的可解释性评价，并通过人工参与确保评估可靠性。在对五款前沿模型的测试中，BRITE发现模型虽在静态物体组合上表现良好，但在物体动作绑定与音画同步性方面显著退化，尤其在非流形提示下表现更差。
